So every rule in this phase lives in a file that survives (schemas, services, repos, hooks, `programText.ts`, `customize.ts`, `northStarService.ts`), and the edits to those four components are the thinnest wiring that makes the rule visible today. Each such step names its surviving half. Open question 6 asks the owner to confirm this split.

**Whose clock:** every date in this phase comes from the profile timezone (`profiles.timezone` via `settingsRepo.getUserTimezone`, `src/db/settingsRepo.ts:153`) — the workout log's local date is computed on the server (step 21), and Life Mastery's own "today" stops being the browser clock (step 23). No `new Date()` decides "today" on the server anywhere in this phase. No default name, label or copy embeds a date.

**Migrations:** none. No RLS, auth, payments or permissions are touched. No table changes. (Phase 1's fix for lm-templates-06 may need one — see above.)

### Steps

**A. One rulebook for a starting weight (lm-templates-01, lm-build-own-01) — and one rule "a self-built week starts under a name" (lm-build-own-06)**

1. **One working-weight rule, in one place; a nameless self-built start is refused at the door.** `src/programs/schemas.ts` · new exported `WorkingWeightsSchema`, new exported `EnrollSchema`. Today: the enrol route spells its own rule inline — `workingWeights: z.record(z.string(), z.number().positive())` (`app/api/programs/enrollments/route.ts:12`) — while `UpdateScheduleSchema` (`schemas.ts:213`) and `DraftBody` (`schemas.ts:359`) each spell out `min(0).max(MAX_WEIGHT_KG)` separately (six mentions of `MAX_WEIGHT_KG` in the file); three copies, one of them wrong, so a 0 kg push-up can be saved as a draft and edited into a running program but never started. The route's schema has no `label` at all (`route.ts:7–14`), although `enrollInProgram` accepts one (`src/db/programRepo.ts:256`). Change: export `WorkingWeightsSchema = z.record(z.string(), z.number().min(0).max(MAX_WEIGHT_KG))` once; `UpdateScheduleSchema`, `DraftBody.workingWeights` and the new `EnrollSchema` (moved out of the route: `programId`, `level`, `unitSystem`, `oneRepMaxes`, `workingWeights: WorkingWeightsSchema.optional()`, `customSchedule: CustomScheduleSchema.nullish()`, `label: DraftBody.name.optional()`) all reference the same object. Add `.superRefine`: when `programId === CUSTOM_PROGRAM_ID` (`src/programs/data/customProgram.ts:28`) and `label` is absent or blank → issue at path `label`, message "Give this week a name before starting it." — the server is the one owner of "a self-built week starts under a name", so no screen (today's builder, Phase 8's `?view=build`, a curl) can create another "Your own program". `oneRepMaxes` stays `.positive()` with a one-line comment: a one-rep max of nothing is not a max. `startDraft` (`src/db/programDraftRepo.ts:190–229`) already passes `label: draft.name` and is unaffected. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/schemas.test.ts` — "a bodyweight lift at 0 kg is accepted by enrol, schedule update and draft alike", "the three weight maps are one rule: −1, MAX_WEIGHT_KG + 1 and 1000 are refused identically and `EnrollSchema.shape.workingWeights`, `UpdateScheduleSchema.shape.workingWeights` and `CreateDraftSchema.shape.workingWeights` unwrap to the same `WorkingWeightsSchema` object" (so a fourth inline copy cannot appear without failing it), "a custom programId with no label is refused at path `label`; StrongLifts with no label is accepted".

2. **The enrol route says which field was wrong.** `app/api/programs/enrollments/route.ts` · `POST`. Today: lines 7–14 define the schema locally; line 35 answers the bare string "Validation failed", which `WorkoutPrograms.tsx:203–206` and `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx:231–235` show verbatim. Change: import `EnrollSchema` from `src/programs/schemas.ts`; on failure `const issue = parsed.error.issues[0]; return err(\`${issue.path.join(".")}: ${issue.message}\`, 400)` (the schedule route at `app/api/programs/enrollments/[id]/schedule/route.ts:15` returns only `issues[0]?.message`; the path is added here because the weight map has one entry per lift and the message alone does not say which); `parsed.data` (now carrying `label`) goes to `enrollInProgram` unchanged. Phase 0 step 5 edits this same file (`ProgramBusy` → 409); apply Phase 0 first and keep its branch. The route stays under 50 lines (architecture test). *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/enrollRoute.test.ts` (mocks `@/src/db/auth` and `@/src/db/programRepo`) — "POST with `{ lib_push_up: 0 }` reaches enrollInProgram with the zero intact", "POST with `{ lib_push_up: -1 }` is refused with a body naming `workingWeights.lib_push_up`", "POST for a custom week with no label is refused with a body naming `label`; with a label the label reaches enrollInProgram".

**B. Day names: one trim rule, applied on commit, not per keystroke (lm-templates-04, lm-build-own-04)**

3. **A text box that lets you type.** `components/ui/draft-input.tsx` · new `DraftInput` (built on `Input` from `components/ui/input.tsx`, so it is the app's kit, not the programs kit). Today: three day-name inputs are controlled by the stored name and push every keystroke through a trimming commit function — `ProgramEditor.tsx:163` and `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx:496` call `renameDay` (`src/programs/customize.ts:176–183`, trims and throws on empty; the `|| day.label` guard snaps an emptied box back), `RoutineCard.tsx:589` calls `renameSplitDay` (`northStarService.ts:1366–1373`, trims and ignores empty) — so a trailing space never survives a render and "Upper Body" is untypeable. `StepMinutes` in the same file (`RoutineCard.tsx:649–665`) already holds a local draft; that is the pattern. Change: `DraftInput({ value, onCommit, ...inputProps })` holds the draft locally while focused; commits `onCommit(draft.trim())` on blur and Enter only when non-empty and different from `value`; an emptied box restores `value` on blur and commits nothing; an outside change to `value` replaces the draft while the box is not focused. *Test:* `tests/unit/shared/draftInput.test.tsx` (there is no `tests/unit/components/`; `tests/unit/shared/` exists) — "a space typed at the end stays on screen and 'Upper A' is committed once, on blur", "Enter commits", "clearing the box and leaving restores the old name and calls onCommit zero times", "a value changed from outside replaces the draft while unfocused".

4. **Every day name goes through it.** `src/programs/components/ProgramEditor.tsx:161–165`, `src/programs/components/CustomProgramBuilder.tsx:494–497`, `src/goals/components/north-star/RoutineCard.tsx:587–591` · the three `<input value={…label} onChange=…>` rows. Change: each becomes `<DraftInput value={day.label} onCommit={(name) => apply(() => renameDay(schedule, day.id, name))} …/>` (RoutineCard: `handlers.onRenameSplitDay(routine.id, d.id, name)`); `renameDay` / `renameSplitDay` keep their trim as the commit rule. `EditActiveProgram` is covered because it renders `ProgramEditor` (`EditActiveProgram.tsx:146`). *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/dayNameRule.test.ts` — "no day-name input commits per keystroke": reads every `.tsx` under `src/` and, for every line containing `renameDay(` or `renameSplitDay(`, fails unless that line or one of the three lines above it contains `onCommit`; and separately fails if any line contains both `onChange` and either call. The count is zero after this step; there is no allowlist to grow.

**C. The written week keeps what you meant (lm-build-own-02, lm-build-own-03)**

5. **The same lift on two days is one lift, in text too.** `src/programs/programText.ts` · `parseProgramText`; `src/programs/customize.ts` · `freshId` (155) and `allExerciseIds` (162) become exported; `src/programs/data/exerciseLibrary.ts` · new exported `customLiftId(name: string): string` (the `custom_${slug}` rule lifted out of `customLibraryEntry`, line 465–470, which then calls it). Today: `programText.ts:228` mints a positional id `${day.id}_e${n}` BEFORE `parseLift` resolves the name, so "Bench Press" on Push A and Push B are `d1_e1` and `d2_e1` — two working weights that drift apart — while the click-to-add path deliberately shares the id (`customize.ts:314–345 addExercise`, "THE SAME LIFT ON TWO DAYS IS ONE LIFT"); `customLibraryEntry` needs a `BodyGroup` the parser does not have. Change: `parseLift` is called with a placeholder and the id is assigned after the name resolves, the way `addExercise` does — a library match uses `entry.id` (`lib_bench_press`), reused when that id already exists on another day and suffixed with `freshId` only for a repeat within one day; an unrecognised name uses `customLiftId(name)`, so a typed "Zercher Squat" and a palette-added one are the same lift. The weights map is keyed by these ids; `formatProgramText` (272) needs no change. Enrollments already running on positional ids are untouched (their `exerciseState` is keyed by their own schedule). *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/programText.test.ts` — "the same lift on two days gets one id and one starting weight", "the same lift twice in one day is two slots", "a typed unknown lift and a palette-added one share an id" (asserts `parseProgramText("Push\nZercher Squat 3x8 @40").schedule` ids equal `customLibraryEntry("Zercher Squat", "legs")!.id`); every existing round-trip test in the file (lines 295–330) stays green; `tests/unit/programs/lifts.test.ts` — "customLibraryEntry's id is customLiftId's" (one slug rule).

6. **Re-applying the text keeps pins, progression, weight steps and notes.** `src/programs/programText.ts` · `carryAuthoredSettings` (314–334). Today: lines 322–333 rebuild each day as `{id, label, exercises}` and never copy `weekday`; the per-lift carry matches on id AND name (327), which after step 5 works for library ids but still loses everything for a lift whose id changed (an old positional-id draft loaded from the account). Change: match days by label (trimmed, case-insensitive) first, else by index when the day count is unchanged — deterministic, never a guess; copy `weekday` from the matched day; within a matched day carry `progression` (which holds `incrementKg`/`incrementLb`), `note` and `dropSets` by exercise id, else by exercise name. *Test:* same file — "re-applying the text keeps a Monday pin, 'leave it to me', the weight step and the note" (the exact round trip from the finding: BEFORE weekday 1 + progression none → AFTER identical), "renaming one day keeps its pin", "a lift whose id changed but whose name did not keeps its settings".

**D. The plan stores a reference, never a copy (lm-templates-02, lm-templates-03, lm-build-own-05, critic-systems-step, walk-08)**

7. **The reference shrinks to the one fact that decides anything — and is actually read back.** `src/goals/types.ts` · `NsRoutineProgram` (1718–1727); `src/goals/northStarService.ts` · `loadNsPlan` (479, routine mapping 520–575). Today: the type carries `programId`, `label` and `startedAt` beside `enrollmentId` — copies, justified in the comment by a signed-out render that no longer exists (`/life-mastery` is signed-in gated, `app/life-mastery/page.tsx:36–38`); and `loadNsPlan` builds each routine without a `program` field at all, so whatever `onProgramStarted` or `applyProgramReference` wrote is gone on the next reload — the same-browser half of walk-08 and of lm-build-own-07's "next visit". Change: `NsRoutineProgram = { enrollmentId: string }`; `loadNsPlan` reads `r.program` and keeps `{ enrollmentId }` when `typeof r.program?.enrollmentId === "string"`, else `null` (old plans with the three extra fields load with only the id). **K. The Track step knows you trained (critic-track-step)**

22. **A finished workout ticks the training step; the tick is derived, not hand-kept.** `src/health/healthService.ts` · new pure `workoutsByLocalDate(logs: Array<{ logged_at: string; session_type: SessionType }>, timezone: string): Map<string, SessionType[]>` and new exported `STEP_FOR_SESSION_TYPE: Record<SessionType, "strength" | "cardio" | "mobility">` (`weights → strength`, `cardio | running → cardio`, `mobility | yoga → mobility`; the ids are the workout routine's library steps, `src/goals/data/northStar.ts:1353–1359`); `app/api/health/workout/route.ts` · `GET` (11–20) adds `local_date` per row from `getUserTimezone` (both the plain and the `include=sets` branch; if the route crosses 50 lines the body moves to `healthService.withLocalDate`); `src/goals/components/north-star/TrackTab.tsx` and `TrackSchedule.tsx`. Today: the Track step's ticks are `plan.logged[date]` in the localStorage plan (`northStarTrackService.ts:744–762`); nothing under `src/goals/components/north-star` reads workout logs (grep is empty), so a week with three finished sessions shows zero on the "Strength session" step until it is ticked by hand — two records of one workout that can disagree; `healthService.workoutsOnDate` (420) keys by the running process's clock, which on the server is UTC. Change: `workoutsByLocalDate` uses `toZonedDate` + `toDateISO` from `src/shared/dateUtils.ts` (never `toISOString().split`); TrackTab (already in `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA`, `architecture.test.ts:629`) loads `/api/health/workout?days=9` through `useLoad` (`src/shared/useLoad.ts:46` — the loader the architecture rule names; 9 days covers the 8-column week grid in any timezone since `getWorkoutLogs` cuts by server time, `healthRepo.ts:234`); for the workout routine, a step whose id is `STEP_FOR_SESSION_TYPE[session_type]` reads done on any `local_date` with a finished session of that type; the derived tick is computed at render (`stepLogged || derived`), never written into `plan.logged`; it shows "from your training log" and its manual toggle is disabled for that day. A failed load shows amber "Could not read your training log — ticks below are hand-ticked only" with Try again and leaves the hand ticks; never a silently empty week. - `tests/unit/programs/schemas.test.ts` — a 0 kg lift passes enrol, schedule update and draft through one shared schema object; the class (a fourth inline weight rule) cannot reappear without the identity assertion failing; a nameless custom start is refused by the server, so no screen can mint another "Your own program".

- `tests/unit/programs/programText.test.ts` + `lifts.test.ts` — one lift on two days is one id under one slug rule; a text re-apply keeps pins, progression, steps and notes; every existing round-trip test still passes.
- `tests/unit/goals/programReferenceKept.test.ts` + `programReferenceReconciled.test.ts` — the plan stores only an enrollment id, READS IT BACK (fails today), starting writes nothing else, and the reference is corrected on open (ended elsewhere → detached; started elsewhere → adopted, routine added if missing; failed list → untouched).
- `tests/unit/programs/describeTrainingWeek.test.ts` — one describing function; no in-turn program ever prints a per-week count; a swapped lift is named from the enrollment's own schedule.
- `tests/unit/goals/routineCardLinked.test.tsx` + `northStarService.test.ts` — a linked week is read-only and derived; several running is a count, not a week; a failed list shows no number; `planAsText` prints no copied day list.

- `tests/unit/programs/programNaming.test.ts` — nothing names an enrollment from the catalogue shell (three accessor forms); the live header and the displaced list use the week's own name.
- `tests/unit/programs/customBuilderStart.test.tsx` + `runningCopyOf.test.ts` + `savedWeeks.test.tsx` — a self-built week starts under a name, a running design shows "Running since" instead of Start, displacement is named, loading a saved week enables Save and saves back to that week.

- `tests/unit/goals/northStarToday.test.ts` — Life Mastery's "today" comes from the profile timezone, and no north-star component decides it alone.
- `tests/e2e/life-mastery-program-link.spec.ts` (training project) + the corrected `life-mastery-saved-weeks.spec.ts` — the whole link, in a browser, including a fresh browser context for the same account seeded without any program data, and the names on Tracking, Training, the live header and History.

### Attack log
- **Step 23 (insert first, deactivate second) would break every second self-built start.** `supabase/migrations/20260618_create_program_tables.sql:30` has a partial unique index on `(user_id, program_id) WHERE is_active`; inserting a second active `custom` row (or restarting the running program, or step 17's "Start a fresh copy") fails before the old row is deactivated. The finding (lm-templates-06) is also Phase 1's by scope. Removed the step and open question 6; handed Phase 1 the index fact and the transactional recommendation in the "Left to other phases" block.
- **`loadNsPlan` never reads the program reference back** (`northStarService.ts:520–575` builds routines without `program`), so the reference is dropped on every reload today; the draft's step 7 said "accepts old plans" as if the field were read. Step 7 now adds the read and its test asserts a serialize→load round trip (fails today).
- **Step 12 named the wrong parent.** `RoutineCard` is mounted by `MilestonesTab.tsx:307`, not NorthStarFlow; `linked` must be threaded through MilestonesTab's props (85–110). Added the file, and a `several` state for 2–3 running (spec S4 says "N programs running", the draft printed one week).
- **Step 12's "Back lands on ?step=systems" was untrue**: `readReturn` (`src/shared/returnTo.ts:28`) has no callers, so `/programs` ignores `?from=` until Phase 5. The link now carries the return address for Phase 5, the claim is removed from the e2e, and the opening paragraph says so.
- **Step 24's fresh-context assertion could not pass as written**: `DEFAULT_ROUTINE_IDS` (`northStar.ts:1493`) has no workout routine, and RoutineCard only renders for an opened routine. Step 9's reconcile now adds the routine via `applyProgramToWorkoutRoutine`, and the spec seeds a plan without program data and opens the routine.
- **Step 20 cited `fromKg`/`toKg` in `programsService.ts`; they are in `src/shared/weight.ts:43–48`**, and `roundToLoadable(weight, unit, style)` needs the lift's load style. Fixed the symbols and the test's expected value.

Replaced with `window.history.replaceState`, which Next 16 keeps in step with the App Router, and added a unit test that `useRouter` is not used for it.

Added `customLiftId(name)` as the one slug rule (used by `customLibraryEntry` too, with a test) and the reorder.

**Found while building it, 19 September (all 25 steps).**
- Step 12's stepper was first hidden with a CSS class rather than not rendered — a control a keyboard can still reach and a screen reader still announces.
- Step 7's change made a freshly created routine differ in SHAPE from a loaded one: no `program` key versus `program: null`. A saved-then-loaded plan no longer equalled the plan it came from, which an existing round-trip test caught. One shape now.
- Step 22's derived map speaks library-step ids (`"strength"`, the same on everybody's plan) while the schedule's rows use the plan's own step ids (`"s3"`, which are not). Translated once, in TrackTab, the only place holding both.
- Step 23's plan said SeasonBand takes `today` from the flow. It does not: `ProgressDashboard` mounts it, with no props, from the tracking dashboard. Held at exactly one browser-clock read with a named, shrinking allowance rather than pretended fixed.
- Step 20's `convertTyped` was a new direct import from `src/goals` into `src/programs`, which step 11's own guard refused. Routed through `forLifeMastery.ts` — the door working as intended on the first thing that tried to go round it.
- The UTC-date guard read whole files including comments, so the moment a second file explained the rule in its own words it fired on the explanation. It reads code now, and its shrink test reads the same way so the two halves cannot disagree.
- Step 18's first by-id test renamed "Monday Push" to "Monday push", which the OLD by-name match found anyway because it lowercased both — it passed under both implementations. It renames to something matching no saved week now, and goes red when the old matching is restored.
- Step 24's first draft asserted no per-week number anywhere on the page. A routine's individual steps legitimately carry their own cadence and their day pickers list those as options; the assertion found ten. Scoped to the card, and the stepper asserted through its buttons.
- Step 25 surfaced a real behaviour change: the saved-weeks spec pressed "Save this week" to save a LOADED week under a new name. That now means "save back to the week I opened" — the point of step 18 — so the spec presses "Save as new".
- Step 24 also caught three things no unit test could: a text box behind a toggle, a label that did not exist, and a navigation racing the POST, which read as "the name did not stick" when the truth was "nothing was started".

### Open questions for Phase 2

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** Confirm design-spec Decision 11: the Life Mastery plan keeps only which program the training week follows (an id), and the week's days, name and shape are read live from the database every time. This means the Systems step can no longer rename or reorder the days of a week a program runs (it links to the Training page instead), and the copied 'N days a week' line disappears for program-linked weeks. It is the only way a phone and a laptop can never show different weeks. (Found while checking: the plan already forgets the reference on every reload, so nothing you rely on today is lost.)

*Recommendation:* Yes — one copy of the fact, read where it is decided.

**Open question 2.** For programs that run their days in turn (StrongLifts A/B/A, Starting Strength, Upper/Lower), should the week line say 'Workout A · Workout B, in turn' as the spec chose, or should each program declare a sessions-per-week number (3 for StrongLifts, 4 for Upper/Lower…) as the finding's fix suggested, so the line can say '3×/wk'?

*Recommendation:* 'In turn', per the spec. The engine does not schedule by week — it prescribes the next session whenever you turn up — so a '3×/wk' would be a promise the app does not keep, and it would contradict the Track grid the first week you train twice.

**Open question 3.** When two or three programs are running at once (test account B has three), which one does the plan's training week follow, and what does the Systems card show?

*Recommendation:* Keep the one it already follows if it is still running; otherwise the most recently started. The Systems card then says 'N programs running — see Training' rather than picking a week to print, and ending the extras happens on the Training page as the spec says.

**Open question 4.** Life Mastery currently decides 'today' from the browser's clock on every step (NorthStarFlow.tsx:234, and SeasonBand on its own). Step 23 switches it to the account's timezone (the one Settings holds), so a workout finished at 23:45 in Copenhagen ticks Monday even if the laptop is set to another zone. This changes 'today' for every Life Mastery step, not just training, for anyone whose device clock and profile timezone differ.

*Recommendation:* Yes — one clock for the whole app; it is the same rule the training feature already follows.

**Open question 5.** When you start a week you built but never saved, the app now needs a name for it (the server refuses a nameless self-built week, so it is never 'Your own program' anywhere). Should it always ask, prefilled with the day names ('Push / Pull / Legs') so one tap works, or silently use that default?

*Recommendation:* Ask, prefilled. One tap costs nothing and a week you named is one you can find in History a year later.

**Open question 6.** Steps 16–18, 20 and 21 make the current Life Mastery builder, saved-weeks box and inline editor behave correctly, but Phase 8 (design spec S4, Decisions 5 and 11) deletes those screens and moves their jobs to the Training page. This phase puts every rule in a file that survives (schemas, services, hooks, the parser) and does only the thinnest wiring in the doomed screens so you get the fixes now. Do you want that — fixes visible immediately, some wiring redone in Phase 8 — or only the surviving rules now with the screens fixed when Phase 8 rebuilds them?

*Recommendation:* Rules now, thin wiring now. Everything you can see today works before Phase 8, and the wiring Phase 8 throws away is a few dozen lines per component; the rules are the part that would be expensive to get wrong twice.


### Blockers for Phase 2

No blockers.


## Phase 3 — The Tracking page is the door — build S1
**What you can do after:** Open Tracking and a training card is always in the first screen: it says what today is (a session with its lifts by name, a run described in words, a rest day with what is next, "Trained today", or a workout you left open) and has one button that does the obvious thing. Start opens exactly the session the card named, "today" is your account's day and not the server's or the phone's, and when the card cannot find out it says so instead of disappearing. While it loads a grey placeholder holds its place; the page still grows once when the card fills in (the placeholder is shorter than the card), but it no longer appears late and never flips from Start to Resume.
**Why it is in this order:** It sits on Phase 0 (a Start that cannot open the wrong day or get stuck on a used key), and Phases 5, 6 and 8 build on the three things this phase creates once: the state function, the one loader, and the training address book.
**Findings this phase closes:** walk-01-card-absent-without-program, tracking-card-detail-failure-vanishes, tracking-card-padding-and-gap, tracking-card-label-and-button-scale, tracking-card-pops-in-and-flips, tracking-card-rest-day-dead-end, tracking-card-second-program-hidden, tracking-card-says-start-after-you-finished, tracking-card-error-copy-contradicts-todaycard, tracking-card-e2e-never-taps-start, tests-and-ci-02, tests-and-ci-03

**Depends on (not fixed here, named so nothing is silently postponed):**
- Phase 0 — the SERVER half of the start key and the wrong-day rule: `startWorkout` (src/db/workoutRepo.ts:148–151) defaults a missing `dayId` to the day `getTodaySession` names (group {tracking-card-start-opens-wrong-day, programs-today-01, data-integrity-02}), and a `client_key` that names an already-finished workout is treated as spent rather than thrown as Postgres text (group {programs-today-04, live-workout-01, data-integrity-01}). This phase owns the CLIENT half outright (step 9: the key is forgotten on every reply that arrives) and makes the card send `dayId`, so the browser test in step 15 passes with or without Phase 0.
- Phase 4 — the receipt page `/programs/workout/[id]` (spec S3 element 24, Decision 7). The done-today button targets it through one constant (step 10). Open question 2 says what happens if Phase 4 has not run yet.
- Phase 5 — `/programs?view=programs`, `?program=<id>` and `?tab=` (spec prerequisite (f)); `TodayCard.tsx` and `StartLooseWorkout.tsx` switching to `startWorkoutRequest` (step 9); deleting `TodayCard.tsx:42 const STALE_HOURS = 6`, a second copy of `STALE_WORKOUT_HOURS` (programsService.ts:1928); and the "last-known card stays on a failed refetch" behaviour S2 asks for, which `useLoad` does not yet carry. Until Phase 5 reads the parameters, the links open `/programs` as it is today — not wrong, only less precise.
- Phase 6 — the live screen's summary button honours `?from` (spec S3 element 24). `components/BackLink.tsx:50–52` already reads `?from`, so the header's back link says "Tracking" from this phase on; the summary's fixed "Back to training" (`src/programs/components/live/LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx:178`) is Phase 6's.
- Phase 9 — Decision 1 (the root `outline` button fix in `components/ui/button.tsx`). The card's button is tinted-primary and reads correctly with or without it.

### Steps

1. **One shape for the facts the door needs, and the type rule finally covers training.** `src/programs/types.ts` · `TrainingDoorFacts`, `TrainingCardState`, `AlsoRunning`, `LiftProgress`, `PlateLoad`; `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` · `slices` (line 192), `ALLOWED_TYPE_EXPORTS` (line 38). Today: `TrainingCardState` is exported from `src/programs/programsService.ts:1920–1926`, beside `LiftProgress` (:1447) and `PlateLoad` (:1725) — and nothing notices, because the test "Type exports should only be in types.ts files" walks `slices = ['qa','inner-game','scenarios','tracking','profile','settings','articles']` and never looks at `src/programs`. There is no type for "everything the card must know"; the card assembles it from three requests (`TrainingCard.tsx:35–37, 85–108`). Change: (a) add `'programs'` to `slices`; move `TrainingCardState`, `LiftProgress` and `PlateLoad` into `types.ts` (programsService imports them; no re-export); seed `ALLOWED_TYPE_EXPORTS` with exactly the other `src/programs` files the test then reports — today's grep finds `src/programs/trainingSettings.ts`, `src/programs/hooks/useLiveWorkout.ts`, `src/programs/customize.ts`, `src/programs/programText.ts`, `src/programs/components/live/SetRow.tsx` (the executor runs the test and lists what it reports, not this list) — each with a one-line "why" comment; the list may only shrink from there. (b) Widen `TrainingCardState` to the spec's states: `live` (`workoutId, enrollmentId: string | null, dayLabel: string | null, startedAt, setsTicked: number, setsAsked: number | null, also`), `stale` (same fields), `today` (`enrollmentId, dayId, dayLabel, lifts: string[], endurance?: { blocks: string; minutes: number }, lastTime?: { loggedAt: string; setsDone: number; complete: boolean }, also`), `rest` (`enrollmentId, nextDayId, nextLabel, nextWeekday?, also`), `done` (`workoutId, enrollmentId: string | null, dayLabel: string | null, durationMin: number | null, sets: number | null, next: { label: string; weekday?: number } | null, also`), `finished` (`enrollmentId, name, sessions: number, startedAt, also`), `none`. `AlsoRunning = { enrollmentId: string; name: string; todayLabel: string | null }` (null = resting today). (c) Add `TrainingDoorFacts`: `{ timezone: string; todayDate: string; todayWeekday: number; live: { id; enrollmentId: string | null; dayId: string | null; dayLabel: string | null; startedAt: string; setsTicked: number; setsAsked: number | null } | null; programs: Array<{ enrollment: ProgramEnrollment; prescription: SessionPrescription; next: { dayId: string; label: string; weekday?: number } | null; lastTimeThisDay: { loggedAt: string; setsDone: number; complete: boolean } | null }>; recentlyFinished: Array<{ workoutId: string; enrollmentId: string | null; dayLabel: string | null; loggedAt: string; durationMin: number | null; sets: number | null }> }`. `todayDate` is `YYYY-MM-DD` in the account's timezone; `recentlyFinished` is the last 48 hours, deliberately NOT filtered to today — step 7 is the one place that decides what "today" contains. Nothing in the facts is a formatted string. *Test:* `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts — "Type exports should only be in types.ts files"` — now fails if `TrainingCardState` (or any new type) is put back in `programsService.ts`, because `programs` is scanned and `programsService.ts` is not on the allowlist.

2. **One server read that knows whose day it is.** `src/db/trainingDoorRepo.ts` (new) · `getTrainingDoorFacts(userId)`. Today: the card chains `/api/programs/enrollments` → `/api/programs/enrollments/[id]` → `/api/workouts/live` in the browser (`TrainingCard.tsx:35–37, 90`), never reads `lastLoggedAt` (`programRepo.ts:176` computes it), never knows today's finished workouts, and formats weekdays with the browser's clock (`TrainingCard.tsx:188`). Change: one function that composes what exists — `getUserTimezone` (`settingsRepo.ts:153`), `listActiveEnrollments` (`programRepo.ts:139`), `getTodaySession` per enrollment (`programRepo.ts:590`), `getLiveWorkout` (`workoutRepo.ts:183`), `prescriptionForDay` for the open workout's `enrollmentId`+`dayId` (`workoutRepo.ts:618`; `setsAsked` = sum of `exercises[].sets.length`, `dayLabel` = its `dayLabel`; both null for a loose workout) — plus two new bounded reads in this file: (a) the newest finished workout for `(user_id, enrollment_id, program_day_id = prescription.dayId)` with `adjustments` and `workout_sets(count)`, `.order("logged_at", { ascending: false }).limit(1)`, giving `lastTimeThisDay = { loggedAt, setsDone, complete }` where `complete` = the stored `adjustments` has no `incomplete` and no `skipped` entries (the record the finish wrote — the prescription's set count today is NOT used, because a program edit would make the two disagree); (b) finished workouts with `logged_at >= now − 48 h`, `.order("logged_at", desc).limit(50)`, with `duration_min`, `program_day_id`, `enrollment_id`, `workout_sets(count)`; `dayLabel` resolved from the enrollment's schedule day (`scheduleDays`) or null. Both reads go through `finishedWorkouts()` (`healthRepo.ts:190`), and the `.limit()` must sit INSIDE the builder passed to `finishedWorkouts(...)` — the unpaged-read counter reads the chain from `.from(` to the closing bracket of the wrapping call, so a `.limit()` chained after `)` is invisible to it and the file would count as unbounded. `todayDate = getTodayInTimezone(timezone)` and `todayWeekday = isoWeekdayInTimezone(timezone)` (`src/shared/dateUtils.ts:23, 102`); `next` from step 5; `setsTicked = live.sets.filter(s => s.completedAt).length`. `new Date()` appears here only as "now" for the 48 h window, never as "today". *Test:* `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts — "no NEW read that asks for more rows than the database will return"` and `"the unpaged-read allowance only shrinks"` stay green with `src/db/trainingDoorRepo.ts` absent from `UNPAGED_READS_ALLOWED` (zero unbounded reads); behaviour is proven end to end by every state test in step 15, which run through the real route against the real database.

3. **One request for the door.** `app/api/programs/today/route.ts` (new) · `GET`. Today: no such route; the card's three requests arrive at three moments, which is the pop-in and the flip (`tracking-card-pops-in-and-flips`). Change: `requireAuth`, `NextResponse.json(await getTrainingDoorFacts(auth.userId))`, 500 with `{ error }` on throw; under 50 lines; imports only `@/src/db/auth` and `@/src/db/trainingDoorRepo`. *Test:* `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts — "API routes should be under 50 lines of code"`; behaviour under `tests/e2e/dashboard-training-card.spec.ts — "when the door cannot be loaded the card says so and offers no Start"` (step 15).

4. **Which program the card is about is a tested rule, not a position in a list.** `src/programs/programsService.ts` · `chooseCardEnrollment(facts, trainedTodayEnrollmentId)`. Today: `const running = enrollments[0]` (`TrainingCard.tsx:36`) — the newest started, whatever it is; two programs and the second is invisible. Change: pure, exported: (0) if `facts.live` belongs to a program, that program; (1) else if `trainedTodayEnrollmentId` is set (decided by step 7, the one owner of "today"), that program — so the done state, its label-row link and its "Also today" line all name the same program; (2) else among programs whose `prescription.restDay` is not true and `prescription.isComplete` is not true, the most recently trained (`enrollment.lastLoggedAt` desc, `types.ts:499`); ties → earliest `started_at`; never-trained ones only when no trained one qualifies; (3) if none qualifies, the same ordering over all programs (the card then shows that one's rest or finished state); (4) `null` with no programs. Returns the chosen entry and `also` (every other program as `AlsoRunning`, `todayLabel` = its `prescription.dayLabel`, or null when `restDay`). *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/chooseCardEnrollment.test.ts` — "prefers a program with a session due today over one resting today"; "among those due, the most recently trained wins and ties go to the earliest started"; "a never-trained program is chosen only when nothing else qualifies"; "the program the open workout belongs to wins outright"; "the program trained today wins over one still due"; "never picks by position — a newer never-trained program loses to an older trained one".

5. **"What is next" has one owner.** `src/programs/programsService.ts` · `nextSessionAfterToday(program, enrollment, todayWeekday)`. Today: the rest branch reads `prescription.dayLabel`/`scheduledWeekday` (`TrainingCard.tsx:252–253`), and after a session is finished on a weekday-pinned program nothing can say what is next (`getTodaySession`, `programRepo.ts:606–618`, names today's own day again). Change: pure, exported: for a weekday-anchored schedule, `pickTodaysDay(schedule, (todayWeekday % 7) + 1)` (`programsService.ts:188`; verified: with tomorrow's weekday it returns tomorrow's pinned day, or the nearest pinned day after it wrapping past Sunday via `(w − today + 7) % 7`, with `scheduledWeekday`) → `{ dayId: days[dayIndex].id, label, weekday: scheduledWeekday }`; for a sequence program, `computePrescription(program, enrollment)` (the cursor's next day, which a finished session has already advanced) → `{ dayId, label }` with no weekday. Used by step 2 for `next`, by the rest state and the done state alike. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/nextSessionAfterToday.test.ts` — "on a pinned week the next session after today is the next pinned weekday, wrapping past Sunday"; "on a sequence program it is the cursor's next day and carries no weekday"; "on a Friday-only week the next session after Friday is next Friday".

6. **An endurance day is described in words, never as "0 lifts".** `src/programs/programsService.ts` · `describeEndurance(sets: EnduranceSet[])`. Today: `lifts: prescription.exercises.length` is 0 for a run and the card prints nothing for it (`programsService.ts:1957`, `TrainingCard.tsx:201`); `enduranceMinutes` exists (`programsService.ts:1895`) and `TodayCard.tsx:153–158` hand-formats blocks with " → ". Change: pure, exported: `{ blocks: "4 × 4 min run / 3 min walk", minutes: enduranceMinutes(sets) }` — `repeat > 1` becomes the `N ×` prefix, block labels joined with " / ", sets joined with " · ". Phase 5 makes `TodayCard` use it. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/describeEndurance.test.ts` — "names the blocks and the repeat count"; "adds the minutes the plan takes and never a lift count".

7. **The decision is one pure function, fed by the facts — and it alone decides what "today" contains.** `src/programs/programsService.ts` · `trainingCardState(facts, now = new Date())`. Today: `trainingCardState(live, prescription, enrollmentId, now)` (`programsService.ts:1930–1960`) has four kinds, no input for "already trained today" (`tracking-card-says-start-after-you-finished`), no lift names, no second program. Change: signature becomes `(facts: TrainingDoorFacts, now?: Date): TrainingCardState`. First it computes `trainedToday = facts.recentlyFinished.filter(w => getTodayInTimezone(facts.timezone, new Date(w.loggedAt)) === facts.todayDate)` — the only place in the app that turns a workout's instant into "today" for the door, which is what makes a 23:45 Copenhagen workout count as Monday and a 00:10 one as Tuesday. Order of precedence: `live`/`stale` (by `STALE_WORKOUT_HOURS`; elapsed = `now − startedAt`, a duration) → `done` if `trainedToday` is non-empty (the newest; program or loose alike — the door never re-offers the next session on a day you trained) → `none` if no programs → chosen program via step 4 (passing the newest trained-today workout's `enrollmentId`): `finished` when `prescription.isComplete` → `rest` (with `next` from the facts) → `today` (`lifts` = exercise names in order; `endurance` from step 6 when `enduranceSets` is set and `exercises` is empty; `lastTime` from `lastTimeThisDay`; `also`). The `also` array is on every state that names a program. No string is formatted here — labels, counts and instants come out; wording is the card's. `now` is used only for elapsed minutes; "today" is `facts.todayDate`, computed by the server in the account's timezone. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/trainingCardState.test.ts` (rewritten) — "a workout in progress beats everything and counts its ticked sets"; "a workout open longer than STALE_WORKOUT_HOURS is still-open, not running"; "names today's session and its lifts by name, never a count"; "an endurance day carries its blocks and minutes and no lift list"; "a rest day carries the next session and its weekday"; "a session finished today is done, and the next session is not offered"; "a workout finished at 23:45 Copenhagen counts as Monday and one at 00:10 counts as Tuesday" (`recentlyFinished` holding a workout at `2026-09-14T21:45:00Z` → `done`; holding only one at `2026-09-14T22:10:00Z` → `today`; `timezone: "Europe/Copenhagen"`, `todayDate: "2026-09-14"`, and the same two instants with `timezone: "UTC"` give the opposite answers — proving the timezone, not the instant, decides); "with no program running the state is none"; "a finished program with nothing else due is finished"; "two programs: the chosen one leads and the other is named as also-running with its own today". Phase 5 consumes this same function for the /programs Today card (spec cross-cutting rule); it must not grow a second copy.

8. **One loader — the app's own, not a new one.** `src/programs/hooks/useTrainingDoor.ts` (new) · `useTrainingDoor()`, `parseTrainingDoorFacts(body)`. Today: the card fetches in the component (`TrainingCard.tsx:85–108`) with no "settled" flag, so the state is computed with `live = null` until the live request returns — the Start→Resume flip. `src/shared/useLoad.ts` is the shared loader `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts:604–609` names as the one a new screen must use; the previous draft invented a second. Change: `useTrainingDoor = () => useLoad<TrainingDoorFacts>(TRAINING_DOOR_API, parseTrainingDoorFacts)` — returns `Load<TrainingDoorFacts>` (`loading` | `ready` with `data` | `failed` with `retry`). `parseTrainingDoorFacts` throws on anything that is not the shape of step 1 (a body it cannot read is not data), so non-ok, non-JSON, thrown and wrong-shape all land in `failed`. One request on every mount (a mount is a page the person is looking at now — `useEnrollment.ts:78–88` reasoning); nothing decision-bearing exists until `ready`. The fetch lives in the hook, not a component, so `TrainingCard.tsx` leaves `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` (step 14). The S1 failed state shows no facts, so "keep the last-known facts on failure" is not needed here; S2 needs it and Phase 5 extends `useLoad` for it. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/useTrainingDoor.test.tsx` (renderHook, `vi.stubGlobal("fetch", …)` as `enrollmentLoadFailure.test.tsx` does) — "is loading until the one request answers, then ready with the facts"; "a 500 is failed, not 'no program'"; "a dropped connection is failed, not 'no program'"; "a body in the wrong shape is failed, not ready"; "retry asks again and is ready on success".

9. **One start request, one wording, one owner of the retry key on the client.** `src/programs/startWorkoutRequest.ts` (new) · `startWorkoutRequest({ enrollmentId, dayId })`. Today: `TrainingCard.startNow` (`TrainingCard.tsx:59–81`) posts no `dayId` (`:68`, with a comment claiming the server falls back to today's session — false, see Phase 0) and on a network error asserts "nothing was started" (`:77`), the wording `TodayCard.tsx:84–90` explicitly rejected; nothing clears the key after a reply (`startKeyFor`/`clearStartKey`, `useLiveWorkout.ts:423, 440`); a 409 "already in progress" offers `/programs`, not the workout. Change: the one client owner of the request: body `{ enrollmentId, dayId, clientKey: startKeyFor(enrollmentId) }` → `{ ok: true, workoutId }` | `{ ok: false, status: number, message: string }` (any HTTP reply that is not ok, with the server's plain words) | `{ ok: false, lostReply: true, message: "Could not reach the server. Tap Start again — if it did go through, this opens that same workout." }` (the fetch threw). The key rule, stated once and unconditionally: `clearStartKey(enrollmentId)` runs on EVERY reply that arrives (201 and every non-ok alike) — the key exists only to survive a lost reply, so it is kept only when the fetch threw. Whether a non-ok reply means "a workout is already open" is NOT decided here by reading the message: the card re-reads the door (step 11 (7)) and lets the facts say. `TodayCard.tsx` and `StartLooseWorkout.tsx` switch to it in Phase 5 (their files); this phase adds a guard that greps `src/programs/components/**` for `fetch("/api/workouts"` and allows only those two files, so no third caller appears. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/startWorkoutRequest.test.ts` — "posts the day the card named with the browser's retry key"; "a lost reply is never reported as 'nothing was started' and keeps the key"; "any reply that arrives forgets the key, ok or not"; "a non-ok reply carries the server's own words and its status"; "no screen posts /api/workouts by hand except TodayCard and StartLooseWorkout, until Phase 5 removes those".

10. **One address book for training routes.** `src/shared/trainingRoutes.ts` (new) · `PROGRAMS`, `LIVE_WORKOUT`, `TRACKING`, `TRAINING_DOOR_API`, `programsHref({ view?, program?, tab? })`, `workoutReceiptHref(id)`, `liveHref(from)`; `components/navTabs.ts` · `TABS`, `ROUTE_LABELS`. Today: `/programs` and `/programs/live` are typed by hand in `TrainingCard.tsx:118, 149, 165, 177, 192, 240`, `TodayCard.tsx`, `StartLooseWorkout.tsx`, `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx`, `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx`, `QuickActionsCard.tsx:60`, `navTabs.ts:31, 52`, `WorkoutPrograms.tsx`, `app/programs/live/page.tsx`; the spec adds `?view=`, `?program=`, `?tab=` and `/programs/workout/[id]`, which would be five more hand-typed families. Change: the pattern of `src/shared/lifeMasteryRoutes.ts` — constants and builders; `TRACKING = "/dashboard/tracking"`; `liveHref(from) = withReturn(LIVE_WORKOUT, from)` (`src/shared/returnTo.ts:42`); `workoutReceiptHref(id) = \`${PROGRAMS}/workout/${id}\`` (Phase 4 builds the page; open question 2); `navTabs.ts` imports `PROGRAMS` and `TRACKING` for its two hrefs and `ROUTE_LABELS` (one owner each, the way it already imports `LIFE_MASTERY`). Guard test modelled on `tests/unit/navigation/lifeMasteryRoutes.test.ts`: walks `src`, `app`, `components`, blanks comments, matches every quote kind against `/["'\`]\/programs/`, exempts the `app/programs/` route folder and this file, and carries an allowlist seeded with exactly the files the first run reports (the executor lists what the test reports; `TrainingCard.tsx` must not be on it, and `QuickActionsCard.tsx` leaves it with step 13) that may only shrink (Phases 5, 6, 8 empty it). *Test:* `tests/unit/navigation/trainingRoutes.test.ts` — "no training address is written out by hand outside the allowlist"; "the hand-typed /programs allowlist only shrinks"; "liveHref carries a safe return address and drops an unsafe one"; "still has a page at every address the constants claim" (`app/programs/page.tsx`, `app/programs/live/page.tsx`; the receipt page's line is added by Phase 4).

11. **The card, exactly as drawn in S1.** `src/programs/components/TrainingCard.tsx` · `TrainingCard({ from, className? })`. Today: returns `null` while loading (`:127`) and when there is nothing to say (`:137`) — the complaint; the error branch has no testid (`:112–125`); detail failures vanish (`:37` drops `error`); `CardContent p-4` on top of `Card py-4 sm:py-6` (`:158`, double padding); `size="sm"` buttons (`:148, 176, 191, 226`) and the black `variant="outline"` Start (`:227`); Resume solid orange (`:176`); rest day with no button (`:248–256`); weekday from the browser clock (`:188`); `enrollments[0]` (`:36`); `live` prop nobody passes (`:34`). Change — build the spec, element by element: (1) `<Card className={cn("gap-0 py-0", className)} data-testid="training-card">` › `div.px-4.py-4.space-y-2`, `sm:flex sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:gap-6` with the button `sm:w-auto sm:min-w-[200px]`; `border-primary/50` in `live`, `border-amber-500/40` in `stale` and `failed`. (2) `load.state === "loading"` → `<div data-testid="training-card-loading" className={cn("h-[92px] rounded-xl bg-muted/40 animate-pulse", className)}>` — a fixed-height block, never `null`. (3) `load.state === "failed"` → the card with the label row, `p.flex.items-center.gap-2.text-sm.text-amber-600.dark:text-amber-400` + `AlertTriangle size-4`, `data-testid="training-card-unavailable"`, text "Could not load your training.", THE button reads "Open training" → `router.push(PROGRAMS)`, and never a Start (it could collide with an open workout); "No program" never appears in this state. (4) Label row: `Dumbbell size-3.5` + "Training" in `text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground`; right side the chosen program's `enrollmentName` (`src/programs/data/catalog.ts:108`) + " ›" as `Link.text-xs.text-muted-foreground.truncate` → `programsHref({ program })`, hidden in `none`/`failed`. (5) Headline `p.text-lg.font-semibold.leading-snug`, context `p.text-sm.text-muted-foreground.truncate`, second context `p.text-sm.text-muted-foreground` — copy per state: `none` "No program running" / "Start a workout and add lifts as you go, or pick a program."; `today` day label / lifts joined " · " (or `endurance.blocks` + " · about N min") / "Last time Fri · all 15 sets" when `lastTime.complete`, else "Last time Fri · 12 sets"; absent when unknown; `rest` "Rest day" / "Next is Workout B on Wed" (weekday only when pinned); `done` "Trained today" / "Workout A · 45 min · 14 sets" ("Workout" when `dayLabel` is null; any null part omitted) / "Next is …" when `next` is set; `live` "Workout A in progress · 23 min" ("Workout in progress" when `dayLabel` is null) / "6 of 15 sets ticked" (count omitted when `setsAsked` is null); `stale` "Monday's Workout A is still open" / "Started Mon 18:02 · 3 of 15 sets ticked"; `finished` "StrongLifts 5×5 is finished" / "47 sessions · started 3 Feb". With 2–3 programs the second context adds "Also today: Run 3 · Sprint Triathlon ›" (→ `programsHref({ program })`) or "Also running: X, Y" (→ `programsHref({ view: "programs" })`). (6) THE button, always exactly one and ALWAYS a real `<button>` (never `Button asChild`/`Link` — the browser test counts `button` elements, and a link would make the count 0 in `live`, `stale`, `done`, `finished`): `Button variant="outline" className="w-full border-primary/50 bg-primary/10 text-primary hover:bg-primary/15 hover:text-primary"` (default size = `h-11 sm:h-9`, `button.tsx:30`): `none` "▶ Start a workout" → `startWorkoutRequest({ enrollmentId: null, dayId: null })` (testid `training-card-start`); `today` "▶ Start workout" → `startWorkoutRequest({ enrollmentId, dayId })` — the day the card named; `rest` "▶ Start Workout B early" → same with `nextDayId`; `done` "See today's workout" → `router.push(workoutReceiptHref(workoutId))` (testid `training-card-see`); `live` "Resume" → `router.push(liveHref(from))` (testid `training-card-resume`); `stale` "Finish or discard it" → `router.push(liveHref(from))`; `finished` "Pick what's next" → `router.push(programsHref({ view: "programs" }))`. `Play` only when the button starts something, `Loader2` while starting, `disabled` while starting (so a second tap is a no-op). On `ok` → `router.push(liveHref(from))`. (7) Start reply that is not ok: call `load.retry()` FIRST and let the facts decide — if the refreshed door has `live`, the card is simply in the `live` state (Resume; the workout that was already open is the offer), which is how "already in progress" is handled without reading the message; if the refreshed door has no `live`, show `p.text-sm.text-destructive role="alert"` with the server's `message`. Lost reply → the helper's message in the same `role="alert"` line; the button is re-enabled in every case. (8) Secondary link (`Link.min-h-11.inline-flex.items-center.text-sm.text-primary.hover:underline`): `none` "Pick a program ›" → `programsHref({ view: "programs" })`; `done` "Start another workout ›" → loose start (a `<button>` styled as the link — still the only `Button`-styled control is THE button; the test counts `button` elements, so this secondary control is an `<a role="button">`? No: render it as a `Link` to `programsHref({ view: "today" })`… — *Resolved:* keep the spec's wording and make it a `<Link>` to `PROGRAMS` where the loose start lives (S2 element 14), so the card keeps exactly one `<button>`); `finished` "Start a workout ›" → the same `Link`. (9) Every weekday and clock time on the card is formatted from `facts.timezone`: weekday = `WEEKDAY_SHORT[isoWeekdayInTimezone(timezone, new Date(iso))]` (`src/programs/config.ts:54`, `dateUtils.ts:102`), time = `toZonedDate(new Date(iso), timezone)` hours/minutes (`dateUtils.ts:50`) — never `toLocaleDateString(undefined, …)`. (10) Delete: `if (loading) return null`, `if (state.kind === "none" && !liveUnknown) return null`, the `live` prop, `liveUnknown`, the `enrollments[0]` logic, the rest sentence without a button, `CardContent`, the comments at `:208–225` justifying `outline`/`sm`, the `fetch` and the `useActiveEnrollments`/`useEnrollment` imports. Nothing on the card is under `text-xs` (12 px). Icons: `Dumbbell` is registered for "workout logging entry point" (`iconRoles.ts:107`) — this is that context; `Play`, `AlertTriangle`, `Loader2`, `ChevronRight` are in `UTILITY_ICONS` (`iconRoles.ts:16–44`). New icons: none. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/trainingCard.test.tsx` (RTL, `useTrainingDoor` and `next/navigation` mocked) — "renders the 92 px skeleton while loading, never nothing"; "renders the unavailable line and no Start when the door failed"; "renders exactly one <button> in every ready state, and it is never a link"; "a rest day offers to start the next session early with its dayId"; "Start posts the day the card named"; "a lost reply keeps the button usable and never says nothing was started"; "a refused start re-reads the door and shows Resume when a workout turns out to be open"; "formats the day a stale workout started in the account's timezone, not the browser's" (`timezone: "Pacific/Auckland"`, `startedAt: "2026-09-14T14:02:00Z"` → "Tue 02:02"; a UTC or European browser clock gives "Mon"); "an endurance day never prints a lift count".

12. **Mount it where the spec puts it, with no late pop-in.** `src/tracking/components/ProgressDashboard.tsx` · `ProgressDashboard`; `src/goals/components/HealthTrackingPanel.tsx` · `HealthTrackingPanel`. Today: both `lazy()` the card with `Suspense` (`ProgressDashboard.tsx:41–43, 95–97`; `HealthTrackingPanel.tsx:20–22, 72`), so even the skeleton arrives late; the card has no bottom margin against `StatTileGrid` (`ProgressDashboard.tsx:100`); neither says where it is. `HealthTrackingPanel` is reached through `GoalHierarchyView.tsx:153` → `GoalsHubContent` → Life Mastery's Track tab (`TrackTab.tsx:298`, `?step=track`) and the archived `/test/archive/goals-hub`. Change: import `TrainingCard` directly (the skeleton paints on first render; the card is small and the page already needs its data), `<TrainingCard from={TRACKING} className="mb-6" />` between `SeasonBand` and `StatTileGrid`; `<TrainingCard from={\`${LIFE_MASTERY}?step=track\`} />` in the health grid (its cell owns the spacing, so no margin; `BackLink` labels it "Your plan" via `ROUTE_LABELS[LIFE_MASTERY]`). Delete the `ProgressDashboard.tsx:84–94` comment that says the card "renders NOTHING AT ALL when there is nothing to say" and the "RENDERS NOTHING without an active enrollment" sentence at `:32–34`. *Test:* `tests/e2e/dashboard-training-card.spec.ts — "with no program and no workout the door is still there, above the fold, and starts a loose workout"` (step 15); `tests/unit/navigation/backNavigation.test.ts — "every sub-page renders BackLink, or shows the tab bar"` and `routeReachability.test.ts — "every product route is reached from a page that is itself reached"` stay green.

### Acceptance
- `tests/unit/programs/trainingCardState.test.ts` (10 titles in step 7) — the pure decision covers every S1 state; "done today" is decided in ONE place, from real instants either side of Copenhagen midnight, and the same instants under UTC give the opposite answer; the next session is never re-offered on a day you trained.
- `tests/unit/programs/chooseCardEnrollment.test.ts` (6 titles) — which program the card shows is a rule, never `enrollments[0]`, and the program trained today is the one the done state names.
- `tests/unit/programs/nextSessionAfterToday.test.ts` (3 titles) and `describeEndurance.test.ts` (2 titles) — "what is next" and "what a run is" each have one owner.
- `tests/unit/programs/useTrainingDoor.test.tsx` (5 titles) — one request through the app's shared loader, a third state for "could not find out", a wrong-shaped body is a failure.
- `tests/unit/programs/startWorkoutRequest.test.ts` (5 titles) — the day the card named is posted, the retry key is forgotten on every reply and kept only on a lost one, a lost reply is never "nothing was started", and no third screen posts `/api/workouts` by hand.
- `tests/unit/navigation/trainingRoutes.test.ts` (4 titles) — every training link is built from one file; the hand-typed allowlist only shrinks; every constant has a page.
- `tests/unit/programs/trainingCard.test.tsx` (9 titles) — skeleton never `null`, failed never "no program", exactly one `<button>` per state, a refused start becomes Resume when a workout is open, timezone formatting, no "0 lifts".
- `tests/e2e/dashboard-training-card.spec.ts` (14 titles) — every state reached through the real route on a phone viewport, Start and Resume pressed, a double tap opens one workout, the day the live screen opens asserted against the card's headline, the real 409 handled, the card's shape (one 44 px `button`, one orange control on the page) asserted in every ready state, one request failed per state with `page.route`.
- `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts — "Type exports should only be in types.ts files"` now scans `src/programs` and fails if a type is exported from `programsService.ts` again.
- Guards that must stay green, allowlists may shrink and never grow: `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` (API routes < 50 lines; DB access only in `src/db`; type exports in `types.ts` with `programs` scanned; "no NEW screen fetches its own data" with `TrainingCard.tsx` removed from the allowlist; "no NEW read that asks for more rows than the database will return" with `trainingDoorRepo.ts` at zero; icon registry — no new roles, no new icons; the new "no NEW browser-clock date on a training screen" pair), `tests/unit/navigation/routeReachability.test.ts`, `tests/unit/navigation/backNavigation.test.ts`, `tests/unit/navigation/lifeMasteryRoutes.test.ts`, `tests/unit/writeCoverage.test.ts` (this phase adds reads only; the ratchet does not move), `tests/unit/e2e-isolation.test.ts`.
- Migrations: none in this phase. Do not run `supabase db push`; `20260914120000_drop_four_orphan_tables.sql` in the tree belongs to another agent.

- Step 1's test was vacuous: `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts:192` lists seven slices and `programs` is not one, so "Type exports should only be in types.ts files" passes today with three type exports sitting in `programsService.ts` and would pass with the move reverted. Fixed: add `programs` to `slices`, move all three types (`TrainingCardState`, `LiftProgress`, `PlateLoad`), seed the allowlist with the other offenders the test reports; the test now fails on revert.
- Two owners of "is this workout today": step 2 filtered `trainedToday` in the repo with `getTodayInTimezone`, and step 7's Copenhagen test claimed the pure function decided it — the function would only ever have received pre-filtered rows, so the test proved nothing. Fixed: the repo hands over `recentlyFinished` (48 h, unfiltered); `trainingCardState` alone compares against `todayDate`; the unit test also runs the same instants under UTC so the timezone is what is proven.
- Step 8 invented a second loader while `src/shared/useLoad.ts` is the one the architecture test names as mandatory for new screens. Fixed: `useTrainingDoor` is `useLoad` plus a shape-checking parser; the "keep last-known facts" requirement was dropped because S1's failed state shows no facts (Phase 5 owns it for S2).
- Step 9 decided "already in progress" by reading the 409 body, but `app/api/workouts/route.ts:18` returns 409 for every thrown error (not found, duplicate key, in progress). Fixed: any non-ok reply makes the card re-read the door; the refreshed facts' `live` decides whether it is Resume or an error. No string matching.
- Step 9 left the client key rule conditional on what Phase 0 might have done ("if Phase 0 has already placed…") — an executor would have to guess. Fixed: the helper owns it unconditionally (`clearStartKey` on every reply, kept only on a thrown fetch); Phase 0's dependency narrowed to the server half.
- Step 11 rendered THE button as `Button asChild` + `Link` in `done`, `live`, `stale`, `finished`, while step 15's `assertDoorShape` counts `button` elements — those four states would have failed with a count of 0. Fixed: always a `<button>` with `router.push`; secondary controls are `<a>`.
- "Last time Fri · all 15 sets" needed an asked-count the workout row never stored; the draft took it from today's prescription — a second fact that disagrees after any program edit. Fixed: `complete` comes from the workout's own stored `adjustments` (no `incomplete`, no `skipped`); "all" is printed only then, otherwise the plain count.
- Step 15's finish body `{ durationMin: 30 }` is refused with 400 — `FinishWorkoutSchema` (`schemas.ts:336`) requires `intensity`. Fixed.
- Step 15's "exactly one element with background rgb(255,107,53)" fails on real data: `RecentFieldReportsCard.tsx:210` and `WeeklyReviewsCard.tsx:45` paint `bg-primary` progress bars on the same page. Fixed: count `button, a[href]` controls, which is what the spec's "one orange control" rule means.
- Step 15's wrong-day fixture relied on one `skip` moving the cursor off today's day; when the test runs on the week's second weekday the cursor lands on today and the test proves nothing. Fixed: the week is ordered so day 0 is tomorrow; deterministic on every weekday, no skip.
- Step 15's custom enrolment would be refused: the custom program has one level, `intermediate` (`customProgram.ts`), and `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx:218–226` posts `workingWeights`. Fixed in the fixture.
- Step 15's "already open" case mocked a 409 with no workout actually open, which the corrected step 9/11 design would render as a plain error. Fixed: the test opens a workout by API after the page loads (the other device) and taps Start, so the real 409 is exercised and Resume must appear.
- Step 14 named `WeekStrip.tsx:270` and `ProgramsApp.tsx:231, 326` with regexes that do not match them (`WeekStrip.tsx:82` is the real line; ProgramsApp's offenders are `toLocaleDateString()` with no arguments, `periodStartFor(…, new Date())` and `isoWeekday(new Date(l.logged_at))`).
