**Found while building it, 19 September (all 15 steps).**
- Step 9 asked for a new `src/programs/startWorkoutRequest.ts`. Phase 0 had already built one in `hooks/useLiveWorkout.ts`, AND an architecture guard already names that file as the only place allowed to post `/api/workouts` — so the guard failed on my duplicate within minutes. Phase 0's is the better of the two (it recovers a spent key once, and treats "already open" as the thing the person was reaching for), so the new file was deleted rather than the guard widened.
- `useLoad` exposed `retry` only on its `failed` state. Step 11 (7) needs to re-read from `ready` — that is how a refused Start corrects itself to Resume without reading the refusal's wording. `reload` is now on every state; three existing tests compared the whole returned object with `toEqual` and had to become `toMatchObject`.
- Step 14's first scan fired on `TrainingCard.tsx` — in its own doc comment, which explains the rule using the very string the rule forbids. Same trap as the UTC-date guard two phases earlier. Both comment kinds are blanked now, and the count was taken again afterwards.
- Step 12 turned a lazy `import()` of the card into a real one, which immediately tripped Phase 2's one-door guard: Life Mastery embeds the same card, and the lazy form had been hiding that crossing from the rule. Exported through `forLifeMastery.ts`.
- Step 1 was supposed to seed the type-export allowlist with "what the first run reports". It reported seven files, one of which was `programsService.ts` itself — the three types the plan names. Those moved; the other six are allowlisted with a one-line reason each, as the plan asked.
- Not a defect, but worth recording: four commit attempts failed on another session's untracked `zz-*` scratch tests under `tests/unit/goals`, because the pre-commit hook runs the whole suite. Messaged them rather than reaching for `--no-verify`; the hook had already caught two real regressions of mine that day.

### Open questions for Phase 3

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** Remove the 'Training →' row from Quick Actions on the Tracking page? The card at the top is now the door; the row sits about 2,800 px further down and goes to the same place. (Spec Decision 2; step 13 is marked DESTRUCTIVE and waits on this.)

*Recommendation:* Yes, remove it. The bottom tab bar and the card both reach Training, and no test or link depends on the row (grep verified).

**Open question 2.** The 'Trained today' state has a button 'See today's workout' that opens the receipt page /programs/workout/[id], which Phase 4 builds (spec Decision 7 says build it before S1, but the phase order puts Phase 4 after Phase 3). Should Phase 4's receipt-page step run before this phase, or should this phase ship first with the button opening Training until Phase 4 flips the one constant?

*Recommendation:* Run Phase 4's receipt-page step first, as the spec already recommends; the button then works the day it ships. If that is refused, the executor makes workoutReceiptHref return the Training page's History tab (programsHref({ tab: 'history' })), labels the button 'See it in History', and Phase 4 changes the one constant, one label and one test assertion.

**Open question 3.** The spec says the card waits for two requests (the enrollment list and /api/workouts/live). This phase proposes one request, /api/programs/today, computed on the server in your account's timezone — because with two or three programs the two-request design needs a further request per program, and facts arriving at two moments is exactly what caused the Start→Resume flip. The failure tests then fail that one route instead of /api/programs/enrollments. Agree?

*Recommendation:* One request. It removes the class of fault (facts arriving at different moments) rather than the instance, and Phases 5 and 8 reuse the same loader.

**Open question 4.** After a workout that was NOT part of a program (a loose workout) on a day when a program session is also due, the spec's rule makes the card say 'Trained today' and offer 'Start another workout' rather than offering the program session. Is that the behaviour you want, or should a loose workout leave the program's session on offer?

*Recommendation:* Follow the spec: the day is done, the door says so, and 'Start another workout' is one tap away. Hevy and Strong behave this way.

**Open question 5.** The spec says the card is at most 200 px tall in every state, but three states (no program, trained today, program finished) carry a second link under the button that must be 44 px tall to be tappable, and with the label row, headline, sentence, button and padding they measure about 216–236 px on a phone. Accept 240 px for those three states, or drop the second link from them?

*Recommendation:* Accept 240 px for those three states and keep 200 px for the rest; the link is the only way from the card to 'Pick a program' and to a second workout, and shrinking it below 44 px breaks the touch-target rule.


### Blockers for Phase 3

No blockers.


## Phase 4 — One way to log a workout
**What you can do after:** There is exactly one way to record a workout — the set-by-set live screen — whether you are in the gym right now or writing up Tuesday's session on Thursday. "Log a past workout" on the History tab opens that same screen at the time you choose; the finish sheet lets you set both Started and Ended in your account's time zone; a run or mobility session finishes with its minutes and distance and is counted as a run, not as a gym session; an empty workout with no program asks what kind of session it was before it saves. The two fill-in-afterwards forms ("I did all of this — save it" and "Log a past workout" with its kg-only boxes and 90-day "New PR"), the "Anything else" tab, and their two separate save paths are gone, so a workout can no longer be recorded as pounds-called-kilos, saved twice on a slow connection, or invented as "45 minutes at effort 3". What you lose: the one-tap "I did all of this — save it"; what replaces it: a "Did the fixed sets as shown" button on each lift when you are logging a past session.
**Why it is in this order:** Phase 0 has already made starting and finishing safe (one start helper, one personal-best definition, a run stored as a run) and Phase 3 has built the Tracking door on the six-hour rule; this phase removes the two other writers so that every screen Phases 5–8 build (Today, live, History, Life Mastery) sits on one write path with one set of rules.
**Findings this phase closes:** after-the-fact-logging-01, after-the-fact-logging-02, after-the-fact-logging-03, after-the-fact-logging-04, after-the-fact-logging-07, after-the-fact-logging-08, after-the-fact-logging-09, after-the-fact-logging-10, after-the-fact-logging-11, programs-today-09, critic-live-run-is-an-empty-weights-session

**Findings in this area that another phase already lists (not claimed here, so no id is counted twice):** data-integrity-04 (Phase 0 step 10 — a live run is stored as a run; this phase only adds the loose-workout kind and the distance), after-the-fact-logging-05 and after-the-fact-logging-06 (Phase 0 steps 6 and 7 fix them inside WorkoutLogger; deleting WorkoutLogger here makes those two edits moot, and the executor removes them with the file), after-the-fact-logging-12 (Phase 10 lists it; the component tests it asks for have nothing left to test once the forms are gone, and its browser-test half — the mobile-training case that pins the 45-minute lie — is rewritten in step 12 because this phase breaks it). history-progress-01 and critic-three-definitions-of-a-personal-best are Phase 0's (one all-time baseline); this phase only feeds that baseline the right "before" instant (step 5).

**How the deletion closes the after-the-fact findings** (checked against the code, not asserted): 01 pounds-as-kilos — the live path converts with the workout's own unit (`unitFor`, `src/db/workoutRepo.ts:477`). 02 double save / half save — the live path is one row from Start (`client_key`, `uq_workout_logs_client_key`) and one transaction at Finish (`finish_program_workout`). 03 confirmation vanishes — the live summary outlives the workout (`LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx:141-165`). 04 "45 minutes at effort 3" — the live path derives minutes from Started/Ended on the server (step 5) and in past mode refuses to save until Ended is typed (step 6); the three `BRIDGE_*` constants go with their last reader. 07 error details thrown away — the form is gone; the finish route already returns the first problem in plain words (`app/api/workouts/[id]/finish/route.ts:21`). 08 dressed as the live screen / 09 three forms on two screens / programs-today-09 duplicated notices — the fold and both forms are deleted (step 9). 10 set numbers across exercises — the live path numbers per lift (`completeSet`). 11 AMRAP stored as working — the live path writes `kind: amrap` (`LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx:389`).

### Steps
1. **The finish transaction learns the start time, the session kind and the distance, and the database refuses a nonsense distance for everyone.** `supabase/migrations/20260917120000_finish_workout_times_and_kind.sql` · `finish_program_workout`; `tests/integration/schema.sql`. Today: the function (`supabase/migrations/20260907110000_finish_workout.sql:27-92`) takes `p_workout_id, p_ended_at, p_duration_min, p_intensity, p_rpe, p_notes, p_exercise_state, p_cursor, p_replay_events, p_expected_session_count` and updates only `ended_at, duration_min, intensity, rpe, notes`; `started_at` cannot be changed after Start; `distance_km NUMERIC(6,2)` has no CHECK, so anything with the service key can store −5 km. Change: `DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS finish_program_workout(UUID, TIMESTAMPTZ, INTEGER, SMALLINT, SMALLINT, TEXT, JSONB, JSONB, JSONB, INTEGER)` (the parameter list changes, so `CREATE OR REPLACE` cannot do it) and create it again with three more parameters at the end — `p_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ` (when not null: `SET started_at = p_started_at, logged_at = p_started_at`, because `workout_logs_logged_is_start` (`20260907100000:96-99`) requires the two to be equal, and the existing `workout_logs_ended_after_start` then refuses an end before the new start), `p_session_type TEXT` (when not null: `SET session_type = p_session_type`; `workout_logs_session_type_check` refuses anything but the five), `p_distance_km NUMERIC` (`SET distance_km = p_distance_km`; NULL clears). Same `SECURITY INVOKER`, same lock-and-recheck (`WHERE started_at IS NOT NULL AND ended_at IS NULL FOR UPDATE`), same 55000 on a second finish, REVOKE/GRANT repeated for the new signature; the function comment names the three additions. Same file: `ALTER TABLE workout_logs ADD CONSTRAINT workout_logs_distance_range CHECK (distance_km IS NULL OR (distance_km >= 0 AND distance_km <= 1000))` inside the `DO $$ … EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object` wrapper the sibling constraints use (1000 is `MAX_DISTANCE_KM`, `src/shared/weight.ts:61`). `tests/integration/schema.sql`: the file already carries every `workout_logs` CHECK and both partial unique indexes (lines 712-732) and the `authenticated` role (line 594) — add only the new function definition, verbatim, after the `workout_logs` trigger (line 748), and the new distance CHECK inside the `CREATE TABLE workout_logs` block. Phase 10 step 11 ("copy `finish_program_workout` from `20260907110000`") must copy it from THIS migration instead — leave a one-line comment above the function in `schema.sql` saying so. Executor: `supabase migration list --linked` first; every migration up to and including `20260914120000` shows applied (the untracked `20260914120000_drop_four_orphan_tables.sql` belongs to another agent and is already on the database), so this file must be the only pending row — if anything else is pending, stop and say so instead of pushing. Then `supabase db push --linked`. *Test:* `tests/integration/db/finishWorkoutTimes.integration.test.ts` (raw SQL through `getClient()` like `workoutSchema.integration.test.ts`) — "finishing with a started_at moves started_at and logged_at together", "an end before the new start is refused by the constraint, not by the app", "a session kind and a distance are written in the same call as the end time", "a distance of −1 or 1001 is refused by workout_logs_distance_range whoever writes it", "a second finish of the same workout raises 55000".

2. **One owner for "is this workout stale/past" and one owner for reading and writing a typed wall-clock time.** `src/programs/programsService.ts` · new export `isStaleWorkout(startedAt: string, now = new Date()): boolean` beside `STALE_WORKOUT_HOURS` (line 1928); `src/shared/dateUtils.ts` · new exports `instantToWallClock(iso: string, timezone: string): string` and `wallClockToInstant(dateTimeLocal: string, timezone: string): string`; `src/programs/components/TodayCard.tsx` · `TodayCard` (`STALE_HOURS`, line 42, used at line 61). Today: the six-hour rule exists twice — `STALE_WORKOUT_HOURS` in `trainingCardState` (programsService.ts:1938) and a private `STALE_HOURS = 6` in TodayCard.tsx:42; the finish sheet converts a typed time with the browser's clock (`toLocalInput`, `FinishSheet.tsx:53-61`, and `new Date(endedAt).toISOString()`, line 300), while every other "which day did you train" in the app is the profile timezone (`loggedAtForEntry`, `healthService.ts:677`; `workoutDay`, `workoutRepo.ts:419`). The session-kind mapping is NOT touched here: Phase 0 step 10 already moved it to `programsService.sessionTypeFor(program)` and `startWorkout` already writes it — reuse that name, do not mint `sessionTypeForProgram`. Change: `isStaleWorkout` holds the comparison; `trainingCardState` (as Phase 3 left it) replaces its one `minutes > STALE_WORKOUT_HOURS * 60` comparison with a call to it; TodayCard deletes its constant and imports the helper. `instantToWallClock` renders an instant as the `datetime-local` string (`YYYY-MM-DDTHH:mm`) in the given zone via `toZonedDate` (dateUtils.ts:50, whose returned Date is a carrier for wall-clock fields); `wallClockToInstant` splits the string at `T` and returns `localTimeInstant(date, time, timezone)` (dateUtils.ts:316). The rule they own: **a time a person types in training is read in the account's time zone, and a stored time is shown in it — never the device's.** The guard that makes it stick (`tests/unit/architecture.test.ts`, new test, allowlist may only shrink): under `src/programs/components/**` every `toLocaleTimeString(`, `toLocaleDateString(` and `toLocaleString(` call on a Date must pass `timeZone:`, and `getHours()`/`getFullYear()`/`toISOString()` on a Date built from a `datetime-local` value are forbidden; the frozen allowlist of files still offending today is `HistoryTab.tsx, ProgressTab.tsx, ProgressionView.tsx, LiftHistory.tsx, PastPrograms.tsx, RunningPrograms.tsx, ProgramsApp.tsx, TodayCard.tsx, TrainingCard.tsx` (Phases 5, 7 and 8 shrink it as they rebuild those screens); `FinishSheet.tsx`, `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` and the new dialog are not on it. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/staleWorkout.test.ts` — "trainingCardState and TodayCard use the same six-hour rule: 5 h 59 is live, 6 h 01 is stale, and TodayCard.tsx no longer declares a STALE constant" (renders TodayCard with a `live` started 6 h 01 ago and asserts the stale button text; greps the file for `STALE_HOURS`); `tests/unit/shared/wallClock.test.ts` — "18:00 typed by a Europe/Copenhagen account in September is 16:00Z, and 16:00Z shows as 18:00 again", "Monday 23:45 Copenhagen is still Monday for that account even though it is Tuesday in Auckland", "the DST-change morning round-trips"; `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` — "training screens format times in the account's zone, and the device-clock allowlist only shrinks".

3. **The start and finish requests can carry a start time, a session kind and a distance; the server owns the minutes.** `src/programs/schemas.ts` · `StartWorkoutSchema` (line 278), `FinishWorkoutSchema` (line 323); `src/health/schemas.ts` · `CreateWorkoutSchema` (line 74). Today (after Phase 0): `StartWorkoutSchema` has `enrollmentId, dayId, clientKey`; `FinishWorkoutSchema` has `endedAt, durationMin, intensity, rpe, notes` (Phase 0 step 10 removed the dead `sessionType`); `durationMin` is a second copy of a fact the two instants already state, and `finishWorkout` silently clamps a longer span to 599 (`workoutRepo.ts:365`). Change: `StartWorkoutSchema` gains `startedAt: z.string().datetime().optional()`. `FinishWorkoutSchema` gains `startedAt: z.string().datetime().optional()`, `sessionType: z.enum(["weights","cardio","mobility","yoga","running"]).optional()` (back, for loose workouts only — the server refuses it on a program workout, step 5), `distanceKm: z.number().min(0).max(MAX_DISTANCE_KM).nullable().optional()`, and **loses `durationMin`** — minutes are derived on the server from the two instants (step 5), so the sheet stops sending them; a `.refine` requires `endedAt` whenever `startedAt` is present ("Say when it ended as well as when it started."), so a moved start can never be paired with an end guessed from a set's tick time. Delete `LogSessionSchema` (schemas.ts:224-267, with the `entryWhenFields`/`hasDateIfTime`/`NEEDS_DATE_FOR_TIME` import at line 18) and `CreateWorkoutSchema` (health/schemas.ts:74-80) with their routes (step 9); `entryWhenFields` stays — the sleep, weight and nutrition routes use it. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/workoutSchemas.test.ts` — "a start may name when it started, as an instant", "a finish may move the start only together with an end, may name the kind and the distance, and refuses a distance over the ceiling", "a finish no longer accepts durationMin" (`safeParse` of a body with `durationMin` is fine only because the schema is non-strict — assert the key is absent from `parsed.data`), "LogSessionSchema and CreateWorkoutSchema no longer exist" (dynamic import → `undefined`). `tests/unit/health/workoutDate.test.ts`: the two `CreateWorkoutSchema` cases (lines 148-177) and the `"workout"` row of the four-route table (line 204) are deleted; the table's docstring is edited to say three routes. `tests/unit/programs/logSessionSchema.test.ts` is deleted.

4. **Starting a past workout records when it really started, and refuses a time that cannot be right.** `src/db/workoutRepo.ts` · `startWorkout` (line 120) and Phase 0's `StartRefused` (its `code` union gains `"in_future"` and `"before_program"`, both status 400). Today (after Phase 0): `startedAt = new Date().toISOString()` (line 156) with no way to say otherwise; `session_type` already comes from `sessionTypeFor(program)` for a program workout and is `"weights"` for a loose one (Phase 0 step 10). Change: `startedAt = input.startedAt ?? now`; a `startedAt` more than one minute after the server's `now` → `StartRefused("in_future", 400)` with "That is in the future — pick a time you have already trained." (an instant-to-instant comparison, so the server clock is the right clock here); for a program workout a `startedAt` before the enrollment's `started_at` → `StartRefused("before_program", 400)` with "That is before you started this program."; both `logged_at` and `started_at` are written from it (`workout_logs_logged_is_start`), which is what files the workout under the day it happened in the account's time zone everywhere the calendar is read. `dayId` handling is Phase 0 step 1's and is not touched. The architecture guard Phase 0 added ("only sessionTypeFor decides what a workout counts as", grep `session_type:` under `src/db`) must use a word boundary — `\bsession_type:` — so the RPC argument `p_session_type:` added in step 5 does not trip it; keep the test failing on a new bare literal. *Test:* `tests/unit/db/workoutRepoStart.test.ts` (Phase 0's file, same table-keyed fake client) — "a past start is inserted with started_at and logged_at both equal to it", "a start in the future is refused as in_future and nothing is inserted", "a program session dated before the program began is refused as before_program".

5. **Finishing writes the start, the kind and the distance in the one transaction; the minutes are derived, never clamped; a backdated program session is judged and replayed against what came before it.** `src/db/workoutRepo.ts` · `finishWorkout` (line 326) and Phase 0's `summaryFor`; `src/programs/types.ts` · `WorkoutSummary` (line 749). Today (after Phase 0): `startedAt` cannot be changed; `durationMin = Math.min(599, Math.max(1, input.durationMin ?? derived))` (line 365) — a 700-minute span is silently stored as 599; the personal-best baseline (`personalBestBaseline(userId, before)`) is given the workout's original `logged_at`. Change: (a) the RPC call passes `p_started_at: input.startedAt ?? null`, `p_session_type`, `p_distance_km: input.distanceKm ?? null`; `started` for every check is `input.startedAt ?? live.startedAt`; `durationMin` is derived from `ended − started` (the sub-minute tolerance at lines 350-363 stays) and a span over 599 minutes throws "A workout cannot be longer than ten hours — check when it really ended." — never clamped. (b) Kind: for a workout attached to a program a caller-supplied `sessionType` throws "The program decides what kind of session this is." (one owner, `sessionTypeFor`); for a loose workout `sessionType` is required when the workout has no ticked sets ("Say what kind of session it was.") and `"weights"` is passed when it has ticked sets and none was named — the RPC gets `p_session_type` only for loose workouts. (c) Records: `personalBestBaseline(userId, { workoutId, loggedAt: started })` — the edited start, so a session dated last Tuesday ignores Thursday's sets; `workoutDay` (line 419) is computed from the same instant. (d) Backdated program session — **gated on open question 5, written for the recommended answer (b)**: when `live.enrollmentId` is set and `started` is earlier than the newest stored session's `logged_at` (`getSessionLogs`, ordered newest first), then before the RPC: if `enr.initialExerciseState` is null, throw "This program was started before starting weights were kept, so a session cannot be dated before your last one. Date it after your last session, or end and restart the program." (nothing is written); otherwise build the timeline of stored sessions plus this one (`entriesFromSets(live.sets…)`, `logged_at: started`, its `dayId/cycle/week`) and run `replayEnrollment(program, { ...enr, exerciseState: seed }, logs, enr.replayEvents)` (`programsService.ts:1815`, which sorts by `logged_at`) to get `exerciseState`/`cursor` for the RPC; `changes` for the summary come from `applyLog` of this session against the state replayed up to just before it. If the answer is (a), skip (d): the existing `applyLog` on the current state stands. (e) `WorkoutSummary` gains `sessionType` and `distanceKm | null`; `summaryFor` fills both from the row so a reload shows the same receipt. *Test:* `tests/unit/db/workoutRepoFinish.test.ts` (Phase 0's file) — "the RPC receives started_at, session_type and distance in one call and no second update follows", "minutes are derived from the two instants; a 700-minute span is refused, not clamped", "a program workout that names a session kind is refused before the RPC is called", "a loose workout with no sets and no kind is refused; with a ticked set it is saved as weights", "records for a workout dated last Tuesday are judged against sets logged before last Tuesday (the baseline is asked for that instant)", "a backdated session on a program with a stored seed is replayed into date order and the RPC receives the replayed cursor", "a backdated session on a program without a stored seed is refused before anything is written".

6. **The finish sheet asks when it started and ended, how far you went, and — for a workout with no program — what it was.** `src/programs/components/live/FinishSheet.tsx` · `FinishSheet` (props lines 25-44; `toLocalInput` 53-61; time row 199-236; `longGap` note 245-250; save 291-309). Today: only Ended can be edited, in the browser's clock; no distance; a run ends as "weights" with "No sets were recorded" in History; `onFinish` sends `durationMin` computed on the client. Change: new props `timezone: string`, `past: boolean`, `endurance: boolean`, `loose: boolean` (the unit is the existing `workout.unit` — the workout's own, never the account's, per `app/programs/live/page.tsx:27-36`). "Change" reveals **Started** and **Ended** (`Input type="datetime-local" className="h-11"`, each with a `Label`), rendered with `instantToWallClock(…, timezone)` and read back with `wallClockToInstant`; `toLocalInput` is deleted; the "→ · N min" line formats both instants with `timeZone: timezone`. In past mode both inputs are open on mount, Ended starts **empty** (nothing prescribed, nothing invented), the `longGap` note is hidden (the open row is the message), and Save is disabled with the text "Say when it ended" until Ended is filled; the minutes line and the 599-minute guard use the two typed times. When `endurance` (the prescription has `enduranceSets`) or the chosen kind is running/cardio, a "How far" box appears: optional, `Input type="number" inputMode="decimal"`, labelled "km" for a kg workout and "miles" for a lb workout (`distanceUnitFor(unit)` and `toKm`-style `milesToKm` in programsService — new exports, one place), converted to km before sending, ceiling `MAX_DISTANCE_KM`. When `loose`, a "What was this?" row of five chips (weights / cardio / mobility / yoga / running, `Button variant="outline" size="sm" aria-pressed`, `min-h-11`) — "weights" preselected only when at least one set was ticked, otherwise nothing is preselected and Save is disabled with "Say what kind of session it was"; a program workout never shows the row. `onFinish` sends `startedAt` (only when it was changed), `endedAt`, `intensity`, `notes`, `distanceKm`, `sessionType` (loose only) — no `durationMin`. The summary prints a line "Run · 5 km · 31 min" (kind, distance, minutes; a missing number omitted) when the saved kind is not weights, until Phase 6 replaces the summary with `WorkoutReceipt`. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/finishSheet.test.tsx` — "in past mode Started and Ended are open, Ended is empty and Save says why it is disabled", "typing Ended 18:45 after Started 18:00 reads 45 min and sends both instants in the account's time zone, not the device's" (run with `TZ=Pacific/Auckland` in the test via `vi.stubEnv`/`process.env.TZ` and a Copenhagen `timezone` prop), "no durationMin is sent", "an endurance day shows a distance box and sends distanceKm", "a pound workout is asked in miles and sends kilometres", "a loose workout with no sets asks what it was and refuses to save until told; with a set it preselects weights", "a program workout never shows the kind row", "a ten-hour span disables Save with the too-long text".

7. **The live screen knows when it is describing the past.** `src/programs/components/live/LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` · `LiveWorkoutScreen`, `Elapsed` (line 496); `app/programs/live/page.tsx` · `LiveWorkoutPage`. Today: the header counts "N min" from `startedAt` (line 517) — 1,440 for a workout dated yesterday; every tick starts the rest bar (line 397); the page never passes a time zone (`prescriptionForDay` returns one, `workoutRepo.ts:644`, but only in the program branch and it is unused). Change: `page.tsx` reads `getUserTimezone(auth.userId)` once for every workout and passes `timezone`; the screen derives `past = isStaleWorkout(workout.startedAt)` (the same six-hour rule as the door's "still open" state — a stale workout from Monday and a backdated workout are the same situation and get the same screen). In past mode: `Elapsed` shows "since Tue 16 Sep, 18:00" formatted with `timeZone: timezone` instead of the minute count; the tick handler does not call `setRestFrom`; each lift card gains one button "Did the N fixed sets as shown" (`min-h-11`, `data-testid="tick-all-{id}"`) that ticks, in order through `live.tick`, every untouched prescribed row that has one definite prescription — no `repRangeMax`, not `amrap`, not a bodyweight row with reps 0 — because a range's `reps` is the bottom of the range (`types.ts:543`) and an AMRAP's is a minimum, and recording either "as shown" would invent a number; range and AMRAP rows are left for the person, and the button is absent when no row qualifies. This is the replacement for the deleted "I did all of this — save it", and it exists only in past mode. The finish sheet receives `timezone`, `past`, `endurance = Boolean(prescription?.enduranceSets?.length)`, `loose = !workout.enrollmentId`. Phase 6 owns everything else on this screen (menus, rows, receipt, `?from`) and keeps these four props. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/liveScreenPastMode.test.tsx` — "a workout started yesterday shows 'since' with the account's local time, not a minute count", "ticking a set in past mode does not start the rest bar", "'Did the 5 fixed sets as shown' ticks five sets and the finish sheet then lists no unfinished lift", "a 5/3/1 day ticks the fixed sets and leaves the AMRAP row untouched, and the label counts only the fixed ones", "a workout started ten minutes ago shows the minute count and no tick-all button".

8. **History gets "Log a past workout", which opens the live screen at the time you choose.** New `src/programs/components/LogPastWorkoutDialog.tsx` · `LogPastWorkoutDialog`; `src/programs/components/HistoryTab.tsx` · `HistoryTab` (header, beside the lift filter at line 264); `src/programs/components/TrainingScreen.tsx` and `app/programs/page.tsx` pass `enrollments` (`initialActive`), `live` and `timezone` (`getUserTimezone`, added to the page's `Promise.all`) down to `HistoryTab`; `src/programs/hooks/useLiveWorkout.ts` · Phase 0's `startWorkoutRequest` gains `startedAt?: string`. Today: there is no way to open the live screen for a past time; the only past entry points are the two forms this phase deletes; Phase 0's architecture test allows only `startWorkoutRequest` to POST `/api/workouts`, so the dialog must go through it (it inherits the key rule: the key is kept on a lost reply and cleared on any answer, which is what makes a retry open the same workout). Change (design spec S2 element 17, built as written): `Button variant="outline" size="sm" data-testid="log-past-workout"` "Log a past workout" opens a `Dialog` (`components/ui/dialog.tsx`): `Label` "When" + `Input type="datetime-local" className="h-11"` whose `max` is now in the account's zone (`instantToWallClock(new Date().toISOString(), timezone)`); `Label` "Which session" + chips (`Button variant="outline" size="sm" aria-pressed`, `min-h-11`): for each running program whose schedule has days, one chip per day of `scheduleDays(effectiveProgram(getProgram(e.program_id), e.customSchedule))` (`src/programs/customize.ts:123`, `src/programs/data/catalog.ts:51`), labelled "Workout A · StrongLifts 5×5" when more than one program runs; for an endurance program (`schedule.kind === "endurance_weeks"` — `scheduleDays` throws on it) one chip "Next run · Couch to 5K" that sends no `dayId` (the server picks the next session); a program `getProgram` no longer knows is listed as a muted line with no chip; plus "Empty workout". `Button` "Open it" calls `startWorkoutRequest({ enrollmentId, dayId, startedAt: wallClockToInstant(when, timezone) })`, then `router.push("/programs/live")` (Phase 6 adds `?from`). States: while a workout is open the dialog shows "Finish the workout you have open first" with a link to `/programs/live` and no Open button; a `refused` outcome shows its message under the button and re-enables it; `unreachable` shows the helper's own "Could not reach the server…" sentence; the button is disabled until both When and a session are chosen. A past program session advances the program per step 5 (d). *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/logPastWorkoutDialog.test.tsx` — "calls startWorkoutRequest with the chosen day's dayId and a startedAt resolved in the account's time zone", "lists every day of every running program, one 'Next run' chip for an endurance program, and an empty workout", "refuses to open while a workout is open and links to it", "a refused start is shown in the dialog and the button re-enables", "Open is disabled until both a time and a session are chosen".
