"primary" : "row"}` — `primary` is spec S2 element 16's bar (keeps `data-testid="start-loose-workout"`, the empty-state Card's text stays above it), `row` is the "Start an empty workout instead ›" row of element 14 (`min-h-11`, hidden while a workout is open); Phase 5 owns the look of both, this phase owns the count. `tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts:316-318` stops clicking "Anything else" and clicks `start-loose-workout` on Today. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/oneWayToLog.test.ts` (same file as step 9) — "`<StartLooseWorkout` appears exactly once in src/ and only in ProgramsApp.tsx", "TrainingScreen offers no tab called Anything else", "a running program shows the row, no program shows the bar, and neither shows while a workout is open"; plus the existing e2e "starts with no program, takes a lift added on the day, and keeps it" now passing from the Today tab.

11. **History says what a run was.** `src/health/healthService.ts` · new export `describeSessionRow(log: { session_type: string; distance_km: number | null; duration_min: number | null })`; `src/programs/components/HistoryTab.tsx` · summary line (388) and the set-less detail (479, "No sets were recorded for this one."). Today: a finished run prints `running` (the raw column value) in the row and "No sets were recorded for this one." when opened. Change: `describeSessionRow` returns "Run · 5 km · 31 min" / "Mobility · 20 min" / "Cardio · 45 min" / "Weights · 20 min" (kind capitalised, "running" → "Run"; distance only when present; minutes only when present — a missing number is omitted, never printed as 0); HistoryTab uses it in both places for a row whose `session_type` is not `weights` or whose sets are empty. Phase 7 restyles History and keeps the helper. *Test:* `tests/unit/health/describeSessionRow.test.ts` — "a running row with distance and minutes", "a mobility row with minutes and no distance prints no 0 km", "a weights row with no sets still says Weights", "a null duration prints no minutes".

12. **The browser proves it, on the one shared account, in the training project.** New `tests/e2e/programs-past-workout.spec.ts` (added to the `training` project's `testMatch` in `playwright.config.ts:140-148`, replacing `/health-past-workout\.spec\.ts/` at line 145; `tests/e2e/health-past-workout.spec.ts` is deleted — it tested the deleted POST); new helper `tests/e2e/helpers/seedWorkout.ts` (`seedFinishedWorkout(page, { startedAt, endedAt, sets })` — in `page.evaluate`: POST `/api/workouts` with `startedAt` and a fresh key, POST each set to `/api/workouts/[id]/sets` (`kind` carries `warmup`/`working`), POST `/api/workouts/[id]/finish` with `endedAt` and `intensity`) used by `tests/e2e/programs-history-progress.spec.ts` in place of its `POST /api/health/workout` seeding (lines 28 and 106 — the `entry_date` days become `startedAt` instants at 10:00 in the account's zone); `tests/e2e/mobile/mobile-training.spec.ts`: "both ways of logging are reachable without scrolling" (88-96) becomes "the loose start and the past-workout entry are reachable" (Today shows `start-loose-workout`; History shows `log-past-workout`), and "a session records what you actually did, not what it asked for" (98-129) is rewritten through the dialog: yesterday 18:00 → Workout A → "Did the 5 fixed sets as shown" on Squat, then untick one → Finish → the sheet says "Squat 4 of 5" → Ended 18:40 → Save → History shows that row (found by `history-row-<id>`, the id from `/api/health/workout?days=3&include=sets`) with "40 min". The account's time zone is read once from `GET /api/settings/time-preferences` (`timezone`), and every expected instant is computed from it with `localTimeInstant`; the History row's printed date is NOT asserted, because HistoryTab still prints it in the browser's zone (HistoryTab.tsx:117, 370 — Phase 7's whose-clock item). Phase 10 owns tests-and-ci-08's week-strip half. *Test:* `tests/e2e/programs-past-workout.spec.ts` — "a past StrongLifts session logged for yesterday 18:00 lands in History, the stored row's started_at and logged_at both equal 18:00 yesterday in the account's zone (read back through `/api/health/workout?days=3&include=sets`, which carries `started_at`; `/api/workouts/[id]` returns sets only), and the program's session count moved by one (`/api/programs/enrollments/[id]` → `enrollment.cursor.sessionCount`)", "a couch-to-5k session finished with 5 km is stored as running with distance 5 and 31 minutes, History reads 'Run · 5 km · 31 min', and after `PUT /api/tracking/dashboard` puts `running_sessions_weekly` beside `gym_sessions_weekly` (the pattern of `tracking-tiles.spec.ts:17`) the Tracking page's `stat-tile-running_sessions_weekly` reads 1 while `stat-tile-gym_sessions_weekly` (or its legacy testid) did not move", "opening the dialog while a workout is open offers only 'Finish the workout you have open first'".

### Acceptance
- `tests/integration/db/finishWorkoutTimes.integration.test.ts` passes — the database, not the app, guarantees start ≤ end, a sane distance and one finish per workout, and the start/kind/distance land in the same transaction as the end.
- `tests/unit/programs/staleWorkout.test.ts`, `tests/unit/shared/wallClock.test.ts` and the new architecture test pass — one six-hour rule (TodayCard's private constant gone) and every typed time in training read and shown in the account's zone; reverting step 2 fails the grep case.
- `tests/unit/programs/workoutSchemas.test.ts` passes and fails if `LogSessionSchema`, `CreateWorkoutSchema` or `durationMin` come back.
- `tests/unit/db/workoutRepoStart.test.ts` and `workoutRepoFinish.test.ts` pass — a past start writes `logged_at = started_at`, a future or pre-program start is refused by name, minutes are derived and never clamped, a program workout cannot be renamed a run, a loose workout must say what it was, records and the replayed program respect the backdated instant.
- `tests/unit/programs/finishSheet.test.tsx`, `liveScreenPastMode.test.tsx`, `logPastWorkoutDialog.test.tsx` pass — past mode refuses to invent an end time or a rep count, the rest bar stays off, "Did the N fixed sets as shown" replaces the one-tap save, the dialog goes through Phase 0's one start helper with the right day and instant and refuses while a workout is open.
- `tests/unit/programs/oneWayToLog.test.ts` passes — no second writer, no fourth tab, one `StartLooseWorkout` mount; deleting any listed file or adding a POST back fails it.
- `tests/unit/health/describeSessionRow.test.ts` passes — a set-less run reads as a run with its distance and minutes, never as 0.
- `tests/e2e/programs-past-workout.spec.ts`, the rewritten `mobile-training.spec.ts` cases and `programs-history-progress.spec.ts` (seeded through the live path) pass in the `training` project on one worker; `health-past-workout.spec.ts` is gone from disk and from `playwright.config.ts`.
- Guards stay green with allowlists that only shrink: `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` (two `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` lines removed, nothing added; Phase 0's `session_type` guard still passes with the `\b`; the new device-clock allowlist is frozen at the nine files named in step 2; routes still under 50 lines; no direct Supabase import outside `src/db`), `tests/unit/navigation/routeReachability.test.ts` (no new page; the deleted route was an API), `tests/unit/navigation/backNavigation.test.ts` (no new screen), `tests/unit/writeCoverage.test.ts` (two baseline entries removed, `MAX_UNASSERTED` 136 → 135, never raised), `tests/unit/navigation/testRoutesSealed.test.ts` untouched. `npm test` after every step, `npm run test:integration` after step 1, `npm run test:e2e -- --project=setup --project=training` before calling the phase done, and a read of `.playwright-mcp/` screenshots of the dialog, the past-mode live screen and the finish sheet at 390×844.

### Attack log
- Phase 0 (which runs first) already lists data-integrity-04, after-the-fact-logging-05 and -06, and Phase 10 lists after-the-fact-logging-12; the draft claimed all four. Dropped from coveredIds with the reason stated in the phase header, so no id is counted twice.
- Phase 0 step 10 already moved the session-kind mapping to `programsService.sessionTypeFor`, wrote it in `startWorkout` and deleted the follow-up UPDATE and the dead `sessionType` field; the draft re-did all of it under a second name (`sessionTypeForProgram`). Steps 2, 4 and 5 now build on Phase 0's names and only add what is new (loose-workout kind, distance, start time).
- Phase 0 step 6 deletes `priorSetsFor` and `PR_HISTORY_WORKOUTS`; the draft's step 5 edited a function that will not exist. Rewritten to pass the edited start instant to Phase 0's `personalBestBaseline(userId, before)`.
- Phase 0 step 3 makes `startWorkoutRequest` the only place allowed to POST `/api/workouts` (architecture test); the draft's dialog POSTed directly and invented a second client-key scheme. Step 8 now goes through the helper (which gains `startedAt`), and the retry rule has one owner.
- Phase 0 step 2 introduces `StartRefused` codes; the draft's future-start refusal was a bare `Error`, which Phase 0's route turns into a 500 "Could not start the workout." — the person would never see the sentence. Step 4 adds `"in_future"` and `"before_program"` codes at 400. Also added the missing lower bound: a program session dated before the program began.
- Step 1 claimed `tests/integration/schema.sql` lacks the `workout_logs` CHECKs and partial unique indexes; it has all of them (lines 712-732), only `finish_program_workout` is missing. Copying them again would duplicate constraints. Fixed; and Phase 10 step 11 is told to copy the function from this phase's migration, not the 20260907110000 one it names.
- What can be written that shouldn't: `distance_km` had no database CHECK (only the deleted zod schema bounded it). Step 1 adds `workout_logs_distance_range` and an integration test for it.
- Two facts stored apart: `duration_min` was sent by the client and also derivable from the two instants, and the server silently clamped anything over 599 (`workoutRepo.ts:365`). Step 3 removes `durationMin` from the request; step 5 derives it and refuses instead of clamping.
- Whose clock: `finishWorkout` defaults the end to the last set's `completed_at`, which for a past-mode tick is the moment you typed it — later than the session. Step 3's `.refine` requires `endedAt` whenever `startedAt` moves, so a backdated workout can never borrow a tick time as its end.
- "Did all N as shown" would have invented numbers: a rep-range row's `reps` is the bottom of the range (`types.ts:543`) and an AMRAP row's is a minimum. Step 7 ticks only rows with one definite prescription, counts only those in the label, and a test pins the 5/3/1 case.
- Step 2's grep test ("no file under src/programs converts a datetime-local string with the device clock") was not greppable. Replaced with a checkable rule (every `toLocale*String` must pass `timeZone:`; no wall-clock getters on datetime-local values) and a frozen, shrink-only allowlist of the nine files that offend today.
- Step 7 said "Phase 3 keeps the call" inside `trainingCardState`; Phase 3 runs before this phase. Corrected: this phase substitutes the one comparison in whatever Phase 3 left.
- Step 10 mounted `StartLooseWorkout` twice while its own test demanded exactly one occurrence. Now one JSX mount with a `variant` chosen from the enrollment count, and never in the `browse`/`detail` views (ProgramsApp.tsx:26-29).
- Step 8's day chips called `scheduleDays` on every program; it throws for endurance programs (`customize.ts:122-126`), so a couch-to-5k enrollment would have crashed the dialog. Endurance programs get a "Next run" chip with no `dayId`.
- Step 12's browser test read `started_at` "through `/api/workouts/[id]`", which returns sets only (`healthRepo.ts:321`); switched to `/api/health/workout?days=3&include=sets`. It also assumed a running tile on the Tracking page — it is not there by default (`metricsRepo.ts:65`) and its testid is `stat-tile-running_sessions_weekly`; the spec now adds it through `PUT /api/tracking/dashboard` first.
- Whose clock in the browser test: HistoryTab prints the row date with the browser's clock (HistoryTab.tsx:117, 370), so "row dated yesterday" would flake whenever the browser's zone differs from the account's. The spec asserts the day through the API instant and finds the History row by id; the printed date stays Phase 7's whose-clock item.
- Open question 5 had no step behind it — the executor would have had to design the backdated replay. Step 5 (d) now writes the recommended answer as a concrete, single-transaction rule with the refusal for programs without a stored seed, gated on the answer.
- Phase 0's `session_type:` grep guard would match the new `p_session_type:` RPC argument; step 4 tells the executor to use `\bsession_type:` and keep the guard failing on a real literal.
- Step 6 said the distance unit follows the "account"; the live screen's unit is the workout's own (`page.tsx:27-36`). Corrected to `workout.unit`, one representation.
- Step 9's conditional deletion of `summarizeWorkoutSets`/`findLastExerciseSets`/`workoutsOnDate` ("only if the grep finds no caller") was checked: the only callers are WorkoutLogger and one HistoryTab comment. Made definite, with the test blocks named by line.
- `tests/unit/health/workoutDate.test.ts:148-177` and the "workout" row at 204 verified as the only `CreateWorkoutSchema` uses; `timezoneCounters.test.ts:451` verified as the only remaining `createWorkoutLog` caller; `programRepo.integration.test.ts` verified to reference nothing deleted here.
- Checked and left standing: `finish_program_workout` needs DROP before re-create (signature change) — the draft had this right; the `authenticated` role exists in `schema.sql` (line 594) so the GRANT works in the integration container; `writeCoverage` arithmetic (136 → 135: one "unasserted", one "mentioned") is right; the two `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` lines are 640 and 655.
- Not re-run by the attacker: `supabase migration list --linked` (the draft's "everything applied on 2026-09-17" is the walk's reading; the executor re-checks before pushing, as step 1 says).


**Found while building it, 19 September (all 12 steps).**
- The migration nearly dropped the wrong function. The plan named the ten-parameter `finish_program_workout` from `20260907110000`; Phase 0's `20260917100000` had already grown it to twelve. Caught by reading `tests/integration/schema.sql` before pushing, not by the plan.
- **Clearing the Started box crashed the live screen.** `wallClockToInstant("")` throws, and the sheet fed `startedAt` straight into `new Date(...)` during render — so the first thing anybody does before typing a different time took the whole screen down, with every unsaved set on it. An empty box is a state now, and Save says "Say when it started".
- **The long-gap note read the DEVICE's clock**, so a traveller was told their session started on a day they were not training. It was the last browser clock in `FinishSheet.tsx`; its allowance in the guard is lowered to 0.
- **Enrol in a program and you could not log the session you just did.** `before_program` compared to the second while the time box has MINUTE resolution: it offers 19:30:00 while the enrollment began at 19:30:14, and the only time it could express was refused — on the screen whose job is to accept it. Same minute of slack as the future check. Found by the browser test, not by reading.
- **The first draft of the past-mode test passed with past mode switched OFF.** Its stub answered the set write instantly, and a reply that is not a saved set clears the rest bar anyway — so "no rest bar" could not tell the new behaviour from the stub's. Held open, all three go red without the code. Proved by setting `past = false`.
- **The tap-size guard's own `sm:` exemption never worked.** `sm:min-h-9` was flagged — not through the `min-h-9` branch the lookbehind excludes, but through the bare `h-9` one, whose match starts after the hyphen where nothing says "sm:". The rule was quietly stricter than its comment for every `min-h-*` class. Anchored at a class boundary, and proved both ways.
- **Two browser specs seeded their history through the deleted POST**, which the plan did not name: `programs-history-progress.spec.ts` (two places) and `health-past-workout.spec.ts`. They go through one shared helper now — start, tick, finish — so a fixture cannot drift away from the path a person takes.
- `health-past-workout.spec.ts` was to be deleted (step 12). It was rewritten instead: both faults it proved are real and still worth pinning. Its "half-written workout" half could not be reproduced — `completeSet` UPDATES a re-ticked slot rather than being refused — so the assertion became the structural one: nothing is FINISHED until you finish it, so no phantom session can exist at any point between.
- **The bulk tick cannot be proved in a browser**, and the plan assumed it could. It is past-mode only and needs a prescription, so it needs a program enrollment older than six hours — and nothing in the app can backdate an enrollment. It is unit-tested and revert-proved; the browser spec says so where the assertion would have been.
- Deleting `logProgramSession` first took `skipSession` with it — the script anchored on the wrong doc comment. Caught by the typecheck ratchet within a minute, restored, redone against the section banner.
- `BRIDGE_SESSION_TYPE` survives its two siblings: it is still the session kind a program is recorded under when the program does not say. Renamed `DEFAULT_SESSION_TYPE`, because "bridge" named a thing that no longer exists.
- Deleting the two forms cascaded through fifteen now-unused imports across six files, two of which (`ExerciseSummary`, `round2`) were the last users of their own declarations.

### Open questions for Phase 4

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** 1 (DESTRUCTIVE, gates step 9). May both fill-in-afterwards forms be deleted — the "Log a workout you already did" fold on Training's Today tab ("I did all of this — save it") and the older "Log a past workout" card on the Anything-else tab, the goals-hub Health panel and the /test/health page — along with the two save paths behind them? What you lose: the one-tap "I did all of this — save it". What replaces it: History → "Log a past workout" → pick when and which session → the normal set-by-set screen opens at that time, with a "Did the N fixed sets as shown" button on each lift (it fills only sets with one fixed number — a range like 8–12 or an all-out set you still type yourself), and Started/Ended editable on the finish sheet.

*Recommendation:* Yes, delete both. Every fault in this phase's list (pounds saved as kilos, a slow connection saving a session twice, "45 minutes at effort 3" invented, a blank weight thrown away, three forms with three sets of rules) lives in the two forms, and this is how Hevy and Strong log a past session.

**Open question 2.** 2. Should the server address that the old "Log a past workout" card posted to (POST /api/health/workout) be removed as well, now that nothing in the app calls it? The browser tests that used it to seed workouts would seed through the live path instead (start → sets → finish).

*Recommendation:* Yes, remove it. An address that accepts a workout with no start, no unit conversion and no double-save protection is a loaded gun; the repo already removed one such route (the PATCH on enrollments/[id]/log/[logId]) for the same reason.

**Open question 3.** 3. For a workout started with no program ("Start an empty workout"), when should the app ask what kind of session it was (weights / cardio / mobility / yoga / running)? At the start, or on the finish sheet — where it is pre-set to weights if you ticked at least one set, and otherwise must be chosen before saving? (Phase 0 left a loose workout as "weights" until this phase gave it a way to say otherwise; Phase 6 must not add a second one.)

*Recommendation:* On the finish sheet. Most empty workouts are lifting sessions that tick sets, so the question answers itself; asking at the start adds a step to the one door the Tracking card offers.

**Open question 4.** 4. Distance for a run: the app has no separate distance unit, only kilograms-or-pounds for weights. Is "a pounds workout types miles, a kilos workout types kilometres (stored as km)" the right rule? The unit is the one the workout is already in (its program's, or the account's for an empty workout).

*Recommendation:* Yes. It matches how the two unit systems are used in practice, needs no new setting, and one helper (distanceUnitFor) owns it; if a kilos lifter runs in miles that is a Settings option for later, and the label always says which unit the box is in.

**Open question 5.** 5. If you log a past program session dated BEFORE your most recent one (say you forgot Tuesday and log it on Friday after Thursday's session), should the program (a) count it as your next session and move the weights from where they are now — the rule the deleted form used — or (b) re-run its weights with all sessions in date order, so Tuesday's result sits between Monday's and Thursday's? (b) needs the program's stored starting weights; a program started before those were kept cannot do it and would refuse the backdated session with a plain message BEFORE anything is saved, telling you to date it after your last session or end and restart the program.

*Recommendation:* (b), and refuse loudly on the few old programs without a stored seed. It is the same replay the correction screen already uses, so there is one rule for "what do the weights say now"; (a) leaves the program's weights disagreeing with what a later correction would compute. Step 5 (d) is written for (b); answering (a) means the executor skips it.

**Open question 6.** 6. The finish sheet and the past-workout dialog will read every time you type in your ACCOUNT's time zone (Settings → time preferences), not your phone's. For a person who travels, "18:00" then means 18:00 at home, and the workout files under the home-zone day — the same day History, the week strip and the Tracking tiles use. Is that the rule you want?

*Recommendation:* Yes. The app already files every day in the account's time zone; reading typed times in the phone's would make a 23:30 workout land on a different day than the one the Tracking card calls "today". Phase 1 owns fixing the UTC default for people who never set a time zone; until then those people see UTC times labelled as such on these two screens.


### Blockers for Phase 4

No blockers.


## Phase 5 — /programs Today shows sessions, not an inventory — build S2
**What you can do after:** Open Training and see today's workout — one card, this week as seven dots at the top of it, one orange Start — instead of a list of programs, a blue tab strip and no bottom bar. Managing programs (pick one, change it, put it on set days, skip, reset, end, the finished ones) lives behind one ⋮ menu and one Programs screen, and pressing any of those buttons no longer blanks the page or claims success when the server said no. One thing stays old on purpose: when you open "Change this program", the editor *inside* the new card is still the blue-grey one until Phase 8 repaints it.
**Why it is in this order:** It needs Phase 0's one Start helper (so the card cannot open the wrong day or print a database error), Phase 3's widened "what is today" function (so the Tracking card and this card cannot disagree), and Phase 4's receipt page (so "See today's workout" has somewhere to go); Phases 6–9 then build on the tabs, sheet, row and URL contract this phase lays down.
**Findings this phase closes:** programs-today-02, programs-today-03, programs-today-05, programs-today-06, design-system-05, programs-today-07, programs-today-08, programs-today-10, programs-today-11, design-system-06, walk-02-two-primary-buttons-no-program, walk-03-deleting-label-on-resume, critic-reset-says-weights-go-back-but-keeps-them, tests-and-ci-08

**Dropped from this phase, on purpose:** history-progress-10 — it has two halves. The "every tab switch refetches" half is fixed here (step 2 keeps History and Progress mounted); the "Progress downloads the same year twice, and History and Progress each download it" half needs one shared `useWorkoutHistory(days)` hook replacing the three fetches at `HistoryTab.tsx:85`, `ProgressTab.tsx:50` and `LiftHistory.tsx:61`, and those three files belong to Phase 7. Phase 7 must list history-progress-10; this phase only asserts that a second visit to History issues no new request.

**Depends on (not built here):** Phase 0 — `startWorkoutRequest` (one Start helper with the honest "the request may have landed" copy, stale-key rotation, the wrong-day fix) and the repo guard that refuses ending or displacing a program while its workout is open (critic-end-or-displace-program-while-workout-open; this phase only *shows* that refusal). Phase 3 — `trainingCardState` widened to `unavailable / done / finished` with lift names, `lastTime`, `doneToday`, `setsTicked`, the weekday name of a stale workout's start (S1's "Started Mon 18:02") — all computed from the **profile timezone** — and `chooseCardEnrollment()`. Phase 4 — `/programs/workout/[id]` receipt page, and the deletion of `TodaySessionWidget.tsx`, `RestTimer.tsx`, `WorkoutLogger.tsx` plus the History-tab "Log a past workout" dialog (Decision 3). **Left for later phases on purpose:** the inside of `ProgramEditor` stays the old blue-grey kit until Phase 8 repaints it — after this phase it is the one non-token thing on `/programs`, framed by a token card at `?view=edit`; "Yours" lists finished programs only until Phase 8 adds saved weeks and the "+ Build your own" row; History/Progress content is Phase 7; deleting `src/programs/components/ui.tsx` and the grep test that forbids `sky-`/`zinc-`/10 px text is Phase 9 (design-system-01, -03, -04, -10) — this phase removes `TrainingScreen`'s import of it, which is one of the last two importers.

### Plain-language map of the screen this builds (design spec S2)
- **Today tab, one program:** `← Dashboard` → `Training  ·  StrongLifts 5×5 ›` (link to the Programs screen) → tabs Today / History / Progress → one card: `STRONGLIFTS 5×5 · SESSION 12   ⋮` → this week as seven dots → `Workout A   Change day ▾` → notices → lift rows (`Squat  5 × 5 · 100 kg`) → `▶ Start workout` (the only orange on the page) → `Last time Fri · all 15 sets`. Under the card one quiet row: `Start an empty workout instead ›`. Bottom bar at the bottom.
- **Several programs:** the same card for the program `chooseCardEnrollment` picks (or the one `?program=` names), then `ALSO RUNNING` with one row per other program (`Sprint Triathlon   Run 2 today ›`); tapping a row swaps the card and puts `?program=<id>` in the address so Tracking and Life Mastery can link straight to it.
- **No program:** one card — dumbbell, "No program running", one sentence, `▶ Start a workout` (the orange), `Pick a program ›` row.
- **⋮ menu (bottom sheet, mounted only while open):** Change this program · Put it on set days / Change the days · Skip this session · Reset to start · All programs · End program (red, asks first).
- **Programs screen (`?view=programs`):** `← Training` → **Programs** → "Your Life Mastery plan ›" → RUNNING (rows, each with ⋮) → YOURS (finished programs; ⋮ → Start again / Delete) → CHOOSE ONE (discipline tabs with "All" first, one tappable row per program with a level badge — no buttons) → tapping a row opens `ProgramDetail`, whose `Start …` is that screen's only orange.
- Every screen state is one of loading (92 px pulse) / failed (amber line + Try again, the last-known card stays, never the empty state, never a Start) / empty / ready.
- **Whose clock, everywhere on this page:** every calendar fact — today's weekday, which days were trained this week, "trained today", "last Fri", "started 3 Feb", "Monday's workout" — is computed on the server from `profiles.timezone` (`settingsRepo.getUserTimezone`) and arrives as a weekday number or a `YYYY-MM-DD` string. The browser formats those; it never turns an instant into a date.

### Steps
1. **The address bar owns which screen you are on.** `src/programs/programsService.ts` · new `parseProgramsLocation(params: URLSearchParams, running: ProgramEnrollment[]): ProgramsLocation` (type in `src/programs/types.ts`: `{ tab, view, programId: string | null, catalogId: string | null, enrollmentId: string | null, from: string | null, notice: string | null }`); `src/programs/components/ProgramsApp.tsx` · `ProgramsApp`; `src/programs/components/TrainingScreen.tsx` · `TrainingScreen`; `app/programs/page.tsx` · `ProgramsPage`. Today: the view is component state `useState<View>({ mode: "home" })` (ProgramsApp.tsx:47) and the tab is `useState<Tab | null>` (TrainingScreen.tsx:121), so nothing can link to a tab, a program or the catalogue, and Back always lands on the inventory. Change: one pure function reads `?tab=` (`today|history|progress`), `?view=` (`today|programs|detail|edit`), `?program=` (an enrollment id — kept only when it names a running program, otherwise `programId` is null and `notice` = "That program is not running any more", which `TrainingScreen` renders once as `p.text-sm.text-muted-foreground role="status"` above the card), `?catalog=` (a catalogue id for `view=detail`), `?enrollment=` (for `view=edit`) and `?from=` (through `readReturn`); anything unknown falls back to `today`. `ProgramsPage` (Next 16: `searchParams` is a Promise) awaits it, builds a `URLSearchParams` from it and passes the parsed location down, so the first paint is already the right screen; the client reads `useSearchParams` inside a `Suspense` (the `BackLink` pattern). Two kinds of navigation, deliberately: **tab and `?program=` changes** use `window.history.replaceState(null, "", url)` (Next ≥ 14.1 feeds it to `useSearchParams` without re-running the server page — a tab switch must not re-run `ProgramsPage`'s five queries); **`?view=` changes** are `Link href` or `router.replace` because they change what data the page needs. No `setView` remains. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/programsLocation.test.ts` — "an unknown ?view falls back to today, ?program= survives only for a running enrollment and otherwise yields the not-running notice, and ?from= goes through readReturn".
2. **Three tabs in the app's own language, and switching tabs stops throwing History away.** `src/programs/components/TrainingScreen.tsx` · `TrainingScreen`. Today: the kit `Segmented` (TrainingScreen.tsx:188–198 → ui.tsx:116–120) draws four 11 px sky-blue options including "Anything else", left-aligned, and lines 208–222 mount whichever tab is active so every switch unmounts History/Progress and refetches a year of sets (HistoryTab.tsx:85, ProgressTab.tsx:50); the `training-load-failed` strip's Try again is a raw 26 px `text-xs` button (177–183). Change: shadcn `Tabs` from `components/ui/tabs.tsx` — `<TabsList className="grid h-auto w-full grid-cols-3 p-1">`, `<TabsTrigger className="h-11 sm:h-8">` × 3 (Today / History / Progress), value from `?tab=`, `onValueChange` → `replaceState` per step 1; the "Anything else" tab, its two paragraphs, the `StartLooseWorkout` lazy import and the `WorkoutLogger` lazy import (36–42, 224–250) are deleted — the loose start moves to step 9 and the past-workout dialog is Phase 4's. History and Progress stay lazy (first open downloads the chunk) but once opened they stay mounted behind `TabsContent forceMount` + `hidden`, so a second visit issues no request. The `import { Segmented } from "./ui"` line goes. Try again in `training-load-failed` becomes `<Button size="sm" variant="outline">`. Header: `h1.text-base.font-semibold` "Training" stays one line; the "· part of your Life Mastery plan" aside (145–160) is deleted and becomes a `Link.min-h-11.text-sm.text-muted-foreground` "Your Life Mastery plan ›" → `LIFE_MASTERY` on the Programs screen (step 8); the right side of the h1 row is `Link.min-h-11.inline-flex.items-center.text-sm.text-muted-foreground` "StrongLifts 5×5 ›" / "Programs ›" / "3 programs ›" → `?view=programs`. **Data ownership moves up one level:** `TrainingScreen` now owns `useEnrollment(location.programId ?? chooseCardEnrollment(enrollments)?.id ?? null, initialDetail)` and hands `detail` down to `ProgramsApp` (Today) and to `ProgressTab`'s `programSection` (step 6) — the one hook feeds both tabs, so they cannot disagree. *Test:* `tests/e2e/programs-today.spec.ts` — "the tab strip is three tabs the width of the page, each 44 px tall, no 'Anything else', and a second visit to History issues no request" (asserts `[role=tablist]` width = container ± 2, 3 `[role=tab]` ≥ 44 px, no text "Anything else", and that the count of `/api/health/workout` requests made during History → Progress → History is unchanged by the final History visit).
3. **The bottom bar is on /programs, and the page is the same width as the workout that follows it.** `src/programs/components/TrainingScreen.tsx` · `TrainingScreen`; `components/navTabs.ts` · new export `HIDDEN_ROUTE_PREFIXES`; `components/MobileTabBar.tsx` · `MobileTabBar`. Today: `mx-auto max-w-3xl px-4 pb-6 pt-4` (TrainingScreen.tsx:130) under a comment claiming "a bottom bar this route never mounts" (126–129); no file under `app/programs` or `src/programs` mounts `MobileTabBar` (mount points verified: DashboardPage, ProgressDashboard, ScenariosHub, TimetrackScreen, SettingsPage, HomePage, qa/articles/inner-game pages), so tapping the bar's own "Training" tab lands on the one page without a bar (navTabs.ts:31 → MobileTabBar.tsx:49–63); `HIDDEN_ROUTE_PREFIXES` is a module-private const at MobileTabBar.tsx:12 that no test can import; `/programs/live` is `max-w-2xl` (LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx:228) so the column shrinks twice on the way in. Change: shell becomes `div.mx-auto.max-w-2xl.px-4.pb-tab-bar.pt-4` (`pb-tab-bar` exists, globals.css:251) with `data-testid="training-screen"` and `<MobileTabBar />` rendered last; the false comment is deleted; `HIDDEN_ROUTE_PREFIXES` moves to `components/navTabs.ts` (next to `TABS`, exported, "as data rather than as markup" like the file says), gains `"/programs/live"` (RestBar owns that screen's bottom edge; `pb-28` at LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx:217 already reserves it), and `MobileTabBar` imports it. Width changes only at the route boundary from Tracking (6xl) to Training (2xl), as the spec's cross-cutting rule says. Widened to the class: a derived guard that every route the bar reaches renders the bar. *Test:* `tests/unit/navigation/tabBarDestinations.test.ts` — "every route in TAB_ROUTES renders MobileTabBar somewhere in its page's component tree, and /programs/live is in HIDDEN_ROUTE_PREFIXES" (walks imports the way routeReachability does; fails if a new tab destination forgets); and `tests/e2e/programs-today.spec.ts` — "the bottom bar is on /programs at 390 px with Training aria-current=page, absent at 1280 px and absent on /programs/live, and the content column is the same width on /programs and /programs/live at 1280 px".
4. **The session card is drawn from the one shared "what is today" object, in the spec's shape.** `src/programs/components/TodayCard.tsx` · `TodayCard`; `src/programs/components/SessionNotices.tsx` · `SessionNotices`. Today: renders straight from `prescription` in a `Card` + `CardContent p-4` (TodayCard.tsx:99–100 — double padding, per the cross-cutting rule), shows every day chip permanently (121–140), has no done-today state, no week, does its own `fetch("/api/workouts")` (67–78), and names a stale workout's day with `new Date(live.startedAt).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: "long" })` (176) — the **browser's** zone for a calendar fact; `SessionNotices`' two buttons are raw `min-h-9` buttons (SessionNotices.tsx:66–79). Change: props become `{ state: TrainingCardState, enrollment, prescription, week, days, from }` where `state` is Phase 3's widened `trainingCardState(...)` result computed once in `TrainingScreen` from server facts only (`detail.week` from step 5, `live`, `prescription`) — the card receives the stale workout's weekday name from `state`, never computes it; layout per S2 (b) 5–13: `<Card data-testid="today-card" className="gap-0 py-0">` › `div.px-4.py-4.space-y-3`; program line `span.text-xs.uppercase.tracking-wide.text-muted-foreground` "StrongLifts 5×5 · session 12" + `Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="hover:bg-muted/50" aria-label="Program options" data-testid="program-menu"` `MoreVertical` (opens step 6's sheet); `WeekStrip` (step 5) inside the card; title row `p.text-lg.font-semibold` day label + `Button variant="ghost" size="sm"` "Change day ▾" that reveals the chips as `Button variant="outline" size="sm" aria-pressed` (active `border-primary/50 bg-primary/10 text-primary`), only for multi-day programs, open by default on a rest day under "Train anyway?"; `SessionNotices` unchanged copy with its two buttons as `Button size="sm" variant="outline"` / `"ghost"`; lift rows `ul.space-y-1.5 › li.flex.justify-between.gap-3.text-sm` name + `span.text-xs.tabular-nums.text-muted-foreground` `describeSets()`, endurance days list blocks in `text-sm`; THE button `<Button size="lg" className="w-full" data-testid="start-workout"><Play/>Start workout</Button>` per the S2 state table — rest day "Start Workout B early" (POST with `dayId`), live `Resume · 23 min` (`resume-workout`), stale "Finish or discard Monday's workout" (weekday from `state`), another program's workout open → `variant="outline"` "Finish the workout you have open first", done today → `variant="outline"` "See today's workout" → `/programs/workout/[id]` (Phase 4) plus a text link "Train again", program finished → no Start (the emerald notice's two buttons only); Start calls Phase 0's `startWorkoutRequest({ enrollmentId, dayId, clientKey: startKeyFor(enrollmentId) })` and pushes `/programs/live?from=/programs`; start failure = `p.text-sm.text-destructive role="alert"` with the helper's sentence, button re-enabled; last-time line `p.text-xs.text-muted-foreground` only when a log for this day exists. No `fetch(`, no `toLocaleDateString(` and no `new Date()` for a calendar fact remains in the file. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/todayCard.test.tsx` — "the day label is the first text-lg element, there is exactly one Start, a rest day reads 'Start Workout B early' with the chips open, done-today shows 'See today's workout' with no Start, and a stale workout names the weekday the state object carries, not the machine's" (RTL, router mocked, `TZ=Pacific/Auckland` for the run so a browser-zone weekday would differ).
5. **The week is seven dots on the account's clock, not the phone's — and every date label on the page comes from the server.** `src/programs/components/WeekStrip.tsx` · `WeekStrip`; `src/programs/programsService.ts` · new pure `weekSoFar(logs, timezone, now: Date): WeekSoFar` and new pure `formatDateOnly(iso: string, style: "weekday" | "short"): string`; `src/db/programRepo.ts` · new `getEnrollmentDetail(userId, enrollmentId): Promise<EnrollmentDetail>`, and `listActiveEnrollments` / `listPastEnrollments` gain date-only labels; `src/programs/types.ts` · `EnrollmentDetail` gains `week: WeekSoFar` = `{ timezone, weekStartedOn, todayWeekday, trainedWeekdays: number[], trainedToday: boolean }`, `ProgramEnrollment` gains `startedOn: string` and `lastLoggedOn: string | null` (both `YYYY-MM-DD` in the profile timezone, beside the instants they are derived from). Today: the strip highlights `isoWeekday(new Date())` (WeekStrip.tsx:270) while the card's day comes from the server's `todayWeekday` (programRepo.ts:606–617); ProgramsApp works out "trained this week" with the browser's zone (`periodStartFor("weekly", new Date())` at ProgramsApp.tsx:231, `isoWeekday(new Date(l.logged_at))` at 327); PastPrograms and the inventory print `new Date(x).toLocaleDateString()` for "started" and "last" (PastPrograms.tsx:202, 213; ProgramsApp.tsx:182, 187) — four readings of one fact, three of them on the wrong clock; the tiles are 56 px with 10 px captions and the word "done" (WeekStrip.tsx:362–370); the unanchored branch is a sentence plus an underlined text button (304–342). Change: `weekSoFar` uses `periodStartFor("weekly", getNowInTimezone(timezone, now))` for the week start and `getTodayInTimezone(timezone, new Date(log.logged_at))` per log (both helpers exist in `src/shared/dateUtils.ts` and take an `at`; `periodStartInTimezone` does **not** take a `now`, so it is not used) — the timezone is `profiles.timezone` via `settingsRepo.getUserTimezone`, `now` is passed in, and `new Date()` is never used for "today" on the server. `getEnrollmentDetail` is the ONE constructor of `EnrollmentDetail` (`{ enrollment, prescription, logs, week }`) and both `app/programs/page.tsx` (lines 52–58, which now resolve the detail for `location.programId ?? chooseCardEnrollment(active)?.id`, not only when `active.length === 1`, so three programs also paint the card first time) and `app/api/programs/enrollments/[id]/route.ts` GET (lines 14–18) call it, so the two can never disagree. The list functions compute `startedOn`/`lastLoggedOn` with the same `getTodayInTimezone(tz, instant)` in one private helper, and every "last Fri" / "last 12 Sep" / "started 3 Feb" on this page is `formatDateOnly(...)` of those strings (a `YYYY-MM-DD` string has no zone to get wrong). `WeekStrip` becomes presentational: `div[data-testid=week-strip].grid.grid-cols-7.gap-1`, cells `h-11 rounded-md text-xs` (`button` when anchored — tapping opens step 6's day picker for that weekday — `div` when not), weekday `text-xs uppercase text-muted-foreground`, dot `size-2 rounded-full`: `bg-emerald-500` trained this week, `ring-1 ring-primary` today, `bg-muted-foreground/30` to come; no legend, no "Runs in order…" sentence, no "done", no `new Date()`, no `fetch(` (assignment moves to step 6's hook). Delete `src/programs/components/WeekStrip.tsx` from `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` and from `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA`. Widened to the class: a new architecture test forbids `isoWeekday(new Date`, `periodStartFor(` and `toLocaleDateString(` anywhere under `src/programs/components/` — the way a calendar fact reaches a training screen is a server-computed weekday number or date-only string, full stop. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/weekSoFar.test.ts` — "a 23:45 Monday workout in Europe/Copenhagen is Monday's dot and trainedToday, and a 00:10 Tuesday workout is Tuesday's, when the process clock is UTC"; `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` — "training screens never read the browser clock for a calendar fact"; `tests/e2e/programs-today.spec.ts` — "the week strip has seven cells ≥ 44 px, shows no emerald dot before training this week, and today's cell gets one after a workout is finished".
6. **One ⋮ menu for the program, and every action checks the server's answer.** New `components/BottomSheet.tsx` · `BottomSheet` (extracted verbatim from `MobileTabBar.tsx:79–130` — `fixed inset-0 z-50`, backdrop `bg-background/60 backdrop-blur-sm`, panel `bg-card rounded-t-2xl border-t border-border pb-safe animate-slide-up`, rows `min-h-11 px-3 py-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium`; **rendered only while `open`**, like the More sheet, so a closed sheet leaves nothing in the DOM; at ≥ 640 px it renders as a centred `Dialog`); `components/MobileTabBar.tsx` · `MobileTabBar` switches its "More" sheet to it; new `src/programs/hooks/useProgramActions.ts` · `useProgramActions(enrollmentId)` returning `endProgram / skipSession / resetProgram / restartProgram / assignWeekday / updateSchedule / enroll / resumePast / deletePast`, each `await fetch` → `res.ok` → `{ ok: true }` or `{ ok: false, error: body.error ?? <plain sentence> }` and `refreshEnrollments()` (exported at useEnrollment.ts:175) **only** on success; new `src/programs/components/ProgramSheet.tsx` · `ProgramSheet`; `src/programs/components/ProgressionView.tsx` · `ProgressionView` → renamed export `ProgramProgress`; `src/programs/programsService.ts` · new `export const RESET_EVENT = { kind: "reset", cursor: true, weights: false } as const` and `RESET_CONFIRM_COPY` **derived from** `RESET_EVENT.weights`; `src/db/programRepo.ts` · `resetEnrollment` writes `{ at, ...RESET_EVENT }` (lines 480–491). Today: `unenroll()` does `await fetch(DELETE)` then `onUnenrolled()` with no `res.ok` check (ProgressionView.tsx:107–108), and ProgramsApp's `onFinish` does the same for archive and restart (ProgramsApp.tsx:350–359), so a refused End jumps to the catalogue while the program keeps prescribing; Skip has no confirmation (272–274) and on a weekday-pinned program only bumps the session number because `getTodaySession` picks by weekday (programRepo.ts:606–620); Reset's confirm says "Your current weights go back to where you began" (284) while `resetEnrollment` keeps every weight and writes `{kind:'reset', cursor:true, weights:false}` (programRepo.ts:488; the replay branch at programsService.ts:1865–1867 says so) — the sentence and the flag are two hands that never meet; the four buttons and the History disclosure sit on the Today tab (266–294, 139–155). Change: `ProgramSheet` (`data-testid="program-sheet"`) rows — "Change this program" → `?view=edit&enrollment=<id>`; "Put it on set days" (unanchored) / "Change the days" (anchored) → the day-assignment picker moved out of WeekStrip: `Button variant="outline" size="sm" aria-pressed` chips per day → `assignWeekday(dayId, weekday | null)` (the `setWeekday` + PUT `/schedule` path from WeekStrip.tsx:272–294), hidden for endurance; "Skip this session" → `Dialog` "Skip Workout B? The program moves on to the next one." → `skipSession`, and the row is **absent** when `isWeekdayAnchored(schedule)` (programsService.ts:162; open question 4); "Reset to start" → `Dialog` whose copy is `RESET_CONFIRM_COPY` = "Start again from week 1? Your weights stay where they are." (open question 1) — one owner: change `RESET_EVENT.weights` and the sentence, the repo write and the replay all change together; "All programs" → `?view=programs`; "End program" (`text-destructive`) → `Dialog` "End StrongLifts 5×5? It stops prescribing sessions. Everything you logged is kept." → `endProgram` → on success `router.replace("/programs?view=programs")`; every failure renders `p.role=alert.text-sm.text-destructive` with the server's sentence inside the sheet (the Phase 0 guard's "Finish or throw away the open workout first" arrives here verbatim). `SessionNotices`' archive/restart go through the same hook. `ProgressionView.tsx` loses the button row, `unenroll`, `action`, the History disclosure and `data-testid="history-toggle"`; what is left — the unbroken-run notice, the per-lift sparklines and the sessions list (169–257) — is exported as `ProgramProgress` and mounted by `ProgressTab` through one new optional prop `programSection?: ReactNode`, supplied by `TrainingScreen` from the `detail` it owns after step 2 (that one prop is the only edit to ProgressTab in this phase; Phase 7 owns the rest of that file). Delete `ProgressionView.tsx` from `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA`. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/useProgramActions.test.ts` — "End program reports a 400 or 409 in the server's words and never reports the program gone; Skip is refused for a weekday-anchored schedule" (mocked fetch); `tests/unit/programs/resetCopy.test.ts` — "RESET_CONFIRM_COPY says the weights stay exactly when RESET_EVENT.weights is false, and replaying RESET_EVENT leaves exercise_state untouched" (flip the flag in the test and the sentence must change); `tests/e2e/programs-today.spec.ts` — "the program menu lists Change this program, Skip this session, Reset to start, All programs, End program, each ≥ 44 px; End opens a dialog; with DELETE mocked to 409 the program is still on screen and the sheet shows the server's sentence".
7. **"Change this program" opens the editor in one tap, in the app's colours.** `src/programs/components/EditActiveProgram.tsx` · `EditActiveProgram`; `src/programs/components/ProgramsApp.tsx` · `ProgramsApp`. Each section has its own failed state (amber line + `Button size="sm" variant="outline"` Try again) and never renders as empty on failure. `ProgramDetail` keeps its card, `Label`+`Input` weights and its `Start …` (the flow's one orange); `onBack` is replaced by `BackLink fallback="/programs?view=programs" fallbackLabel="Programs"`; `enroll` goes through `useProgramActions.enroll` and on success `router.replace("/programs?view=today&program=<newId>")` (Phase 8 adds the repainted `ProgramEditor` inside it). `PastPrograms.tsx` keeps only the row rendering (dates via `formatDateOnly`) and is mounted nowhere but `ProgramsScreen`. 9. **The Today tab is a session, the empty state has one orange, and nothing on it blanks the screen.** `src/programs/components/ProgramsApp.tsx` · `ProgramsApp` (absorbs `ActiveProgram`); `src/programs/components/StartLooseWorkout.tsx` · deleted.
