### Acceptance
- `tests/unit/programs/programsLocation.test.ts` — "an unknown ?view falls back to today…": the address bar is the only source of which screen shows; a stale `?program=` cannot pin a program that ended and says so once; `?from=` cannot be an open redirect.
- `tests/e2e/programs-today.spec.ts` — "the tab strip is three tabs the width of the page…": the kit tab strip is gone, tabs are 44 px, no "Anything else", and a second visit to History issues no request (the tab-switch half of history-progress-10; the shared-fetch half is Phase 7's).
- `tests/unit/navigation/tabBarDestinations.test.ts` + "the bottom bar is on /programs…": tapping Training in the bar no longer lands on the one page without the bar (programs-today-06 / design-system-05), the live screen keeps its own bottom bar, and `/programs` and `/programs/live` share one column width (programs-today-11).
- `tests/unit/programs/todayCard.test.tsx`: the card is the spec's shape with one primary per state, a rest day offers a way in, done-today does not re-offer the next session as if today were untouched, and a stale workout's weekday is the account's, not the machine's.
- `tests/unit/programs/weekSoFar.test.ts` + "training screens never read the browser clock…" + "the week strip has seven cells ≥ 44 px…": strip, card and every date label agree about today and about which days were trained, on the account's timezone (programs-today-08); no dot before this week's first workout, one after.
- `tests/unit/programs/useProgramActions.test.ts` + "the program menu lists…": End/Skip/Reset/restart only report success when the server said so (programs-today-03); Skip asks first and is absent where it does nothing (programs-today-07); a refused End keeps the program on screen with the reason.
- `tests/unit/programs/resetCopy.test.ts`: the Reset sentence, the reset event the repo writes and the replay all derive from one `RESET_EVENT` (critic-reset-says-weights-go-back-but-keeps-them).
- `tests/unit/programs/editActiveProgram.test.tsx` + "Change this program in the menu opens the editor in one tap": one button, one tap, one look (programs-today-05, the EditActiveProgram half of programs-today-10).
- `tests/unit/programs/programCatalog.test.tsx` + `tests/e2e/programs-catalog.spec.ts`: the catalogue is rows you can tap, the Programs screen has no orange, enrolling through the screen works end to end, and "Start again" never says "Deleting…" (walk-03-deleting-label-on-resume).
- `tests/unit/programs/programsApp.test.tsx` + "every Today-tab action keeps the card on screen…" + "no program: start-loose-workout is the only orange…" + "three programs…" + "spec hygiene…": the Today tab is a session not an inventory, three programs paint a card on the first paint, the empty state has one primary (walk-02-two-primary-buttons-no-program), no action blanks the tab (programs-today-02), the six button styles and the raw `<details>` are gone (design-system-06; the Segmented/blue half of programs-today-10), the page is ≤ 1,100 px and every control ≥ 44 px.
- `tests/e2e/mobile/mobile-training.spec.ts` rewritten cases: the week-strip touch-target test measures, and "both ways" proves a set can be started from the Today tab (tests-and-ci-08).
- **Guards that must stay green, with allowlists that shrink and never grow:** `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` — `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` loses eight lines (`EditActiveProgram`, `PastPrograms`, `ProgramDetail`, `ProgramsApp`, `ProgressionView`, `StartLooseWorkout`, `TodayCard`, `WeekStrip` — all eight confirmed present at lines 640–658; the "only shrinks" test fails until they are removed) and the new files `ProgramSheet.tsx` / `ProgramsScreen.tsx` / `ProgramRow.tsx` / `BottomSheet.tsx` contain no `fetch(`; `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` loses `EditActiveProgram.tsx` and `WeekStrip.tsx` (confirmed at lines 700–730); the new "no browser clock for a calendar fact" test starts with an empty allowlist; icon registry — `MoreVertical`, `ChevronRight`, `Trash2`, `Play`, `Loader2`, `AlertTriangle`, `X` are utility (confirmed in `iconRoles.ts`), `Dumbbell` is registered for "workout logging entry point", nothing new; `tests/unit/navigation/routeReachability.test.ts` — every new screen is reached by an `href` from a mounted component (`?view=programs` from the header link, `?view=detail` from catalogue rows, `?view=edit` from the sheet); `tests/unit/navigation/backNavigation.test.ts` — `/programs` is already skipped as a `TAB_ROUTES` destination (backNavigation.test.ts:141), and `ProgramsScreen` / `ProgramDetail` / `EditActiveProgram` each carry a `BackLink`; `tests/unit/writeCoverage.test.ts` — `MAX_UNASSERTED` (136) unchanged (this phase adds reads and hooks, no new `*Service`/`*Repo` write; `getEnrollmentDetail` and the list-label helper are reads); `tests/e2e/sweep/route-sweep.spec.ts` `TAP_TARGET_DEBT` loses `/programs`.
- **Read it in the browser before calling it done** (ui.md): sign in as `test-user-b@…`, walk one program / three programs / none / rest day / a mocked 409 on End at 390 × 844 and 1280 × 900, keep the screenshots under `.playwright-mcp/`, and report which states were seen versus inferred.

**No blockers.** This phase adds no migration. The executor still runs `supabase migration list --linked` read-only before anything else; the browser walkthrough of 2026-09-14 (walk-findings §7) found every migration applied, including the untracked `20260914120000_drop_four_orphan_tables.sql`, which belongs to another agent and must not be pushed.

### Attack log
- **coveredIds named findings that do not exist.** `walk-02` / `walk-03` are not ids in findings-compact.json; the real ids are `walk-02-two-primary-buttons-no-program` and `walk-03-deleting-label-on-resume`. Corrected everywhere.
- **Step 3 named a symbol a test cannot import.** `HIDDEN_ROUTE_PREFIXES` is a module-private `const` at MobileTabBar.tsx:12, so "tabBarDestinations asserts /programs/live is in HIDDEN_ROUTE_PREFIXES" could not be written. Moved it to `components/navTabs.ts` as an export (the file that already holds the bar's data), MobileTabBar imports it.
- **Step 5 named a helper signature that does not exist.** `periodStartInTimezone(period, timezone)` (dateUtils.ts:156) takes no `now`, so `weekSoFar(logs, timezone, now)` could not be pure through it. Rewritten on `periodStartFor("weekly", getNowInTimezone(tz, now))` and `getTodayInTimezone(tz, instant)`, both of which take an `at`.
- **Step 2's e2e assertion was false as written.** "Exactly one `/api/health/workout` request across History → Progress → History" would count three: HistoryTab.tsx:85, ProgressTab.tsx:50 and LiftHistory.tsx:61 each fetch. The assertion now says the final History visit adds no request, and history-progress-10 is dropped to Phase 7 (which owns the three files) with the reason written in the phase.
- **Whose clock, four times.** (a) TodayCard.tsx:176 names a stale workout's day with `toLocaleDateString(undefined, {weekday})` — the browser's zone — and the draft kept the copy without saying where "Monday" comes from; now it comes from Phase 3's state and the unit test runs under `TZ=Pacific/Auckland`. (b) The draft's Running rows "last Fri" and PastPrograms' "started / last" (PastPrograms.tsx:202, 213) format instants in the browser's zone; the repo now returns `startedOn`/`lastLoggedOn` as date-only strings in the profile timezone and the rows format those. (c) `trainingCardState` with `doneToday` needs "today's logs" — the draft computed it "once in ProgramsApp" with no timezone in hand; `EnrollmentDetail.week` now carries `trainedToday`, `todayWeekday`, `trainedWeekdays` from the server. (d) The architecture guard widened from two patterns to three (`toLocaleDateString(` added) so the class is closed, not the instance.
- **Step 6's `programSection` had no data source.** `ProgramProgress` needs `logs` and `enrollment`, which lived in `ActiveProgram`'s `useEnrollment` inside ProgramsApp; TrainingScreen could not supply it. The hook is hoisted to TrainingScreen (one hook feeds Today and Progress), stated in step 2.
- **Three programs would paint a pulse, not a card.** page.tsx:52 resolves `detail` only when `active.length === 1`; the draft claimed "the first paint is already the right screen". page.tsx now resolves `getEnrollmentDetail` for `location.programId ?? chooseCardEnrollment(active)?.id`, and the three-programs e2e asserts no pulse on first paint.
- **Reset copy was still two representations.** A `RESET_CONFIRM_COPY` string beside a `weights:false` literal in programRepo.ts:488 is exactly the "two hands" the critic named. Now one `RESET_EVENT` constant owns the flag; the repo writes it, the replay reads it, the sentence derives from it, and the test flips the flag to prove the sentence follows.
- **Spec conflict left silent: 13 rows vs discipline tabs.** With a discipline tab selected only that discipline's rows exist, so "13 catalogue rows ≥ 56 px" cannot pass. Added an "All" default tab and open question 6.
- **Tab switches would re-run the server page.** `router.replace` with a changed query re-renders `ProgramsPage` (five queries) on every tab tap — the refetch the phase claims to remove, moved one layer down. Tab and `?program=` changes now use `window.history.replaceState` (Next 16 feeds it to `useSearchParams`); `?view=` changes keep `Link`/`router.replace`.
- **Step 11 named the wrong table.** The per-page count `"/programs": 3` at route-sweep.spec.ts:165 lives in `TAP_TARGET_DEBT` (line 81); `KNOWN_DEBT` (line 60) is the rule-exemption table. Corrected.
- **The "not running any more" notice had no home.** Step 1 said it is shown but no step rendered it. `ProgramsLocation.notice` + one `role=status` line in TrainingScreen.
- **Spec S2 (e) checks the draft never asserted.** Page height ≤ 1,100 px, every control ≥ 44 px, the forbidden-strings list, "Start an empty workout" at most once — added as a "spec hygiene" e2e case. Note that "All programs" is a sheet row, so the sheet must be unmounted when closed (BottomSheet renders only while open) for that assertion to hold; written into step 6.
- **Spec S2 element 19 was missed.** `training-load-failed`'s Try again is a raw 26 px button (TrainingScreen.tsx:177–183); now `Button size="sm" variant="outline"` in step 2. `SessionNotices.tsx` was edited by step 4 but absent from `touches`; added.
- **Test fixtures were unnamed.** "StrongLifts + Couch to 5K + Bodyweight Foundations" now carry their catalogue ids (`stronglifts-5x5`, `couch-to-5k`, `bodyweight-foundations` — strength / cardio / calisthenics, verified, so enrolling one does not displace another) and every e2e that starts a program ends it.
- **The plain-language opening hid the one thing left ugly.** The editor inside "Change this program" stays blue-grey until Phase 8; the opening now says so.
- **Blockers claimed a command this drafter never ran.** "`supabase migration list --linked` was run read-only" — the attacker did not run it either; the claim is now attributed to the walkthrough that did, and the executor is told to run it.
- **Checked and found sound:** all eight `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` names and both `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` names exist in the allowlists; `refreshEnrollments`, `startKeyFor`, `isWeekdayAnchored`, `readReturn`/`withReturn`, `BackLink{fallback,fallbackLabel}`, `pb-tab-bar`, `hide-scrollbar`, `components/ui/tabs.tsx` (Radix, `forceMount` available), `Dialog`, all named icons in the utility list, `ProgramPicker`'s single importer, `e2e-isolation`'s serial rule, `backNavigation`'s TAB_ROUTES skip, and every line number cited in "Today:" clauses.

### Open questions for Phase 5

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** "Reset to start" today warns that your weights go back to where you began, but the code keeps every weight and only rewinds you to week 1. Which one do you want: keep the weights and fix the sentence, or make the button really reset the weights to the starting numbers?

*Recommendation:* Keep the weights and fix the sentence to "Start again from week 1? Your weights stay where they are." The engine's replay is built around weights surviving a reset; making the button wipe them would need a new replay event and a risky rewrite of the correction path. Either way the phase makes one constant own the answer, so changing your mind later is one line.

**Open question 2.** May I delete the old green-and-black program picker (ProgramPicker.tsx)? Its only use is the "attach a program" boxes on the localhost-only /test/new-goals lab, which would become a link to the Training page's Programs screen instead of enrolling you on save.

*Recommendation:* Yes, delete it and link out. The lab never ships to production, and keeping a second copy of the catalogue is exactly what the design spec removes.

**Open question 3.** This phase removes the "Anything else" tab and its "Start a workout now" button from Training (the loose start moves under the session card). If you say no to Decision 3 (deleting both write-up-afterwards forms, which Phase 4 acts on), where should "Log a past workout" live once that tab is gone?

*Recommendation:* Say yes to Decision 3; then it is the History tab's "Log a past workout" dialog that Phase 4 builds. If no, put the old form behind that same History-tab button rather than bringing the fourth tab back.

**Open question 4.** On a program whose days are pinned to weekdays (Mon/Wed/Fri), "Skip this session" changes nothing you can see — today's day is picked by the calendar, not by the cursor. Should the Skip row simply not appear for those programs, or should skipping mean something new (for example, mark today as missed)?

*Recommendation:* Hide the row for weekday-pinned programs. A control that records a skip nobody can see is worse than no control; if a "mark today as missed" is wanted later it is a new feature, not a reinterpretation of Skip.

**Open question 5.** Design-spec Decision 12: the bottom tab bar goes on /programs and NOT on /programs/live (the live screen keeps its own rest-timer bar at the bottom). Confirm?

*Recommendation:* Yes. The Training tab currently lands on the only page without the bar, and two fixed bars on the live screen would cover the rest timer.

**Open question 6.** The design spec asks for two things on the Programs screen that cannot both be true at once: discipline tabs (Strength | Bodybuilding | …) AND all 13 catalogue rows visible. With a discipline selected you only see that discipline's programs. May the first tab be "All", selected by default, so the full list shows and the discipline tabs narrow it?

*Recommendation:* Yes — "All" first and default. It keeps the spec's row-count check honest and a first-time visitor sees the whole catalogue without knowing which discipline to tap.


### Blockers for Phase 5

No blockers.


## Phase 6 — The live screen keeps its promises — build S3
**What you can do after:** During a workout you can swap a lift when the rack is taken, move lifts up or down, add a warm-up set, change the rest time (and it stays changed after the phone locks), tap a set number to mark it warm-up / working / drop set and say how hard it was, swipe a set away, and see what you did last time even on a lift you added or a workout with no program. The finish screen stops calling an added lift a "miss", stops claiming one missed set "will bring the weight down" when it only holds it, lets you fix both the start and the end time, and — once a self-built week carries a name (Phase 8 gives it one at start) — the header shows that name instead of "Your own program". A run done live records its distance and counts as a run on the Tracking tiles; the History row's wording for a run is Phase 7's.
**Why it is in this order:** It needs Phase 4's receipt component and Phase 5's BottomSheet and loose-start row, and Phase 7 needs the facts this phase starts writing (set kinds, per-set effort, edited rest, distance, session type) before History can show them.
**Findings this phase closes:** live-workout-02, live-workout-03, live-workout-04, live-workout-05, live-workout-06, live-workout-07, live-workout-08, live-workout-09, live-workout-10, critic-live-lift-menu-still-only-skips, critic-live-header-calls-your-week-your-own-program, walk-04-added-lift-counted-as-miss, walk-09-start-key-flash-not-saved, design-system-09, critic-live-run-is-an-empty-weights-session, data-integrity-04

**Relies on, not fixed here:** the lost-finish guard and the stale start key (programs-today-04 / live-workout-01 / data-integrity-01 / data-integrity-10 → Phase 0; spec S3 element 23); `WorkoutReceipt` and `/programs/workout/[id]` (Phase 4); `components/BottomSheet.tsx` and the `?from=` convention on the Start buttons (Phase 5); one definition of a personal best and the "First time logged" line inside the receipt (walk-05 / critic-three-definitions → Phase 7 — **Phase 7 also owns the change to tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts L189, which today expects "New best" on the account's first-ever set; this phase leaves that assertion alone**); the History row reading "Run · 5.2 km · 31 min" instead of "No sets were recorded for this one." (`src/programs/components/HistoryTab.tsx` L388 / L480 — the wording half of critic-live-run → Phase 7, which owns HistoryTab); the enrollment `label` being set when a self-built week is started (lm-build-own-06 → Phase 8; step 16 here reads the label, Phase 8 writes it); the deletion of `WorkoutLogger` closes after-the-fact-logging-06 (Phase 4) and the deletion of `ui.tsx` closes design-system-10 (Phase 9).

**Whose clock:** elapsed and rest countdowns compare browser `Date.now()` against instants the browser itself recorded (never against the server's `completed_at`); every date or wall-clock time shown ("Workout · 12 Sep", "since Mon 18:02", the Started/Ended editors, the lift-history rows) is rendered in the account's timezone read once on the server with `settingsRepo.getUserTimezone` (already returned by `prescriptionForDay`) and passed to the screen as a `timezone` prop; the server never derives a *date* from `new Date()` (an *instant* fallback such as `finishWorkout`'s `endedAt ?? lastSet ?? now` is fine — it is a moment, not a calendar day).

**Shallow-merge rule the executor must not forget:** `workoutRepo.adjustWorkout` (L298) merges `{ ...live.adjustments, ...patch }` one key deep. Every `adjust()` call in this phase therefore sends the **whole** value of the key it changes (`rest` as the full map, `added` as the full array, `order` as the full list, `blocksDone` as the full list), built from the screen's latest copy. A step that sends a partial map silently erases the rest.

### Steps
1. **The program author's rest reaches the screen, and one function owns the rest target.** `src/programs/types.ts` · `PrescribedExercise` (L550, no `restSec`); `src/programs/programsService.ts` · `carried()` (L560, copies supersetGroup/dropSets/note/perSide/repUnit and drops `restSec`); `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` L272 calls `restSecondsFor({ name: ex.name })` so every program's rest is "our suggestion". Change: add `restSec?: number` to `PrescribedExercise`, copy it in `carried()`, and export `restTargetFor(ex: PrescribedExercise, adjustments: WorkoutAdjustments, opts?: { warmup?: boolean }): { seconds; ours; edited }` from programsService = `adjustments.rest?.[ex.exerciseId]` (edited: true, ours: false) → else `restSecondsFor(ex, opts)` (so a warm-up tick still gets the 45 s warm-up rest unless the lift's rest was edited). The sub line ("· rest 3:00"), the lift-menu row, and RestBar's target all call this one function; nothing else on the screen computes a rest. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/liveRest.test.ts — "Bodyweight Foundations' own 90 s / 180 s rest reaches the prescription and is not our suggestion"; "an edited rest on the workout beats the author's and the default"; "a warm-up tick on an unedited lift rests 45 s".
2. **An edited rest is saved on the workout so it survives a reload.** `src/programs/schemas.ts` · `AdjustWorkoutSchema` (L302, no `rest`); `src/programs/types.ts` · `WorkoutAdjustments` (L729: skipped/incomplete/swapped/added/order). Change: add `rest?: Record<string, number>` (integer 15–600) to both. The lift menu's "Rest after this lift [−15 s] 2:30 [+15 s]" row edits a **local draft** in the sheet and writes **once**, when the sheet closes or 600 ms after the last tap, through `programsService.withRest(adjustments, exerciseId, seconds)` (returns the full `rest` map, clamped 15–600) → `live.adjust({ rest })` (PATCH `/api/workouts/[id]`, `adjustWorkout` L298). Two quick taps must read 3:30, not 3:15 — the draft is the source until the write lands. RestBar's `−30 s / +30 s` change only the running countdown, never the saved target. Caption reads "3:00 is our suggestion" until edited, then "2:30, your own". *Test:* tests/unit/programs/schemas.test.ts — "AdjustWorkoutSchema accepts rest per lift and refuses 10 s and 601 s"; tests/unit/programs/liveRest.test.ts — "withRest keeps every other lift's rest and replaces one"; tests/e2e/programs-live-menus.spec.ts — "rest +15 s tapped twice within 200 ms reads 3:30, exactly one PATCH is sent, the next tick starts at 3:30, and it is still 3:30 after a reload".
3. **The rest countdown comes back after a reload.** `src/programs/components/live/LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` · `restFrom` / `restSeconds` / `restOurs` (L70–72, plain `useState`, gone on reload). Change: the hook `useLiveWorkout` owns `rest: { workoutId, exerciseId, from, seconds, ours } | null` and exposes `startRest(exerciseId, seconds, ours)`, `extendRest(delta)`, `dismissRest()`; it is written to localStorage `live-workout-rest-v1` next to the queue, restored on mount when `workoutId` matches and `from + seconds·1000 + 5 000 > Date.now()`, cleared by `finish`/`discard` together with the queue (`writeQueue([])` L386 gets a sibling). Both instants are browser time; the server's `completed_at` is never used for this. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/useLiveWorkout.test.tsx — "the rest clock comes back after a reload with the seconds that were left, and not for a different workout"; "finish and discard both forget the rest clock"; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "the rest bar is still counting after a hard reload".
4. **Superset pairs read A1/A2 and share one card.** `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` L291–295 prints `{ex.supersetGroup}{i + 1}` with `i` from the whole list. Change: export `groupOrdinal(exercises, i)` from programsService (count of earlier lifts with the same group + 1); a pair renders as one `<Card className="gap-0 py-0" data-testid="lift-{firstId}">` with `divide-y`, two headers, tag `rounded bg-primary/15 px-1.5 text-xs uppercase text-primary`, and a "rest 2:00 after the pair" line from step 1's `restTargetFor` of the pair's last lift; `isLastOfGroup` (L210, exists) keeps the clock on the pair's last tick. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — "Push/Pull/Legs Push A numbers its pairs A1/A2 and B1/B2, never A4/A5"; tests/e2e/programs-live-menus.spec.ts — "a superset day shows two lifts in one lift card; A1's last tick shows no rest bar, A2's does".
5. **A set the server refuses is dropped at once, starts no rest, and the boxes carry the server's limits.** `src/programs/hooks/useLiveWorkout.ts` · `tick` (L265, `if (!res.ok) throw` queues a 400 as if offline; the screen starts the rest bar regardless at L394); `SetRow.tsx` inputs L125–144 have no min/max; `app/api/workouts/[id]/sets/route.ts` L18 answers every invalid body with the fixed "Could not save that set". Change: `tick` returns `"saved" | "queued" | "refused"`; a 4xx removes the optimistic set, sets `error` to the route's sentence, and the screen calls `startRest` only on `saved`/`queued`. `schemas.ts` exports `SET_LIMITS = { weightMax: MAX_WEIGHT_KG, repsMax: 1000, setMax: 50 }` and `CompleteSetSchema` is built from it **with a message per field** ("weight must be between 0 and 999.99", "reps must be a whole number between 0 and 1000"); the sets route returns `parsed.error.issues[0].message` the way the finish route already does (L21 there), and stays under 50 lines. `SetRow` puts the same numbers on `min`/`max`/`step` and refuses the tick client-side with the same sentence plus the unit label, so the limit lives in one place. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/useLiveWorkout.test.tsx — "a 400 drops the set immediately, reports refused, and queues nothing"; tests/unit/programs/setRow.test.tsx — "the weight and reps boxes carry exactly SET_LIMITS' numbers"; tests/unit/programs/schemas.test.ts — "CompleteSetSchema's first issue for weight 1000 is the sentence the row shows"; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "typing 1000 kg and ticking shows the refusal naming the weight, no green row, no rest bar".
6. **A blank weight never becomes 0 kg.** `SetRow.tsx` L151–152: ✓ is disabled only when reps are blank and `Number("")` sends 0. Change: `programsService.setEntryProblem({ weight, reps, bodyweight })` returns `null` or `"weight" | "reps"`; `SetRow` disables ✓ while it returns one and shows the reason as the placeholder; a bodyweight lift keeps the blank-weight path (0 on purpose). This is the one rule; the after-the-fact form that had the same bug is deleted by Phase 4. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — "an empty weight on a barbell lift is refused; an empty weight on a bodyweight lift is 0 on purpose"; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "an added Bench Press with 8 reps and no weight cannot be ticked".
7. **PREVIOUS is filled for every lift, from one source, and says so when it cannot be read.** `app/programs/live/page.tsx` L41–50 fills `lastTime` only inside `if (live.enrollmentId)` from that enrollment's own logs via `lastSetsPerLift` (programsService L1392, no other caller, no test); `src/db/workoutRepo.ts` · `liftHistory` (L584) has one caller (the lifts route), matches by name only, returns kilograms, discards its query error (a silent fallback), and its name already means something else in `src/health/healthService.ts` L319. Change: replace `workoutRepo.liftHistory` with `lastSetsForLifts(userId, lifts: { name; libraryId? }[], unit, sessions = 1)` — one bounded read (`.limit(100)` finished workouts, newest first, `.order("logged_at", { ascending: false }).order("id")`), **throw** on error, convert with `fromKg`. Matching rule, one place (`programsService.setMatchesLift(row, lift)`): a stored set matches when both have a `library_id` and they are equal, **or** when the lowercased trimmed names are equal (rows written by `logProgramSession` carry no `library_id` — `setRowsFor` L910 — so the name is the only key they have); a lift's sessions are the newest `sessions` workouts containing any matching set. `page.tsx` calls it once for prescribed + added + swapped-in lifts and passes `previous` and `previousUnavailable: boolean`; delete `lastSetsPerLift`. The screen renders an amber `text-xs` line "Could not read your past sets — the PREVIOUS column is empty for now" (testid `previous-unavailable`) instead of silently blank. PREVIOUS is a `button.h-11.text-left.text-xs.tabular-nums` that copies into the boxes; blank `span` when none, never "0"; beyond 100 workouts the sheet in step 10 says so. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — "setMatchesLift: a library match, a name match, and neither"; "pickLastSets takes the newest session only"; tests/integration/db/workoutSchema.integration.test.ts — "lastSetsForLifts converts to the lifter's unit, matches a program-logged set by name, and throws when the read fails"; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "after finishing Bench in a program, a loose workout that adds Bench shows PREVIOUS matching /\d+×\d+/, and a lift never done shows nothing and no 0".
8. **The lift menu: swap, move, warm-up, rest, last times, skip, remove.** `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` L318–337 renders one 26 px "Skip this one" chip and a read-only rest; `addedLiftId` lives in a component (`AddLift.tsx` L30). Change: move `addedLiftId(name)` to programsService (AddLift imports it) so it is the **one** derivation of an on-the-day lift's id — `adjustments.swapped[fromId]` keeps its existing shape `{ name, libraryId }` (no new field: the target's id is always `addedLiftId(name)`). `Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Options for Squat"` (`MoreVertical`) opens a `BottomSheet` (`data-testid="lift-menu"`, rows `min-h-11`): **Swap this lift…** (AddLift's search inline; writes the full `swapped` map; ticked sets stay under the old id and show collapsed "2 × 100 kg × 5 logged" above the new lift's empty rows; the new lift's rows tick under `addedLiftId(name)`; `AddLift.alreadyHere` includes swap targets); **Move up / Move down** (`adjustments.order` = full id list via `programsService.moveInOrder(ids, groups, id, ±1)` — a superset pair moves as one block and never splits; disabled at the ends; the screen sorts by `order` when present); **Add a warm-up set** (a `kind: "warmup"` row above set 1, both boxes blank, label "W"; warm-up rows are numbered among warm-ups — the database allows warm-up 1 beside working 1 — and tick with `kind: "warmup"`; the finish count and the volume already exclude warm-ups); **Rest after this lift** (step 2); **Last times for Squat** (step 10); **Skip this lift / I did do this one** (existing `skipped`); **Remove** (added and swapped-in lifts only, `Trash2`, `text-destructive`; on a swapped-in lift with no ticked sets it deletes the `swapped` entry and the original lift returns un-held; DESTRUCTIVE when the lift has ticked sets — see open question 6). The engine: `programsService.entriesFromSets` L1333 `held` = skipped ∪ incomplete ∪ **keys(swapped)**, so a swapped-out lift is held (`applyLog` L~627 treats a `skipped` entry as a hold) rather than scored as a miss, and its sets are kept (open question 4). *Test:* tests/unit/programs/engine.test.ts — "a swapped lift is held, not judged, and its ticked sets stay in the entry"; tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — "moveInOrder keeps every id, moves one, and moves a superset pair as a block"; "a swapped lift's new sets carry addedLiftId(name)"; tests/e2e/programs-live-menus.spec.ts — "Options for Squat lists Swap this lift, Move up, Move down, Add a warm-up set, Rest after this lift, Last times for Squat, Skip this lift, each ≥ 44 px, and Remove only after Add a lift"; "tick 2 Squat sets, swap to Front Squat: title Front Squat, '2 × … logged', workout_sets = 2, finish sheet lists Squat 2 of 5 as 'swapped for Front Squat — not counted' and does not list Front Squat"; "Add a warm-up set gives a first row labelled W with empty boxes, and ticking it does not change 'n of 5'".
9. **The set number is a tag: warm-up / working / drop set, effort, delete.** `SetRow.tsx` L95 is a plain `span`; `useLiveWorkout.tick` L383–391 never sends `rpe` (`CompleteSetSchema` L299 and `completeSet` L225 already accept it); `app/api/workouts/[id]/sets/[setId]/route.ts` has only DELETE; `LiveWorkoutScreen` L359 finds the ticked set by number and `kind !== "warmup"`. Change: **rows are slots.** `rowsFor(ex)` returns rows built from the lift's ticked sets (each with its own `kind` and `setNumber`) plus an empty row for every prescribed working slot not yet ticked plus the warm-up/extra rows revealed locally; the ticked lookup matches on `kind + setNumber + side` (the same slot rule `slotOf` uses in the hook), never on number alone. The label is derived from the kind: `W` for warm-up, `D` for drop, the slot number otherwise. Re-tagging working set 1 as a warm-up moves it to slot `warmup|1` and hands working slot 1 back to the program (the empty row returns; the finish count is honest). The number becomes `button.h-11.w-11.rounded-md.text-sm.tabular-nums aria-label="Set 1 type"` opening a `BottomSheet` with rows Warm-up · Working · Drop set, an "Effort (RPE)" row with `<Slider min={6} max={10} step={1}>` (whole numbers — `rpe` on `workout_sets` is SMALLINT 1–10, migration 20260907100000 L154/L166 — unless open question 3 asks for a migration), and "Delete this set" (`text-destructive`). No "Failure" (the CHECK at L159 has no such value; Decision 6). New `UpdateSetSchema { kind?, rpe? }` in `schemas.ts`, `PATCH` on that route (stays under 50 lines) → `workoutRepo.updateSet(userId, workoutId, setId, patch)`: `requireLive` first; if the new kind would land on a slot that already exists (`uq_workout_sets_slot`, migration L189) it refuses **before writing** with "Squat already has a warm-up set 1 — delete one of them first"; one UPDATE; returns the live workout; classified **asserted** in tests/support/writeCoverage.baseline.json with the integration test below (the ceiling `MAX_UNASSERTED` does not rise). For an unticked row the choice is held locally and travels with the tick (`QueuedSet` gains `rpe`; `kind` already there). *Test:* tests/integration/db/workoutSchema.integration.test.ts — "updateSet changes set_kind and rpe on that row and nothing else, refuses a kind that collides with an existing slot, and is refused once the workout is finished"; tests/unit/programs/useLiveWorkout.test.tsx — "an effort chosen before the tick travels with the tick"; tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — "rowsFor matches ticked sets by slot: a warm-up 1 and a working 1 are two rows, and re-tagging working 1 as warm-up gives working slot 1 back"; tests/e2e/programs-live-menus.spec.ts — "the set menu on set 1 lists Warm-up, Working, Drop set, Delete this set and a [role=slider]; choosing Warm-up shows W and /api/workouts/live reads set_kind warmup; no row reads Failure".
10. **The lift history sheet, from the lift's name or "Last times".** `src/programs/components/LiftHistory.tsx` renders a whole-account list; `app/api/workouts/lifts/route.ts` L17 returns kilograms via the function step 7 replaces. Change: new `src/programs/components/live/LiftHistorySheet.tsx` — `BottomSheet` fed by `useLoad("/api/workouts/lifts?exercise=…&libraryId=…&unit=kg", parse)`; the route gains `unit` and `libraryId` and returns `{ unit, sessions: [{ at, sets: [{ weight, reps, kind }] }] }` from `lastSetsForLifts(userId, [lift], unit, 5)` (step 7 — one read, one matching rule); rows `min-h-11` "Fri 12 Sep · 5 × 100 kg × 5" (date in the account's timezone, passed as a prop) and `text-xs muted` "Best: 105 kg × 5"; states loading (3 × `h-11 animate-pulse bg-muted/40`), failed (amber "Could not load your past sets."), empty ("Nothing logged for this lift in your last 100 workouts."), ready. The lift name is `button.min-h-11.text-left.text-base.font-semibold.truncate` → this sheet. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/liftHistorySheet.test.tsx — "renders loading, failed, empty and ready from the four responses, never a 0 kg row, and the date in the timezone it was given"; tests/e2e/programs-live-menus.spec.ts — "after one finished session, tapping Squat opens a sheet whose first row reads 5 × <weight> kg × 5".
11. **Swipe to delete, hover to delete, and the sheet row — three doors, one action.** `SetRow.tsx` has no delete except un-ticking. Change: touch drag ≤ −88 px reveals a `w-22 bg-destructive text-white text-sm` "Delete" zone (44 px); pointer devices get a `group-hover` `Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm"` `Trash2`; the set menu's "Delete this set" does the same. Ticked → `live.removeSet` (DELETE, exists L279); an unticked row → local hide (`hiddenRows` beside `extraRows`, keyed by slot; the finish count still uses the prescription — open question 5). *Test:* tests/unit/programs/setRow.test.tsx — "a swipe past −88 px reveals Delete; a shorter one snaps back"; tests/e2e/programs-live-menus.spec.ts (touch context) — "swiping set 3 −100 px reveals Delete; tapping it reduces set-row count by 1 and issues a DELETE for a ticked set and none for an untouched extra row".

Change: `programsService.saveStateFor({ pendingSince, queued }, now)` → `"saved" | "saving" | "slow" | "queued"`; the row shows nothing before 1,500 ms, "Saving…" after, "Not saved — no signal. It will retry." once queued or after 10 s, and never hides it until the server acks; the hook exposes `pendingSince` per slot (the `at` already on each `QueuedSet`) and the queued slots. Footer "2 not saved yet" only when > 0 (exists). *Test:* tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — "no text before 1.5 s, Saving… after, Not saved once queued"; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "with a normal connection 'Saving' and 'Not saved' are never attached to the DOM (poll 400 ms); with the sets route delayed 3 s 'Saving…' appears after ≥ 1,400 ms"; tests/e2e/programs-offline.spec.ts L58 assertion changes from /not saved yet/ to /Not saved — no signal/.
13. **The finish sheet lists prescribed lifts only, words each miss by the program's own rule, and offers to drop an untouched added lift.** `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` L131–139 computes `unfinished` over `exercises` including added lifts with three invented sets; `FinishSheet.tsx` L174 hard-codes "will bring the weight down". Change: `programsService.finishOutlook(prescription, adjustments, sets, missRules)` → `{ unfinished: [{ exerciseId, name, done, asked, outcome: "hold" | "deload" | "unknown" | "held", failsSoFar, deloadAfter, deloadPct, swappedFor? }], addedUntouched: [{ exerciseId, name }] }` where `unfinished` is prescribed lifts (`asked > 0`, not in `added`, not a swap target) with `done < asked` — a swapped-out or skipped lift is listed with `outcome: "held"` and no "not a miss" link; `missRulesFor(program, enrollment)` (pure; from the lift's linear rule `deloadAfterFails` / `deloadPct` — `types.ts` L80–81, optional at L115 — and `exerciseState[id].consecutiveFails`) is computed in `page.tsx` and passed down. Copy per lift: "counts as miss 1 of 3 — the weight stays" / "third miss — the weight drops 10 %" / "counts as a miss" when the program has no linear rule / "swapped for Front Squat — not counted" / "skipped — not counted"; added lifts read "Front Squat — added, nothing ticked · drop it" where "drop it" sends the full `added` array minus that lift (never "0 of 3"). *Test:* tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — "an added, unticked lift is never listed as a miss"; "a swapped-out lift is listed as held, not as a miss"; "StrongLifts with 0 prior fails reads 'the weight stays'; with 2 prior fails reads 'the weight drops'"; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "Add a lift, do nothing, Finish: the amber box does not contain the added lift; 'drop it' removes it".
14. **Started and Ended are both editable, in the account's timezone, with the ten-hour guard on the span; a stale workout opens with the row expanded.** `FinishSheet.tsx` L79–86 edits `endedAt` only and `toLocalInput` (L53) uses the device clock; `FinishWorkoutSchema` (L323) has no `startedAt`; `finish_program_workout` (migration 20260907110000 L27–40) takes no start, no distance, no session type. Change: migration `supabase/migrations/20260917100000_finish_workout_started_at.sql` — **first `DROP FUNCTION finish_program_workout(UUID, TIMESTAMPTZ, INTEGER, SMALLINT, SMALLINT, TEXT, JSONB, JSONB, JSONB, INTEGER)`** (a `CREATE OR REPLACE` with a different parameter list creates a second overload beside the old one, and PostgREST then refuses every call as ambiguous), then `CREATE FUNCTION finish_program_workout(... the ten existing parameters ..., p_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NULL, p_distance_km NUMERIC DEFAULT NULL, p_session_type TEXT DEFAULT NULL)` with the same body plus `started_at = COALESCE(p_started_at, started_at)`, `logged_at = COALESCE(p_started_at, logged_at)` (a workout belongs to the day it started — the same rule `startWorkout` L156–158 writes), `distance_km = COALESCE(p_distance_km, distance_km)`, `session_type = COALESCE(p_session_type, session_type)`, and the `REVOKE … FROM PUBLIC` / `GRANT EXECUTE … TO authenticated` lines re-issued for the new signature (the old ones at L92–97 name the old one). `FinishWorkoutSchema` gains `startedAt` (ISO) and `distanceKm` (0–500) and **loses `sessionType`** (step 15: the server decides it). `finishWorkout` (L326) refuses `ended < started` (exists, L360) and a span > 599 min and a `startedAt` later than the earliest ticked set's `completedAt` ("Started cannot be after your first set at 18:07"), each with the plain sentence, and passes all three new parameters to the RPC. `src/shared/dateUtils.ts` gains `instantToLocalInput(iso, timezone)` (keeps `toLocalInput`'s round-up-to-the-minute rule, L49–51) / `localInputToInstant(value, timezone)` (splits on `T` into `localTimeInstant(dateISO, timeHHMM, timezone)`, L316); the sheet uses them with the `timezone` prop, so the header, the sentence "18:02 → 18:47 · 45 min" and the editors all speak the account's clock. "Change" reveals Started and Ended `Input type=datetime-local h-11`; a stale workout (`isStaleWorkout`, step 16) opens with them expanded. Applying the migration: `supabase migration list --linked` first — run today, it lists everything through 20260914120000 as applied, the untracked file included, so `supabase db push --linked` is safe if that is still true; if something else is pending, apply this single file only. *Test:* tests/unit/shared/dateUtils.test.ts — "a Copenhagen 18:02 round-trips through the input across the October DST change, and 18:02:37 rounds up to 18:03"; tests/integration/db/workoutSchema.integration.test.ts — "finishing with startedAt moves started_at and logged_at together; a 10-hour span and a start after the first set are refused; the old ten-parameter signature no longer exists"; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "Change reveals two datetime-local inputs labelled Started and Ended; Started −60 min reads '· 60 min'; a 10-hour span disables Save with the too-long sentence".
15. **A run done live is a run: tickable blocks, distance at the finish, the right session type — one mapping.** `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` L237–260 renders the endurance plan read-only ("Nothing to tick off here"); `FinishSheet.tsx` L36–42 has no distance; `workoutRepo.startWorkout` L161 writes `session_type: "weights"` for every program; `finishWorkout` L408–413 patches `session_type` after the transaction only when a caller sends one (none does) and throws the error away; `programRepo.sessionTypeFor` (L901) is private. Change: export `sessionTypeFor(program)` from `programsService` (moved from programRepo; `logProgramSession` L870 imports it); `startWorkout` L161 writes `program ? sessionTypeFor(program) : "weights"` (a loose workout has no program and is a gym session — if Phase 0 has already made this exact edit the step is a no-op there, and the same test proves it either way); `finishWorkout` passes the same mapping as `p_session_type` (step 14) and **deletes L408–413**. The plan card becomes `<Card data-testid="endurance-plan" className="gap-0 py-0">` with one `h-11 w-11` tick per **row of `prescription.enduranceSets`** (the repeat group as drawn today, not each inner block) writing `adjustments.blocksDone: number[]` (full list of ticked indexes; schema + type) through `adjust()`; the finish sheet shows "Distance" (`Input inputMode=decimal h-11`, unit per open question 2, converted in one place `programsService.distanceToKm`) when `prescription.enduranceSets` is set, and "3 of 5 blocks" with no miss wording. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/engine.test.ts — "sessionTypeFor: couch-to-5k → running, triathlon → cardio, splits mobility → mobility, StrongLifts → weights"; tests/integration/db/workoutSchema.integration.test.ts — "a running-program workout is running from start to finish and stores distance_km in one transaction"; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "Couch to 5K: the plan card has one tick per row; Finish asks Distance; after Save /api/health/workout returns session_type running with the distance".
16. **The header tells the truth: mm:ss, the week's own name, the loose date, "since Mon 18:02", and where back goes.** `app/programs/live/page.tsx` L46 `getProgram(prescription.programId)?.name` ("Your own program" for a self-built week); `Elapsed` L514/528 prints "N min" in `text-xs`; L522 `BackLink className="shrink-0 text-xs"` with no height; L178 hard-codes "Back to training". Change: `workoutRepo.prescriptionForDay` (L618) additionally returns `enrollment: enr` (it already fetched it); `page.tsx` takes `searchParams: Promise<{ from?: string }>` (the pattern at `app/life-mastery/page.tsx` L29), uses `enrollmentName(enrollment)` (`src/programs/data/catalog.ts` L108, takes `{ program_id, label }`), reads `readReturn(from)` (`src/shared/returnTo.ts` L28) and passes `timezone` (from `prescriptionForDay`, or `getUserTimezone(auth.userId)` for a loose workout), `stale` (`programsService.isStaleWorkout(startedAt, now)` extracted from `trainingCardState` L1937 so the door and the screen agree — `trainingCardState` calls it), and `returnLabel` (`ROUTE_LABELS[from]` from `components/navTabs.ts` L48, else "Training"). Header: `BackLink text-sm min-h-11` (it already honours `?from` itself, `components/BackLink.tsx` L47–52); title `text-base font-semibold truncate` "Workout A · StrongLifts 5×5" / "Push · Winter block" / "Workout · 12 Sep" (loose, account timezone); elapsed `text-sm tabular-nums` as `mm:ss`, `h:mm:ss` past an hour (`programsService.formatElapsed(seconds)`); stale → "since Mon 18:02" plus an amber line "This workout was started on Monday. Finish it with the right times, or discard it."; the summary's button reads "Back to Tracking" / "Back to Training" from `returnLabel`. *Test:* tests/unit/programs/enrollmentName.test.ts — "the live header names a labelled enrollment by its label"; tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — "formatElapsed: 1421 s is 23:41 and 3 661 s is 1:01:01; isStaleWorkout is true at 6 h 1 min and false at 5 h 59 min"; tests/unit/programs/trainingCardState.test.ts — the existing "calls a workout left open for hours forgotten" and "is still running right up to the cutoff" stay green through `isStaleWorkout`; tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts — "started via ?from=/dashboard/tracking the BackLink reads Tracking and the summary button Back to Tracking"; tests/e2e/programs-live-menus.spec.ts — "a workout whose started_at is 7 h old shows 'since' in the header and opens Finish with Started and Ended expanded".
17. **Every control is 44 px, only the caption row is 11 px, and the kit's parts are the app's own.** `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx` L329 (`px-2 py-1 text-xs` chip), L413/L426 (`min-h-9`), L348 caption grid `1.75rem` first column, L255/L435 `text-[11px]`; `SetRow.tsx` L114 (11 px PREVIOUS with no height), L91 grid; `RestBar.tsx` L125–148 hand-rolled buttons, L155 `text-[11px]`; `FinishSheet.tsx` intensity cells `w-11` (L269). week-by-week plans show "This plan cannot be edited" instead). `programsService.keepableChanges(prescription, adjustments, sets, unit)` is the one rule for what can be kept: a swapped-in or added lift is kept only when it has a `libraryId` **and** at least one ticked working set (its starting weight = the heaviest ticked working set, in the enrollment's unit); anything else is listed under the switch as "Front Squat stays a one-off — no set was ticked" / "— not in the lift list"; `order` and `rest` are always keepable. `FinishWorkoutSchema.keepChanges?: boolean`; `finishWorkout` calls `customize.applyAdjustmentsToSchedule(schedule, adjustments, keepable)` (pure: `swapExercise` L352 / `addExercise` L314 with `loadExerciseFromLibrary`, `moveExercise` L379 for `order`, `restSec` for `rest`; returns `{ schedule, workingWeights }`) then `programRepo.updateEnrollmentSchedule(userId, enrollmentId, schedule, workingWeights)` (L512, exists; it re-reads the enrollment, so it must run **after** the RPC has committed and sees the advanced cursor and state); if that second write fails the finish stands and the summary carries `scheduleNotKept: true` → amber "Saved, but the program could not be changed for next time." *Test:* tests/unit/programs/customize.test.ts — "applyAdjustmentsToSchedule swaps, appends with the ticked weight, reorders and sets rest, and leaves every other day untouched";
