### Deleted
The plain-div lift card; the inline two-chip menu; the read-only "Rest 3 min (our suggestion)"; `MoreHorizontal`; the "LAST" caption and the 1.75rem number column; "+ one more set" / "one fewer"; `window.confirm`; the immediate `unsaved` render on a `pending:` id; "N min" elapsed; added lifts counted as misses; "Your own program" as a header fallback; the Ended-only editor; the summary's own markup; "Back to training" as a fixed label; hand-rolled −30s/+30s/✕; every `text-[11px]` except the caption row; `workoutRepo.liftHistory` and `programsService.lastSetsPerLift` (both replaced by `lastSetsForLifts`); `programRepo.sessionTypeFor` (moved); the post-transaction `session_type` update in `finishWorkout` L408–413 and `sessionType` on `FinishWorkoutSchema`; the old ten-parameter `finish_program_workout`; the dead `rpe` parameter on `FinishSheet.onFinish` (open question 3).

### Acceptance
- tests/unit/programs/liveRest.test.ts — the author's rest reaches the screen, an edited rest wins, a warm-up rests 45 s, `withRest` keeps the other lifts (live-workout-03, steps 1–2).
- tests/unit/programs/schemas.test.ts — `rest`, `blocksDone`, `startedAt`, `distanceKm`, `keepChanges`, `UpdateSetSchema`, `SET_LIMITS` parity and its sentences; `sessionType` is no longer accepted.
- tests/unit/programs/useLiveWorkout.test.tsx — rest survives reload and is forgotten on finish/discard; a 400 is refused at once; RPE travels with the tick.
- tests/unit/programs/liveScreen.test.ts — A1/A2 ordinals; blank weight rule; set matching and newest-session rule; rows-are-slots; save-state timing; finish outlook (no added-lift miss, held for swapped, hold vs deload copy); keepable changes; elapsed and stale.
- tests/unit/programs/engine.test.ts — swapped lift held not judged; `sessionTypeFor` mapping.
- tests/unit/programs/customize.test.ts — `applyAdjustmentsToSchedule`.
- tests/unit/programs/setRow.test.tsx, liftHistorySheet.test.tsx, liveTouchTargets.test.ts, tests/unit/shared/dateUtils.test.ts — as named per step.
- tests/integration/db/workoutSchema.integration.test.ts — `updateSet` (incl. slot collision), `lastSetsForLifts`, finish with `startedAt`/`distanceKm`/`session_type` in one transaction, start-after-first-set refused, old RPC signature gone, keepChanges after the cursor advanced.
- tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts and tests/e2e/programs-live-menus.spec.ts (`training` project) — the S3 acceptance list: exactly one rgb(255,107,53) element before finishing ("Finish", ≥ 44 px), one on the finish sheet, one on the summary; caption reads SET, PREVIOUS, KG, REPS and "LAST" is absent; every set row's targets ≥ 44 px and grid starts "44px 72px"; PREVIOUS filled after a prior session and empty with no "0" on a lift never done; the lift menu rows, warm-up row, rest 3:30 after two fast taps and one PATCH and a reload, swap keeps two logged sets and lists Squat as "not counted", set menu rows and no "Failure", swipe delete, no "Saving" on a normal connection, superset card, Started/Ended editors and the ten-hour guard, Keep-these-changes switch, Discard dialog with zero native dialogs, summary equals the receipt page, `?from` labels, a 7-hour-old workout opens with times expanded, no text < 11 px and only the caption row at 11 px, scrollHeight ≤ 1,700 px for 3 × 5, RestBar ≤ 96 px + safe area, `[data-testid=mobile-tab-bar]` absent on /programs/live.
- Guards that must stay green, allowlists may shrink and never grow: tests/unit/architecture.test.ts (API routes ≤ 50 lines — the PATCH route and the sets route stay under; the unpaged-read allowance — `lastSetsForLifts` carries `.limit(100)` so `src/db/workoutRepo.ts`'s count does not rise; the icon registry), tests/unit/navigation/routeReachability.test.ts, tests/unit/navigation/backNavigation.test.ts (the live screen keeps `BackLink`), tests/unit/writeCoverage.test.ts (`updateSet` is classified "asserted"; `MAX_UNASSERTED` = 136 does not rise), tests/unit/programs/trainingCardState.test.ts (`isStaleWorkout` is the same rule the door uses). The "no `text-[10px]`" floor is **not** in architecture.test.ts today — it lives in the new liveTouchTargets test for this screen.
- No blockers.

### Attack log
- **Migration would have broken every finish.** Step 14 wrote `CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION finish_program_workout(...)` with three extra defaulted parameters; in Postgres that creates a second overload beside the ten-parameter one (checked: `supabase/migrations/20260907110000_finish_workout.sql` L27–40 and the REVOKE/GRANT at L92–97 name the old signature), and a `supabase.rpc` call would then match both and be refused as ambiguous. Fixed: `DROP FUNCTION` of the old signature first, re-issued REVOKE/GRANT, and an integration test that the old signature no longer exists.
- **Session type was a second, unchecked write.** data-integrity-04's own fix says session_type must be inside the transaction and the unchecked update at `workoutRepo.ts` L408–413 deleted; the draft left it as a vague "inside the same call". Fixed: `p_session_type` on the RPC, the L408–413 update deleted, `sessionType` removed from `FinishWorkoutSchema` (the client never decides it), `startWorkout` L161 uses the same export, with a no-op clause if Phase 0 already made that edit.
- **"Keep these changes" would have failed on every added lift.** `customize.seedForAddedExercises` (L555–583, opened) throws "was added to the program but has no starting weight"; the draft appended added lifts "with @bw / no weight", so the second write would fail every time and the amber "could not be changed" line would be the normal outcome. Also `swapExercise`/`addExercise` (L352, L314) need a `LibraryExercise`, so a free-text lift cannot be kept. Fixed: `keepableChanges` is the one rule (libraryId + a ticked working set → kept with that weight; else listed as a one-off with the reason), `applyAdjustmentsToSchedule` returns the `workingWeights` too, and an integration test proves the write lands after the cursor advanced.
- **Two rapid rest taps would have saved 3:15.** `adjustWorkout` (L298) merges one key deep and the screen's `adjustments` only refresh on the reply, so two `adjust({ rest })` calls built from the same base lose the second tap — the draft's own e2e ("+15 s twice reads 3:30") would fail intermittently. Fixed: local draft in the sheet, one write on close/600 ms, `withRest` builds the full map; the e2e now asserts exactly one PATCH. Added a phase-wide note that every `adjust()` sends the whole key.
- **The refusal sentence could not exist.** Step 5 promised "weight must be 0–999.99" but `app/api/workouts/[id]/sets/route.ts` L18 returns a fixed "Could not save that set" for any 400. Fixed: schema messages per field, the route returns the first issue like the finish route (L21) does, and a schema test pins the sentence the row shows.
- **Re-tagging a set could collide with the unique slot index.** Changing `set_kind` changes the slot (`uq_workout_sets_slot`, migration 20260907100000 L189); "working 1 → warm-up" with a warm-up 1 already there would surface as a raw Postgres error. Also the screen's ticked lookup (L359) matches on number alone and would show a warm-up and a working set 1 as one row. Fixed: `updateSet` checks the target slot before writing and refuses in plain words (tested); rows are slots, labels derived from kind, and the re-tag rule ("working slot 1 is handed back") is stated and unit-tested so the executor does not guess.
- **`liftHistory` already means something else.** `src/health/healthService.ts` L319 exports a `liftHistory` (finished-work rule: "does this name already mean something else here?"). The rename to `lastSetsForLifts` is now mandatory, and `lastSetsPerLift` (programsService L1392) — its only caller is the line the step deletes, no test names it — is deleted rather than left dead. The matching rule was two sentences that disagreed ("library first then name" vs "prefers library over name"); it is now one function with three tests, and notes that program-logged rows have no `library_id` (`setRowsFor` has none), so name is not optional.
- **`swapped.exerciseId` was a second copy of a derived fact.** The target id is always `addedLiftId(name)`; storing it as well is two representations. Fixed: `addedLiftId` moves to programsService as the one derivation, the schema is untouched.
- **The swap e2e contradicted the spec's "never a miss".** The draft's test wanted the finish sheet to list "Squat 2 of 5" while the same step held the lift; spec S3 (c) says swapped is never a miss and (e) says Squat is listed. Fixed: `finishOutlook` lists it with `outcome: "held"` and the copy "swapped for Front Squat — not counted", no "not a miss" link.
- **Move up/down could split a superset pair.** No rule was given; the executor would have to guess. Fixed: `moveInOrder` moves a group as one block, tested.
- **Warm-up rest was silently dropped.** `restSecondsFor` (L296) already takes `{ warmup }` = 45 s; the draft's `restTargetFor` ignored it, so a warm-up tick would rest 3:00. Fixed: `opts.warmup` passes through, tested.
- **`prescriptionForDay` does not return the enrollment.** Step 16 said it "returns the enrollment it already fetched" — it does not (L618–645, opened); `enrollmentName` needs `{ program_id, label }`. Fixed: the return-shape change is named as part of the step; `searchParams` shape is named with the repo's existing pattern; `BackLink` already reads `?from` itself (L47–52) so only the summary label needs the prop.
- **A Started time after the first ticked set could be written.** Nothing refused it (finishWorkout L360 only guards `ended < started`). Fixed: server guard with a plain sentence, in the integration test.
- **Elapsed past an hour was undefined.** "mm:ss" for a 75-minute session is "75:00" or "1:15:00" — a guess. Fixed: `formatElapsed` with the h:mm:ss rule and a test at 3 661 s.
- **Endurance "block" was ambiguous.** `enduranceSets` rows each hold `repeat` and inner `blocks`; "one tick per block" could mean either. Fixed: one tick per row, as drawn today.
- **A guard that does not exist was cited.** The acceptance list claimed architecture.test.ts checks "no `text-[10px]`"; grep of `tests/` finds no such check. Fixed: the floor is the new liveTouchTargets test, and the guard list says so.
- **Phase 7 / Phase 4 / Phase 8 seams were implicit.** tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts L189 expects "New best" on a first-ever set (the thing walk-05 calls wrong) — Phase 7's change, not this phase's; the History row wording for a run (HistoryTab L388/L480, half of critic-live-run) is Phase 7's; the header can only show a self-built week's name once Phase 8 sets the `label` at start (lm-build-own-06). All three now stated in "Relies on" and the opening sentence no longer over-promises the header.
- **`lastSetsPerLift`'s deletion and `hiddenRows`' key were unspecified**; both stated (delete; key by slot).
- **Verified, not inferred:** every file and line number in the steps was opened; `supabase migration list --linked` was run (read-only) and shows everything through 20260914120000 applied, so the draft's claim about the untracked migration holds; `writeCoverage.baseline.json` L110–117 and `MAX_UNASSERTED = 136` were read; `components/ui/{dialog,slider,switch}.tsx` exist; `Trash2`/`Check`/`MoreVertical` are on the utility list; `enrollmentName.test.ts` has no test yet for the live header; RLS: `workout_sets` has an own-row UPDATE policy (20260305 L117), so the PATCH route needs no policy change and nothing here is ASK FIRST.

### Open questions for Phase 6

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** The rest bar uses the same stopwatch icon (Timer) as the Time tab in the bottom bar. May the icon registry gain the role "rest countdown" for Timer, or would you rather a different glyph (Hourglass) so the rest bar never looks like a link to time tracking? (Decision 8 in the design spec.)

*Recommendation:* Approve the extra role for Timer; it is already used that way and a second glyph adds nothing.

**Open question 2.** When a run is finished live, what unit should the Distance box use for an account that trains in pounds? The database stores kilometres.

*Recommendation:* Show miles for pounds accounts and kilometres for kilo accounts, converting to kilometres in one place (programsService.distanceToKm); a pounds lifter thinking in miles should not have to convert.

**Open question 3.** Finding live-workout-09 asks for a whole-workout effort (RPE 1–10) row on the finish sheet; the agreed design keeps only the 1–5 "How hard was it?" row and puts effort per set behind the set number. Two effort scales on one sheet or one? And per-set effort: whole numbers 6–10 (what the database allows today) or halves (needs a migration to change the column)?

*Recommendation:* One: per-set effort in the set menu, the 1–5 row for the workout. Remove the dead rpe parameter from the finish sheet rather than adding a second scale. Whole numbers 6–10, no migration.

**Open question 4.** When you swap a lift because the rack is taken, how should the program treat the lift you did not do? Today it would be scored as a miss (a step towards a weight drop).

*Recommendation:* Hold it — no miss counted, the weight stays, the sets you did before swapping stay in the record and the finish sheet lists it as 'swapped for Front Squat — not counted'. The alternative (judging the replacement lift's sets against the original's numbers) compares two different exercises.

**Open question 5.** Swiping away a set the program prescribed but you never ticked: should the row simply hide on this phone (the finish sheet still says "4 of 5"), or should it be impossible to delete a prescribed row?

*Recommendation:* Hide it locally; the finish sheet keeps counting the prescription so nothing is hidden from the program.

**Open question 6.** DESTRUCTIVE — "Remove" on a lift you added or swapped in mid-workout: if you already ticked sets under it, should Remove delete those sets too (after a confirmation dialog), or be unavailable until the sets are un-ticked?

*Recommendation:* Delete them after a dialog that names the count ("Remove Front Squat and its 2 sets?"); removing a lift and keeping invisible sets in the totals would be the worse lie.

**Open question 7.** "Keep these changes for next time" can only keep a swapped-in or added lift that is in the lift list and had at least one set ticked (the program needs a starting weight, and a lift typed by hand has no library entry). Is it acceptable that such a lift is listed under the switch as "stays a one-off" with the reason, rather than being kept with a guessed weight?

*Recommendation:* Yes — a guessed starting weight is exactly the invented number the rest of this plan removes; the reason is shown, and the person can add the lift properly from the Programs screen.


### Blockers for Phase 6

No blockers.


## Phase 7 — History and Progress you can trust

**What you can do after:** Open History and see every workout you have ever logged, a month at a time, with totals that mean what they say — filter to one lift and the numbers are that lift's only, and the list tells you when it has reached the beginning. Tap a workout and it opens as its own page (the receipt Phase 4 builds), where "Correct this" and "Delete" live; the editor lets you add the set you forgot, fix a mistyped lift, mark a warm-up, and it refuses to save a blank box as zero. Open Progress and "Your bests" is your best ever, not your best this year; the weekly chart, the History totals and the finish screen's total are one number computed by one rule; the month under each bar is the right month wherever you live; and if Progress cannot load, it says so instead of showing an empty year. Deleting a program session tells you first that the program's weights will be recomputed, then confirms it happened — and a correction or delete either fully happens or does not happen at all. Switching tabs no longer re-downloads a year of sets.

**Why it is in this order:** It needs Phase 1's safe recalculation (deleting a session on an edited program must not crash the replay), Phase 4's receipt page `/programs/workout/[id]` (History rows open it; the editor and Delete are mounted on it), Phase 5's shadcn Tabs with `?tab=` on `/programs` (this phase's tab-keeping step and its "Back to History" link rely on both), and Phase 6's finish summary consumes the one definition of a personal best this phase creates. Note for the executor: "Phase N" here is this plan's numbering, not the numbering in `docs/plans/training-rebuild.md`.

**Findings this phase closes:** history-progress-01, history-progress-02, history-progress-03, history-progress-04, history-progress-05, history-progress-06, history-progress-07, history-progress-08, history-progress-09, history-progress-10, history-progress-11, history-progress-12, critic-three-definitions-of-a-personal-best, walk-05-new-best-on-first-set, after-the-fact-logging-05, tests-and-ci-09, design-system-07

**Not fixed here, on purpose (owned elsewhere, named so nothing is postponed silently):**
- data-integrity-03 (replay against the schedule that was in force) — Phase 1. Step 7 below runs *after* that replay is safe; until Phase 1 lands, the atomic delete/revise still refuses cleanly with the engine's own message instead of half-applying, which is the improvement this phase owns.
- data-integrity-08 — the `programRepo.sessionLogsFor` half is Phase 1's. Step 12 does the `healthRepo` half (three lifetime cardio reads) because that file is this phase's.
- The Today-tab and WorkoutLogger halves of design-system-07 — Phase 5 (Today cards) and Phase 4 / spec Decision 3 (WorkoutLogger is deleted). This phase makes History and Progress one style with the spec's primitives; the finding is listed here because this is the phase that touches both of those tabs.

### Plain-language decisions baked into the steps
- **Whose clock.** Every "which day / which week / which month was this" is decided on the server in the account's timezone (`profiles.timezone` via `settingsRepo.getUserTimezone`), never from the phone's clock and never from `new Date()` on the server used as a *day*. `new Date()` is only ever the *instant* "now", and the zoned day is derived from it. The browser only *formats* instants it is handed, with that timezone passed in. Two existing owners are reused, not re-invented: `getTodayInTimezone(tz, instant)` (`src/shared/dateUtils.ts:23`) for the day, and `periodStartFor("weekly", toZonedDate(instant, tz))` (`dateUtils.ts`, the one implementation of a Monday boundary) for the week. This makes the week squares on Progress agree with the week strip on Today (Phase 5's `trainingCardState`, same source). Consequence to know: an account that never opened Settings still says UTC until critic-today-is-utc-until-you-find-settings is closed — open question 3.
- **One rule for "weight moved".** Working sets only, timed lifts excluded — in History totals, the row summary, the weekly chart, and the finish summary. A guard test fails if any file outside `healthService` multiplies a weight by reps.
- **One definition of a personal best.** All-time, working sets, heavier-then-more-reps, matched by the library id first and the name second (the rule `getExerciseMax` already documents: a name is the wrong key), computed on the server from bounded reads. The finish sheet, Progress and (only if spec Decision 3 is answered "keep") the past-workout form all read it. A lift with no earlier finished set is "first time logged", never a best.
- **One Epley.** `estimateOneRepMax` (`programsService.ts:311`) stays the formula; the "above ten reps, do not estimate" cap gets one home and both the Squat-1RM tile and "est. max" on Progress use it.
- **History is loaded a month at a time, oldest on demand.** A month is always fetched whole, so its header total is exact by construction; "Show more" asks the server for older months; the end of the list says "That is everything" because the server probed for anything older and found nothing.
- **History rows open the workout's receipt page.** This is spec S2 item 20 (`rows min-h-14 → /programs/workout/[id]`, no bin on rows), not a choice this phase makes. "Correct this" and "Delete" therefore live on the receipt, where you can see what you are about to change.
- **Screen states.** Every list and section is loading (92 px pulse, spec pattern) / failed (amber `AlertTriangle` line + outline Try again, `*-unavailable` testid) / empty / ready. A failed read is never rendered as "nothing logged".

### Steps

1. **One rule for weight moved, and one place that decides if a lift is timed.** `src/programs/data/exerciseLibrary.ts` · new export `isTimedLift(set: { exercise: string; library_id?: string | null }): boolean` — `library_id` first (`LIBRARY_BY_ID`, line 487), then `libraryByName` (line 550) — replacing the private `isTimedLift(name)` at `src/health/healthService.ts:755`, which becomes an import. `src/health/healthService.ts` · new exports `setVolumeKg(set: { weight_kg; reps; set_kind?; exercise; library_id? })` (0 for a non-working set or a timed lift, else `weight_kg × reps`) and `workingVolumeKg(sets)`. Today: HistoryTab.tsx:255 and :319 sum `weight_kg * reps` over `isWorkingSet` only; `weeklyVolume` (healthService.ts:793–794) also excludes timed lifts; `finishWorkout` (workoutRepo.ts:414–417) sums `weightKg * reps` under a comment claiming timed work is excluded when it is not. Change: all four call `workingVolumeKg`; `finishWorkout` passes `live.sets.map(toStored)` (the mapper it already uses at :372, whose output has `weight_kg`/`set_kind`); `weeklyVolume` keeps its signature apart from step 3's `timezone`. *Test:* `tests/unit/health/volumeRule.test.ts` — "a timed lift adds no weight-moved, in the week chart, the history total and the finish summary alike" (one fixture: Farmer Carry 40 kg × 30 s × 3 plus Squat 100 × 5 × 5 → `weeklyVolume`, `workingVolumeKg` and the value `finishWorkout` returns from a fake database all say 2,500) and "no file outside healthService multiplies a weight by reps" (greps `src/**` for `/weight(_kg|Kg)\s*\*\s*[\w.]*reps/`; the only hit allowed is inside `setVolumeKg`).

2. **One way to say what a logged set was.** `src/programs/programsService.ts` · new export `describeLoggedSet({ exercise, weightKg, reps, count?, libraryId? }, unit: UnitSystem): string` (the `CollapsedSet` shape from `collapseSets`, healthService.ts:881, plus the library id). Today: HistoryTab.tsx:386 prints `${show(kg)}×${reps}` ("Pull-up 0×12"), :506 prints `0 kg × 12`; ProgressTab.tsx:234 has its own `bodyweight` branch. Change: bodyweight (0 kg, not timed) → "12 reps"; timed → "40 kg × 30 s" / "60 s" when bodyweight; loaded → "100 kg × 5" in the lifter's unit via `fromKg` + `formatLoad`; `count` > 1 prefixes "3 × ". HistoryTab (summary line and month header), ProgressTab (bests row), and — dependency — Phase 4's `WorkoutReceipt` use it; the three inline formatters are deleted. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/describeLoggedSet.test.ts` — "a bodyweight set reads as reps, a timed set as seconds, a loaded set as weight × reps, in the lifter's unit" (kg and lb cases; asserts "0×12" and "0 kg" cannot be produced).

3. **Day, week and month keys follow the account's timezone.** `src/health/healthService.ts` · `adherenceThisWeek`, `weeklyVolume`, `liftBests`, `liftHistory`, `liftsWithHistory`, `workoutsToCsv`. Today: each buckets on `localDateKey(new Date(log.logged_at))` (`localDateKey` at :282, `mondayOf` at :293 on the same machine-local Date), i.e. the machine's zone — on the server that is UTC. Change: each gains a required `timezone: string` parameter (`liftsWithHistory(sets, timezone, minDays = 2)`; `workoutsToCsv(logs, timezone)`); day keys come from `getTodayInTimezone(timezone, new Date(log.logged_at))`, week keys from `periodStartFor("weekly", toZonedDate(new Date(log.logged_at), timezone))`; `adherenceThisWeek(logs, plannedPerWeek, now: Date, timezone)` and `weeklyVolume(logs, now: Date, weeks, timezone)` take `now` as an *instant* from the caller (the route passes `new Date()`) and build "today" and the seven days from `toZonedDate(now, timezone)`. `localDateKey` and `mondayOf` keep exactly three callers — `buildWorkoutHeatmapWeeks`, `computeWeekStreak`, `workoutsOnDate` — which are not this phase's screens and are the remaining debt. The existing cases in `tests/unit/health/progressReads.test.ts` and `tests/unit/health/healthService.test.ts` (`liftsWithHistory` ×3 at :276–292, `workoutsToCsv` at :296–) are updated to the new signatures with explicit instants (`…T12:00:00Z`) and an explicit zone, not machine-local Dates. *Test:* `tests/unit/health/progressReads.test.ts` (existing file, extended) — "a 23:30 Monday workout in New York counts on Monday when the machine runs in Kiritimati" (sets `process.env.TZ` the way `tests/unit/shared/dateUtils.test.ts:104–131` already does; asserts `adherenceThisWeek`, `weeklyVolume` and `liftBests` all file it under the Monday / that Monday's week) and "the functions still on the machine's clock are exactly these three" (reads `src/health/healthService.ts`, finds every `localDateKey(` and `mondayOf(` call site, asserts none is outside those three function bodies, and asserts the count inside them equals a named figure that may only shrink).

4. **A date key is labelled with its own month, wherever you are.** `src/shared/dateUtils.ts` · new export `dateKeyLabel(key: "YYYY-MM-DD" | "YYYY-MM", opts: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions): string` (parse the key as UTC fields, format with `timeZone: "UTC"`, so the browser's zone cannot move it). Today: ProgressTab.tsx:195 does `new Date(v.weekStart)` — UTC midnight, the previous local day west of UTC — then `getMonth()` (:199) and `toLocaleDateString(month)` (:208); HistoryTab.tsx:249 keys months by `new Date(at).toLocaleDateString(...)` in the browser's zone. Change: ProgressTab labels bars with `dateKeyLabel(v.weekStart, { month: "short" })` and decides "new month" by comparing `weekStart.slice(0, 7)`; HistoryTab labels months with `dateKeyLabel(monthKey, { month: "long", year: "numeric" })` from the server's `monthKey` (step 5). *Test:* `tests/unit/shared/dateUtils.test.ts` — "a key for the 1st of June is labelled June in every timezone" (loops the file's TZ list with `process.env.TZ`).

5. **History reads a whole month at a time from a bounded, all-time, filtered read.** `src/db/healthRepo.ts` · `getWorkoutLogs` / `getWorkoutLogsWithSets` accept `days: number | "all"` ("all" drops the `gte`; the `since` instant for a number stays `new Date()` minus days — an instant, not a day). New export `readHistoryMonths(userId, { timezone, before?: string, lift?: string, minRows?: 20, maxMonths?: 12 })` → `{ months: Array<{ monthKey: "YYYY-MM", monthStart: string, logs: WorkoutLogWithSets[] }>, nextBefore: string | null }`. Algorithm: `before` defaults to `new Date().toISOString()` (an instant). Probe: the newest finished log with `logged_at < before` (`finishedWorkouts(...).lt("logged_at", before).order("logged_at", desc).order("id", desc).limit(1)`; when `lift` is given the probe selects `id, logged_at, workout_sets!inner(id)` with `.eq("workout_sets.exercise", lift)`); none → return what has been read so far with `nextBefore: null`. Else compute that log's `monthKey` = `getTodayInTimezone(timezone, loggedAt).slice(0, 7)`, `monthStart` = `startOfDayInstant(`${monthKey}-01`, timezone)`, `nextMonthStart` the same for the following month (integer arithmetic on year/month); read the month's logs whole with `readAllRows` (`gte(monthStart)`, `lt(nextMonthStart)`, ordered `logged_at desc, id desc`, and with the same `!inner` lift filter when filtering, so a filtered page holds only workouts that contain the lift), then their sets with `chunkIds` + `readAllRows` (`.eq("exercise", lift)` when filtering); repeat with `before = monthStart` until `minRows` rows or `maxMonths` months. After the loop, probe once more below the oldest `monthStart` read: `nextBefore` = that `monthStart` if something older exists, else `null` — so "That is everything" is a fact the server checked, not the absence of a next page. New route `app/api/workouts/history/route.ts` · `GET ?before=&lift=` (under 50 code lines; `getUserTimezone` for the zone). Today: HistoryTab.tsx:85 fetches `days=365`, pages the array client-side, and "Show more" (:558) disappears with no notice. *Test:* `tests/unit/health/workoutHistoryPaging.test.ts` (capped fake database, the `tests/unit/health/workoutPaging.test.ts` pattern, extended with `lt`/`gte`/`limit`) — "History pages back a whole month at a time and says when it has reached the beginning" (14 months of workouts: page 1 ends on a month boundary; the last page has `nextBefore: null` and no page before it does), "with a lift filter a page carries only that lift's sets and only the workouts that contain it", and "a workout at 23:30 on the last day of May, New York, files under May" (the fake's rows are UTC instants; asserts the `monthKey`).

6. **History becomes the month-paged list with honest totals and receipt links.** `src/programs/components/HistoryTab.tsx` · `HistoryTab` (props become `{ unit, timezone }`). Today: fetches its own year (:83–105), month totals over every lift while filtered (:250–257), summary over every lift (:300–317), row is an expander with inline detail/editor (:348–551), `text-[11px]`/`text-[12px]`/`text-[12.5px]` (:265, :276, :328), native `<select>` (:268), `min-h-9` buttons, `window.confirm` (:118). Change: loads page 1 via `useLoad("/api/workouts/history?lift=…")` (`src/shared/useLoad.ts`); "Show more — older" is `Button variant="outline" className="w-full min-h-11"` (`data-testid="history-load-more"`) that fetches `?before=<nextBefore>` and appends, with its own failed line ("Could not load older workouts." + Try again, `data-testid="history-more-unavailable"`) so a failed second page never reads as the end; when `nextBefore` is null the list ends with `text-sm text-muted-foreground` "That is everything" (`data-testid="history-end"`); the lift filter is the app `Select` (`components/ui/select.tsx`, trigger `h-11`, label `text-xs`) whose options come from the loaded pages plus the current filter value, and changing it refetches page 1 from the server so the filter covers all history; month header sticky, `h3 text-sm font-medium` + `text-xs tabular-nums` "4 sessions · 8,000 kg" or "4 sessions · 8,000 kg of Squat" when filtered; totals = `workingVolumeKg` over the page's (already lift-filtered) sets, so they are exact for the month; each row is a `Link` to `/programs/workout/[id]` (`data-testid="history-row-{id}"`, `min-h-14`, day formatted from `logged_at` with `timeZone: timezone`, right column `text-xs tabular-nums`, summary line via `describeLoggedSet` of each lift's top working set); no expander, no editor, no delete on this screen; failed = amber `AlertTriangle` line + `Button size="sm" variant="outline"` Try again (`data-testid="history-unavailable"`); loading = 92 px pulse; empty = "Nothing logged yet. Finish a workout and it will be here." (the words "last year" are gone). Deletes `remove`, `startEditing`, `save`, `EditableSet`, `setLabel` from this file (they move in step 8) and the row-expander state. `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` (line 614) loses `src/programs/components/HistoryTab.tsx` (line 645). *Test:* `tests/e2e/programs-history-progress.spec.ts` — "History filtered to one lift totals only that lift" (two seeded sessions with Squat 120×5 and Bench 90×5: choose the lift in the `Select`; the month header contains "1,200" and "of ZZHist Squat" and not "2,100"; no row mentions Bench), "History reaches a workout logged fourteen months ago and says when it has reached the beginning" (seed via `entry_date` fourteen months back relative to today — `loggedAtForEntry` accepts any past date; press `history-load-more` until `history-end` is visible, capped at 40 presses; the seeded row is present) and "a bodyweight set reads as reps in History" (Pull-up 0 kg × 12 → text contains "12 reps", not "0×12"). All three open the tab with `getByRole("tab", { name: "History" })` (Phase 5's Tabs; the old `button` locator will not find it).

7. **Deleting or correcting a finished workout is one transaction, computed before anything is written.** Migration `supabase/migrations/20260917120000_delete_and_revise_program_workout.sql` · functions `delete_program_workout(p_workout_id UUID, p_exercise_state JSONB, p_cursor JSONB, p_expected_session_count INTEGER)` and `revise_program_workout(p_workout_id UUID, p_sets JSONB, p_exercise_state JSONB, p_cursor JSONB, p_expected_session_count INTEGER)`, modelled on `finish_program_workout` (`20260907110000_finish_workout.sql`: `LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY INVOKER SET search_path = public`; ERRCODE 55000 refusals; `REVOKE ALL … FROM PUBLIC; GRANT EXECUTE … TO authenticated`; idempotent `CREATE OR REPLACE`) with two deliberate differences: the workout lock predicate is the *finished* one — `WHERE id = p_workout_id AND (ended_at IS NOT NULL OR started_at IS NULL) FOR UPDATE` (finish's predicate is the live one and would refuse every finished workout) — and when `p_exercise_state` is NULL (a loose workout, or a program row whose `enrollment_id` is NULL) the enrollment block is skipped entirely. Otherwise: lock `program_enrollments` where `id = (the workout's enrollment_id) AND (cursor->>'sessionCount')::int = p_expected_session_count`, refuse with 55000 "Your program moved on while this was being corrected — reload and try again" if not found, `UPDATE program_enrollments SET exercise_state = p_exercise_state, cursor = p_cursor`. `delete_program_workout` then `DELETE FROM workout_logs WHERE id = p_workout_id` (sets cascade). `revise_program_workout` deletes the workout's `workout_sets` and inserts from `jsonb_to_recordset(p_sets)` with the columns `reviseWorkout` writes today (workoutRepo.ts:735–753: log_id, exercise, exercise_id, library_id, weight_kg, reps, set_number, set_kind, side, notes, exercise_notes, rpe). No row-level policy is added or changed: under SECURITY INVOKER the statements run as the signed-in person against the policies that already exist (`20260305_create_health_tracking_tables.sql:81–121` own-row read/insert/update/delete on `workout_logs` and, via log ownership, `workout_sets`; `20260618_create_program_tables.sql:41` own-row update on `program_enrollments`). **ASK FIRST — open question 2** (a `GRANT EXECUTE` is a permission). `src/db/healthRepo.ts` · `deleteWorkoutLog` (today: reads `enrollment_id` at :417, DELETEs at :427, then `recalculateEnrollment` at :435, so a recalc failure reports a completed delete as failed and leaves the weights wrong). Change: when the log has an `enrollment_id`, compute the replayed enrollment *first* over `getSessionLogs(userId, enrollmentId)` minus this log, then call the RPC once with the replayed `exerciseState`, `cursor` and the enrollment's current `cursor.sessionCount`; return `{ recalculated: boolean }`. The pure replay comes from Phase 1's export; if Phase 1 has not exported one, this phase extracts `replayedEnrollment(enr: ProgramEnrollment, logs: ProgramSessionLogRow[]): ProgramEnrollment` from the body of `recalculateEnrollment` (programRepo.ts:795–817: the seed check and the `replayEnrollment` call, which is already pure — programsService.ts:1815) and leaves `recalculateEnrollment` as `persistState(await replayedEnrollment(...))`; that extraction is the only change this phase makes inside programRepo besides `reviseSessionLog`. `src/db/workoutRepo.ts` · `reviseWorkout` (today :675–762 deletes, inserts, then recalculates): reads the log with `adjustments, program_day_id, program_cycle, program_week` as well (today's select at :731 lacks them), builds the corrected session's `entries` with `entriesFromSets(rows, adjustments, unit)` (programsService.ts:1271, the same derivation `sessionLogsFor` uses), replays with that session substituted, then one RPC. `src/db/programRepo.ts` · `reviseSessionLog(userId, enrollmentId, logId)` (:737, the door behind `DELETE /api/programs/enrollments/[id]/log/[logId]`, whose response is the enrollment): reads the log's `enrollment_id` and refuses with "That session was not found, so nothing was deleted." when it is not this enrollment, calls `deleteWorkoutLog`, and returns `getEnrollmentById(userId, enrollmentId)` so the route's response shape is unchanged; its own DELETE (:752) and recalc are removed. `app/api/health/workout/route.ts` · `DELETE` returns the repo's `{ recalculated }` and, on a thrown error, the error's message (today: the generic "Failed to delete workout log", :50) with 409 for ERRCODE 55000 and 400 otherwise; the file stays under the 50-code-line rule (`countCodeLines`: blanks and comments are free; `ALLOWED_LONG_ROUTES` does not grow). Executor applies the migration: `supabase migration list --linked` first (run 2026-09-17: every migration through `20260914120000` is applied locally and remotely, and `20260914120000_drop_four_orphan_tables.sql` is now committed in d56d3ef7, so nothing of anyone else's is pending; re-check immediately before `supabase db push --linked`). *Test:* `tests/unit/db/workoutWriteDoors.test.ts` — "every path that removes or rewrites a finished workout goes through delete_program_workout or revise_program_workout" (greps `src/db` for `.from("workout_logs").delete(` and `.from("workout_sets").delete(`; the permitted sites are named in the test: `createWorkoutLog`'s rollback (healthRepo.ts:223), `discardWorkout` (workoutRepo.ts:447, an *open* workout) and `deleteSet` (workoutRepo.ts:286, one live set by id); any other hit fails) and "a recalculation that throws leaves the workout in place" (fake database whose enrollment has `initial_exercise_state` null: `deleteWorkoutLog` and `reviseWorkout` make no RPC call and no delete, and the error carries the replay's message). `tests/e2e/programs-history-progress.spec.ts` — the existing "correcting a program session moves the weights it prescribed" (rewritten in step 8 to open the receipt) and "deleting a program session moves the weights back down" (replaced in step 8) stay green through the new door; the `log/[logId]` DELETE the specs use for cleanup keeps working.

8. **The workout receipt page gets Correct this and Delete, and the correction editor can do the job.** New `src/programs/components/WorkoutActions.tsx` · `WorkoutActions({ workoutId, enrollmentId, programName, loggedAt, timezone, unit })` mounted by Phase 4's `app/programs/workout/[id]/page.tsx` under the receipt (dependency: that page; this phase adds one import and one JSX line to it, passing `getUserTimezone` and the enrollment's program name which that page already resolves or reads via `getEnrollmentById`). Delete: `Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="text-destructive"` (`data-testid="history-delete-{id}"`) → `Dialog` from `components/ui/dialog.tsx` (no `window.confirm`; spec S3 item 19 pattern): "Delete the workout from Mon 7 Sep? Everything in it goes with it." (day formatted with `timeZone: timezone`) plus, when `enrollmentId` is set, one exported constant `PROGRAM_RECALC_WARNING` = "This session belongs to a program. Changing or deleting it recalculates the weights it prescribed from here on." used by both the dialog and the editor (today the sentence exists only in the editor, HistoryTab.tsx:405–406); `Button variant="destructive"` "Delete it" / `variant="outline"` "Keep it". After success the page swaps to "Deleted." + "{programName}'s weights were recalculated from here on." (only when the API said `recalculated: true`) + `Link` "Back to History" (`/programs?tab=history`, Phase 5's `?tab=`). On failure: `role="alert"` destructive line with the server's message; nothing on the page pretends it went through. New `src/programs/components/WorkoutCorrection.tsx` · `WorkoutCorrection({ workoutId, enrollmentId, unit, onSaved })` (the editor moved out of HistoryTab, opened by `Button variant="outline" size="sm"` "Correct this", `data-testid="history-edit-open-{id}"`; it reads its own subject from `GET /api/workouts/[id]` and refuses to open, with a destructive line, when that read fails — the rule in `.claude/rules/database.md`). Today (HistoryTab.tsx:409–450): weight and reps inputs and an X only; `Number(x) || 0` turns a blank into 0 (:189–190); `size-8` X, `h-9` inputs, `min-h-9` buttons. Change: rows grouped per lift with `Input className="h-11"` (weight, reps), a kind `Select` (warmup / working / amrap / backoff / drop — the CHECK constraint's list, spec Decision 6), remove = `Button variant="ghost" size="icon"` (44 px, `aria-label="Remove {setLabel}"` kept); per lift "+ Add a set" (`min-h-11` dashed, appends `{ setNumber: last + 1, kind: "working" }` with blank inputs) and "Change the lift" (opens the search from `src/programs/components/live/AddLift.tsx` and renames every set of that lift; on a loose workout `exerciseId` becomes null and `libraryId` comes from the library; on a program session `exerciseId` is kept — it is the program's slot, and the replay credits the slot — and only the name and library id change); a blank or non-numeric box disables Save and shows `text-sm text-destructive` "Every set needs a weight and reps — 0 reps means you tried and failed" (0 stays allowed, blank does not); Save (`Button`, `data-testid="history-save-{id}"`) and "Leave it as it was" (`Button variant="ghost"`) both `min-h-11`; the program warning is `PROGRAM_RECALC_WARNING`. On success the receipt re-reads the workout and shows "Saved." + the recalculated line when the API said so. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/workoutCorrection.test.tsx` — "Save is refused while a weight or reps box is blank, and 0 reps is accepted", "Add a set appends the next set number of that lift", "Change the lift renames every set of that lift and keeps the program slot on a program session". `tests/e2e/programs-history-progress.spec.ts` — "corrects a set and removes one that never happened" and "correcting a program session moves the weights it prescribed" updated to click `history-row-{id}` (now a navigation to the receipt), wait for the receipt, then use the same `history-edit-open-{id}` / `Weight for …` / `Remove …` / `history-save-{id}` handles; "deleting a program session says it will move the weights, then moves them back down" replaces the `page.once("dialog")` version: the `[role=dialog]` contains "recalculates"; after "Delete it" the page contains "recalculated"; `page.on("dialog")` count is 0; the enrollment's prescription is read back at `askedAt`.

9. **A correction that changes nothing writes nothing, and a pounds lifter's numbers survive a round trip.** `src/programs/components/WorkoutCorrection.tsx` · `EditableSet` keeps `originalKg: number` and `seededText: string`; on save a row whose weight text still equals `seededText` sends `originalKg`, not `toKg(Number(text))`. `src/db/workoutRepo.ts` · `reviseWorkout`: after reading `previous` (:731), compare incoming rows to `previous` — both sorted by exercise, set_number, set_kind — on exercise, exercise_id, weight_kg, reps, set_number, set_kind, side, notes, exercise_notes, rpe; equal → return `{ changed: false, recalculated: false }` before any replay or write. `app/api/workouts/[id]/revise/route.ts` · `PATCH` returns `{ changed, recalculated }` (today: `{ recalculated }`, :18). Today: HistoryTab.tsx:164 seeds `String(show(kg))` (one decimal in lb), :189 converts back and rounds to 0.01 kg — 100 kg → 220.5 lb → 100.02 kg — and reviseWorkout always deletes, inserts and recalculates. *Test:* `tests/unit/db/workoutRevise.test.ts` (fake database, the `workoutPaging.test.ts` pattern) — "saving a correction that changes nothing issues no delete, no insert and no recalculation"; `tests/unit/programs/workoutCorrection.test.tsx` — "an untouched pounds weight is sent back as the original kilograms" (unit lb, 100 kg seeded, Save → the PATCH body carries `weightKg: 100`). `tests/e2e/programs-history-progress.spec.ts` — "a pounds lifter who saves an untouched correction changes nothing" (sets the account unit to lb through the training-settings route `saveTrainingSettings` is behind — the executor locates it by grepping `saveTrainingSettings` in `app/api` — restores it in `finally`; `weight_kg` reads back exactly 100 and the response says `changed: false`).

10. **One definition of a personal best, all-time, on the server.** `src/db/healthRepo.ts` · new export `bestSetFor(userId, exercise, { excludingLogId? })` → `{ weight_kg, reps } | null`: the lift is matched the way `getExerciseMax` (:600–650) already matches it — `.or("library_id.eq.<id>,exercise.ilike.<name>")` via `libraryByName`, or `exercise.ilike` alone when the name is not in the library — over `finishedLogIds` in `chunkIds` chunks, working kinds only (`.in("set_kind", ["working","amrap","backoff"])`, the same list), and per chunk `.order("weight_kg", { ascending: false }).order("reps", { ascending: false }).limit(1)`, taking the best across chunks. New export `priorBestsFor(userId, exercises: string[], { excludingLogId })` → `{ bests: Map<lowerName, { weight_kg, reps } | null>, unavailable: boolean }` (a read error sets `unavailable`, never an empty map — the rule `priorSetsFor` already follows; cost: one `limit(1)` query per lift per 200-log chunk). `src/health/healthService.ts` · new export `bestEstimateKg(weightKg, reps)` = `reps > 10 ? weightKg : estimateOneRepMax(weightKg, reps)` — the cap rule that today lives only inside `liftBests` (:847); `liftBests` calls it, and `getExerciseMax` (:643–648, which today applies Epley with no cap and its own `reps === 1` branch) calls it too, so the Squat-1RM tile and "est. max" on Progress cannot disagree. `src/db/workoutRepo.ts` · `finishWorkout`: replaces `priorSetsFor` (:520, last 400 workouts) and `PR_HISTORY_WORKOUTS` (:505) — both deleted, purpose explained above — with `priorBestsFor` over the lifts in the session; `newRecords` compares each lift's best working set of this session (heavier, then more reps) against a non-null prior; new `firstTimeLogged: string[]` on `WorkoutSummary` (`src/programs/types.ts:749`) = lifts with a null prior and at least one working set here. `detectPersonalRecords` (healthService.ts:232) loses its last server caller; it is deleted with its tests once WorkoutLogger goes (spec Decision 3, recommended "delete"); if Decision 3 is "keep", `app/api/health/workout/route.ts` `POST` returns `personalRecords`/`recordsUnavailable`/`firstTimeLogged` computed with `priorBestsFor` and `WorkoutLogger.tsx:224`'s client-side call is deleted — either way `detectPersonalRecords` is gone and there is one owner. Phase 6's summary renders `firstTimeLogged` as "First time logged: …" (spec S3 item 24) — dependency named, the data is this phase's. `app/api/workouts/progress/route.ts` (step 11) serves `liftBests(allLogs, timezone)` for "Your bests". *Test:* `tests/unit/health/personalBests.test.ts` (capped fake database) — "the finish sheet and Progress agree on the best when the heavier set is fourteen months old" (`priorBestsFor` and `liftBests` over the same fixture both report 105 kg × 5, not this year's 100), "a set stored under the library id but a different spelling still counts", "a lift with no earlier finished set is first-time-logged, never a best", "the open workout's own sets are not its previous best", "a read failure is reported as unavailable, not as no history", and "the Squat-1RM tile and est. max use one estimate" (`getExerciseMax` and `liftBests` agree on a 12-rep set: the weight itself). `tests/e2e/programs-history-progress.spec.ts` — "Your bests is all-time: a heavier set fourteen months old outranks this year's" (`entry_date` fourteen months back; the bests section shows the older number).

11. **Progress is one server-computed snapshot, rendered in one style.** New route `app/api/workouts/progress/route.ts` · `GET` → `{ timezone, thisWeek: { done, days }, weeks: WeekVolume[8], bests: LiftBest[] (top 8), lifts: LiftSeries[] (top 8), empty: boolean }` from `getWorkoutLogsWithSets(userId, "all")` + `getUserTimezone`, composed by a new pure `progressSnapshot(logs, { timezone, now })` in `src/health/healthService.ts` (the route stays under 50 code lines; a thrown read is a 500). `src/programs/components/ProgressTab.tsx` · `ProgressTab({ plannedPerWeek, unit })`: today fetches `days=365` (:50), calls `adherenceThisWeek(logs, plannedPerWeek, new Date())` (:92–93, the browser's clock as the day), `weeklyVolume`, `liftBests` in the browser; `LiftHistory` (:252) fetches 1,095 days again (LiftHistory.tsx:61). Export CSV becomes `Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm"` → `<a href="/api/workouts/export" download>`; new route `app/api/workouts/export/route.ts` · `GET` returns `text/csv` with a `Content-Disposition` filename from `workoutsToCsv(await getWorkoutLogsWithSets(userId, "all"), timezone)` — all-time and in the account's calendar, where today the file (3 years) and the list (1 year) disagreed. `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` loses `src/programs/components/ProgressTab.tsx` (line 649). *Test:* `tests/unit/health/progressReads.test.ts` — "progressSnapshot is empty only when there are no finished workouts" (`empty` is true for `[]`, false for one workout with no working sets — the chart is then all zeros but the account is not empty). `tests/e2e/programs-history-progress.spec.ts` — "shows what each workout was, and what the weeks added up to" rewritten per tests-and-ci-09: seeded dates are *relative* (today −1, −3, −5 days, formatted in the account's zone read from `GET /api/settings/time-preferences`), and the assertions are scoped: `page.locator("section", { hasText: "Your bests" })` contains "ZZHist Squat", "120", `/est\. max 140/`; `week-dots` shows the number of `bg-primary` squares that the seeds' actual zoned days fall in this week (computed in the test from the same dates, so it holds on a Monday too); "the volume chart labels every week with its own month for a New York account" (sets the profile timezone to America/New_York through `PUT /api/settings/time-preferences`, restores it in `finally`; for every bar, the month in the label under it equals the month of the `Week of YYYY-MM-DD` date in that bar's `title` — true on every day of the year, no first-of-month fixture needed); "Progress says it could not load rather than showing an empty year" (`page.route` the progress route to 500 → `progress-unavailable` visible, `progress-empty` absent; `page.unroute` in `finally`).

### Attack log
- Step 10 described `getExerciseMax` (healthRepo.ts:600–650) as an `.ilike(...).order(...).limit(1)` read; it is not — it matches `library_id OR exercise.ilike`, pages every working set and applies Epley in JS with no ten-rep cap and a `reps === 1` branch. Rewrote the step to match the code, made `bestSetFor` match by library id first (a name is the wrong key, per that function's own comment), and added a test for a set stored under a different spelling.
- Step 10 minted `estimatedMaxKg` in healthService beside the existing `estimateOneRepMax` (programsService.ts:311) — two owners of one formula. Replaced with `bestEstimateKg`, which only adds the cap and calls the existing formula; both `liftBests` and `getExerciseMax` use it; test added.
- Step 3 named only the day owner (`getTodayInTimezone`); the week key comes from `mondayOf` → `periodStartFor("weekly", …)` on a wall-clock Date, and the executor would have had to guess how to zone it. Named `periodStartFor("weekly", toZonedDate(instant, tz))` and the `now`-is-an-instant rule for `adherenceThisWeek`/`weeklyVolume`.
- Step 3's signature changes break existing cases in `tests/unit/health/progressReads.test.ts` (3-arg calls, machine-local `logged_at`) and `tests/unit/health/healthService.test.ts` (`liftsWithHistory`, `workoutsToCsv`); the draft did not say so. Named them and the fix (explicit instants + explicit zone).
- Step 3's "exactly these three" test was prose; made it a source-level assertion on `localDateKey(`/`mondayOf(` call sites with a count that may only shrink.
- Step 1's grep guard `weight_kg\s*\*\s*\w*reps` does not match `s.weight_kg * s.reps` (`\w` excludes the dot) — the guard would have passed on the very lines it exists to catch. Regex corrected to `[\w.]*reps`. Also `finishWorkout` works on `LiveWorkoutSet` (`weightKg`, `kind`), not the row shape; named `toStored` (workoutRepo.ts:455) as the bridge.
- Step 1's `isTimedLift(name)` ignored `library_id` on the stored set; widened to library id first, name second.
- Step 5's month read was not lift-filtered for logs, so a filtered page would carry workouts with zero matching sets while its own test demanded otherwise; and "That is everything" was only ever learned one empty request late. Added the `!inner` filter on the month read and a final probe after the loop.
- Step 7 said to copy `finish_program_workout` "line for line"; its lock predicate selects a *live* workout and would refuse every finished one. Wrote the finished predicate explicitly, and the loose-workout skip.
- Step 7 changed `reviseSessionLog` to "a call to deleteWorkoutLog" but the route `DELETE /api/programs/enrollments/[id]/log/[logId]` returns the enrollment; kept the return shape by re-reading it. Also `reviseWorkout`'s replay needs the log's `adjustments`/day/cycle/week, which its select at :731 does not read, and `entriesFromSets` (programsService.ts:1271) to derive the corrected session — both named.
- Step 7's write-door test permitted two delete sites; `deleteSet` (workoutRepo.ts:286, a live set by id) is a third legitimate one the grep would have flagged. Named it.
- Step 8's "Change the lift" on a program session would rename the program slot's `exercise_id`, and the replay throws on an unknown lift (data-integrity-03) — a clean refusal, but the person is stuck. Decided: on a program session the slot id is kept and only name/library change; tested.
- Steps 6/13/14 rely on Phase 5's shadcn Tabs and `?tab=`, but the existing spec clicks `getByRole("button", { name: "History" })`, which stops matching. Every rewritten e2e now says `getByRole("tab", …)`, and the `?tab=history` dependency is named.
- Step 11's unit test title claimed a pure function can tell "failed" from "empty"; it cannot. Split: the unit test pins `empty` semantics, the e2e pins the failed state with `page.route` (+ `page.unroute` for the isolation guard).
- Step 11's New-York e2e only proved anything when a week started on the 1st; replaced with a per-bar check against the bar's own `Week of …` title, valid every day of the year. The seeded-week dot assertion now computes the expected count from the seeds' zoned dates instead of assuming "3".
- Step 6's "Show more" through `useLoad` (a single-URL hook) was unspecified; added the second-page fetch with its own failed line so a failed page 2 never reads as the end.
- Draft open question 1 asked whether rows should open the receipt; the agreed spec (S2 item 20) already says so. Removed as a question; kept as a stated dependency.
- Blocker text said `20260914120000_drop_four_orphan_tables.sql` is untracked; it was committed in d56d3ef7 and `git status` no longer lists it. Corrected, and re-ran `supabase migration list --linked` (all applied through 20260914120000).
- Acceptance cited `KNOWN_DEBT["/programs"]`; that map has no such key. The tap-target budget is `TAP_TARGET_DEBT["/programs"] = 3` (route-sweep.spec.ts:165). Corrected.
- Verified RLS: the four own-row policies on `workout_logs`, the log-ownership policies on `workout_sets` (20260305:81–121) and own-row update on `program_enrollments` (20260618:41) exist, so SECURITY INVOKER functions need no new policy — stated in step 7 so the ASK FIRST is scoped to the GRANT only.
- Checked `loggedAtForEntry` (healthService.ts:677) refuses only future dates, so the fourteen-month seeds work; checked `LiftHistory.tsx:77` `text-[11.5px]` and `:131` browser-zone dates, neither in the draft; both added to step 11.
- Checked `app/api/health/workout/route.ts` is 43 code lines under the guard's counting; the DELETE change fits but the rule and the no-growth of `ALLOWED_LONG_ROUTES` are now stated so the executor does not discover it late.
- Not changed after checking: the existing testids (`history-row-`, `history-edit-open-`, `history-save-`, `history-delete-`, `week-dots`, `lift-bests`, `lift-history`, `export-csv`) are all kept, so the spec's "existing testids" clause holds; `tests/unit/health/workoutPaging.test.ts` mocks a capped database with `in`/`range`/`order` but not `lt`/`limit` — step 5's test extends the fake, stated.

### Open questions for Phase 7

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** The spec lists a 'Calendar toggle' for the History tab (a month grid of trained days) that came from a plan deleted on 2026-09-09; no finding asks for it and its behaviour is not written down anywhere. Build it in this phase or not?

*Recommendation:* Not in this phase. Trained days are already visible as the week strip on Today (Phase 5) and the week squares on Progress. If you want it, say so and it becomes one extra step here plus Calendar being registered for the role 'history calendar toggle' in src/shared/iconRoles.ts (spec Decision 8).

**Open question 2.** ASK FIRST — may this phase add two database functions, delete_program_workout and revise_program_workout, that delete or rewrite one finished workout and move the program's weights in a single transaction? They run with the signed-in person's own permissions (SECURITY INVOKER — your existing row rules still apply, nobody can touch another account's workouts), refuse a stale computation, and are granted only to signed-in users. No row-level policy is added or changed; the only permission written is the GRANT EXECUTE, the same one finish_program_workout already has.

*Recommendation:* Yes. Without them the delete and the weight recalculation are two separate writes and a failure between them is exactly the 'said it failed but it went through' bug this phase closes. If no, the fallback is: compute the replay first, then delete, then save the state — the gap shrinks to one write but does not close, and a dropped connection in that gap leaves the weights wrong until the next correction.

**Open question 3.** History and Progress will switch from the phone's clock to the account's timezone for 'which day / week / month', so they agree with the Today tab and the Tracking card. For an account that has never opened Settings the profile timezone is still the signup default (UTC), so until the phase that closes critic-today-is-utc-until-you-find-settings lands, such an account's late-evening workouts could file under the next day on these two tabs. Accept that ordering, or hold this switch until that fix is in?

*Recommendation:* Accept, and make the timezone fix land in the same run — the two tabs being on one clock with the rest of training is the point, and a half-fixed app where History disagrees with Today is worse than either state. Your own account has a timezone set, so it is unaffected either way.

The executor re-runs the same command immediately before pushing this phase's one migration file and stops if anything else is pending. *Recommendation:* Proceed.


## Phase 8 — The Life Mastery program block becomes a screen — build S4
**What you can do after:** The Templates step of Life Mastery shows one small card that says which training program you are on (or that you are on none), with two taps: "Change program" and "Today's session". Picking, changing, ending and building a program all happen on the Training page, and the back link brings you straight back to the step, where the card already names what you just started. The Systems step's "Training week" reads its days from the program itself, so a phone and a laptop can never show different weeks; if the app cannot reach your programs it says so in amber instead of guessing "2 days a week". Every control on these screens is finger-sized, readable, and stops iPhone Safari zooming when you type. What you will NOT get from this phase: the Templates step stays long, because the wall under the card is every area's goal sets, which this phase does not touch (open question 8).
**Why it is in this order:** It comes after Phase 5 (which gives /programs its Programs screen, the `?view=` / `?program=` addresses, and the shared `ProgramRow` and `BottomSheet`) so the card links only to screens that exist, and before Phase 9 because it deletes the last importers of the blue-grey kit (`src/programs/components/ui.tsx`), which lets Phase 9 forbid that language without an allowlist.
**Findings this phase closes:** lm-templates-03, lm-templates-04, lm-templates-05, lm-templates-07, lm-templates-08, lm-templates-09, lm-templates-10, lm-templates-11, lm-templates-12, lm-build-own-04, lm-build-own-05, lm-build-own-06, lm-build-own-07, lm-build-own-08, lm-build-own-10, lm-build-own-11, lm-build-own-12, design-system-02, design-system-03, design-system-04, design-system-08, design-system-10, tests-and-ci-04, tests-and-ci-11, critic-systems-step-edits-days-the-program-never-hears-about, walk-06-templates-step-12000px, walk-07-day-name-truncated-by-icon-buttons

**Plain-language glossary for this phase.** *Enrollment* = the database row that says "this account is running StrongLifts, started on this date, currently at week 3". *The plan* = the Life Mastery answers, which live only in this browser's storage. *The kit* = the file `src/programs/components/ui.tsx`, a private set of blue-grey buttons and 10-px labels that only the training screens use; the rest of the app uses `components/ui` (orange buttons, slate cards). *Localstorage* = the browser's own storage; it never leaves the device. *Allowlist* = a list inside a test of files that are known to break a rule; the test fails if a new file breaks the rule, and a second test fails if a fixed file is left on the list.

**What this phase relies on from other phases (named, not assumed — the executor runs each check before starting):**
- Phase 5: `components/BottomSheet.tsx` and `src/programs/components/ProgramRow.tsx` exist (`ls` both); `ProgramsApp` reads `?view=programs|edit|build&enrollment=<id>`, `?program=<id>` and `?draft=<id>` from the URL and **every URL it writes keeps the `from` parameter** (otherwise "Your plan" is lost the moment Start hands off to Today — step 7's browser test proves it). Phase 5 also replaces TrainingScreen's kit `Segmented` with shadcn `Tabs` — step 12 cannot delete the kit until `grep -n 'from "./ui"' src/programs/components/TrainingScreen.tsx` is empty.
- Phase 1: `EnrollSchema.workingWeights` accepting 0 kg (lm-templates-01 / lm-build-own-01). Step 10's bodyweight test fails until that lands — the run log says so; the test is not skipped.
- Phase 2: `applyProgramDays` (`northStarService.ts:1271`) no longer copying day names / `daysPerWeek` into the plan (lm-templates-02, walk-08). Step 5 makes the Systems card ignore those copies while a program is linked, so the wrong "2 days a week" stops showing regardless of order.
- Phase 6: the live header naming a self-built week (critic-live-header). Step 10 sends the name; Phase 6 shows it there.
- Phase 3's `chooseCardEnrollment()` is **not** used here (it needs today's session for each program, which this step never fetches). The one rule this phase needs — "adopt the running program when exactly one is running" — is step 2's own, and it never contradicts Phase 3 because with several running nothing is adopted and both cards say "N programs running".

### Steps
1. **One function decides what the week says, on the account's clock.** `src/programs/programWeekService.ts` (new) · `describeProgramWeek(enrollment: ProgramEnrollment, opts: { now: Date; timeZone: string }): { name: string; level: string; week: string; lastTrained: "trained" | "new" | "forgotten"; lastTrainedLine: string }`. Today: the week line is invented in four places — `RunningPrograms.tsx:109-117` reads the *catalogue* days and ignores `customSchedule` (the false "your written week is not the week any of these prescribe" warning, lm-templates-03 / lm-build-own-05), `northStarService.ts:1304` counts named days as days-per-week, `RoutineCard.tsx:274` prints that count as "×/wk", and `WorkoutPrograms.tsx` prints nothing; `RunningPrograms.tsx:106` measures "14 days" with `Date.now()` and `:152` formats the last-logged date with the browser's locale and zone. Change: one pure function, fed the enrollment and the account's timezone (never `new Date()` inside it — `now` and `timeZone` are parameters): schedule = `effectiveProgram(getProgram(e.program_id), e.customSchedule)` (`customize.ts:56`); if `isWeekdayAnchored(schedule)` (`programsService.ts:162`) → "Mon · Wed · Fri — Upper / Lower / Upper" (shorts from `WEEKDAYS`, `config.ts:44`); unanchored → "Workout A · Workout B, in turn" (never a weekly count — the catalogue declares no frequency); endurance (`schedule.kind === "endurance_weeks"`) → "Week N of M · K sessions this week" from `e.cursor.week` (`types.ts:444`), `schedule.weeks.length` and `weeks[week-1].sessions.length`; name from `enrollmentName(e)` (`catalog.ts:108`); `lastTrained`: `e.lastLoggedAt` (`types.ts:499`) set → "trained" and the line "Last trained Fri 12 Sep · 12 sessions" (`Intl.DateTimeFormat` with `timeZone`, count from `e.cursor.sessionCount`); never logged → "forgotten" when `getTodayInTimezone(timeZone, now)` is 14 or more calendar days after `getTodayInTimezone(timeZone, new Date(e.started_at))` (`src/shared/dateUtils.ts:23`) with the line "Never trained — started 18 Jun", else "new" with "Not trained yet". The 14-day rule at `RunningPrograms.tsx:103-108` moves here and is deleted there. Throws `Error("Unknown program: <id>")` if `getProgram` returns nothing — no silent "Day 1"; the *callers* (steps 3 and 5) catch that one error and render their failed state naming the id, so a retired catalogue id never crashes the whole Life Mastery page. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/programWeekService.test.ts` — "an unanchored two-day program reads 'in turn' and never a weekly count", "an anchored program lists its weekdays", "a renamed day comes from the enrollment's own schedule, not the catalogue", "an endurance plan reads week N of M", "started 13 days ago in Europe/Copenhagen is 'new' at 23:30 Copenhagen and 'forgotten' at 00:30 the next day", "the last-trained date is formatted in the account's timezone, not the runner's", "an unknown program id throws".
2. **The plan holds a reference and nothing else; one rule keeps it honest.** `src/goals/programReferenceService.ts` (new) · `reconcileProgramReference(plan: NsPlan, read: { enrollments: ProgramEnrollment[]; loading: boolean; error: string | null }): { plan: NsPlan; adopted: NsRoutineProgram | null; ended: NsRoutineProgram | null }`. Today: the plan learns about a program only when *this browser* pressed Start (`NorthStarFlow.tsx:540-545` → `applyProgramToWorkoutRoutine`), so a program started on the phone is invisible to the laptop's plan (walk §6), and ending a program elsewhere leaves a dead reference. The shared hook `useActiveEnrollments` (`useEnrollment.ts:126`) **never returns `null`** on failure: it keeps the last-known list (`[]` on a first failure) and sets `error` (`useEnrollment.ts:99-113`) — so a rule keyed on the list alone would read "failed" as "none running". Change: (a) `loading || error !== null` → plan untouched, nothing adopted, nothing ended — a failed or unfinished read must never attach or detach; (b) **exactly one** running enrollment that the workout routine does not reference → write the reference via `applyProgramToWorkoutRoutine(plan, [], now, ref)` with `ref = { programId, enrollmentId, label: enrollmentName(e), startedAt: e.started_at }` (`types.ts:1718`; day names deliberately empty — the days are derived by step 1, never copied) and return `adopted`; (c) two or more running and no valid reference → no adoption (both cards say "N programs running"); (d) the referenced enrollment is not in the running list and nothing runs → keep the reference and return it as `ended` so the card can name it ("StrongLifts 5×5 is finished") from the plan's `label` — the only state that reads the plan's copy of the name; (e) referenced and running → the same plan object. `NorthStarFlow.tsx` owns the hook once (`const read = useActiveEnrollments()`), runs one effect that applies the result with `setPlan`, keeps `adopted` in component state (shown once, not stored) and passes `read`, `timezone` and `adopted` down to BuildBoard (→ the card) and RoutineCard; `app/life-mastery/page.tsx:39-42` calls `getUserTimezone(user.id)` (`settingsRepo.ts:153`) for **every** step, not only `today`, and passes `timezone` as a prop. `BoardHandlers.onProgramStarted` / `onProgramEnded` (`BuildBoard.tsx:78-79`, `NorthStarFlow.tsx:540-545`) are deleted — nothing starts a program from this page any more. *Test:* `tests/unit/goals/programReferenceService.test.ts` — "adopts the one running program the plan has never heard of, with no day names", "a read with error set changes nothing even when the list is empty", "a read still loading changes nothing", "an ended program is reported, not silently forgotten", "with three running and no reference it adopts nothing", "an already-referenced program returns the same plan object".
3. **The Templates step shows one status card.** `src/goals/components/north-star/WorkoutPrograms.tsx` (file name kept, per spec S4(b)2) · export renamed `TrainingProgramCard` (the old `WorkoutPrograms` export is deleted so any stale import fails the build); `BuildBoard.tsx:172` mounts it in place of `<WorkoutPrograms … planDays={…}/>` and the `planDays` prop dies. Today: a 628-line catalogue + editor + builder + "RUNNING NOW" band inside a 12,661 px step (walk-06); `mode` state at line 85, `OFFERED` at 55, discipline chips 310-329 (emerald), program grid 333-354, level chips 362-372 (a second "Level" row under BuildBoard's own at 155-166 — lm-templates-09), kg/lb 378-393 (white), `Start tracking this` 511 (emerald, design-system-02), 10-px zinc-600 helper text at 404, 529 (lm-templates-08), "Thirteen cited programs" near 618 (lm-templates-10). Change: build spec S4(a)/(b) exactly, from props (`read`, `timezone`, `adopted`, `ended`, `onRetry = read.refresh`) — the card holds no hook and no `fetch(`: `<p className="text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground">Training program</p>` then `<Card className="gap-0 py-0" data-testid="lm-training-program">` › `div.px-4.py-4.space-y-2`, week/last-trained lines from `describeProgramWeek(e, { now, timeZone })` wrapped in try/catch. States: loading → `div[data-testid=lm-training-program-loading].h-[92px].rounded-xl.bg-muted/40.animate-pulse`; failed (`read.error !== null`, or `describeProgramWeek` threw) → amber `AlertTriangle` line "Could not check which program you are on." (or "StrongLifts is no longer in the catalogue" with the id) + `Button variant="outline" size="sm"` Try again, `data-testid="lm-training-unavailable"`, Pick/Change links still render, "No program yet" never renders; none → "No program yet" / "A goal says where you are going. A program says what you do on Tuesday." / outline "Pick a program" → `withReturn("/programs?view=programs", "/life-mastery?step=templates")` (`src/shared/returnTo.ts:42`) / link "Build my own ›" → `withReturn("/programs?view=build", …)`; one running → `p.text-base.font-semibold.truncate` name + `Badge variant="secondary"` level, `text-sm text-muted-foreground` week line, `text-xs` last-trained line (amber when "forgotten"), outline sm "Change program" → `withReturn("/programs?view=programs&program=<id>", …)` + `Link.min-h-11.text-sm.text-muted-foreground` "Today's session ›" → `/programs?program=<id>`; 2–3 running → "N programs running", one `ProgramRow` per program → `withReturn("/programs?view=programs&program=<id>", …)`, `text-xs text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400` "Training shows one session a day. End the ones you are not doing.", outline "Manage programs" → `withReturn("/programs?view=programs", …)`; ended → the plan's `label` + `text-xs text-emerald-600` "Finished — everything you logged is kept" + outline "Choose what is next"; adoption notice once (`adopted` prop) → `text-xs text-muted-foreground` "Your training week below now follows StrongLifts 5×5." No orange anywhere in the card; the violet step button stays the page's primary; the card uses app tokens (`bg-card border-border`) on the zinc shell exactly as spec S4(a) says — no CSS-variable override on `NorthStarFlow.tsx:696`, and the executor reads the screenshot to confirm it looks intended, not foreign. Copy deleted: every string in `PROGRAM_COPY` (57-62), "Go to today's session" (302, 500), "you will need to be signed in" (529), "Take a ready-made one / Build my own" (`ModeSwitch`, 602-628). BuildBoard's own goal-level pills (155-166) stay but become `Button variant="outline" size="sm" aria-pressed` with the active `border-primary/50 bg-primary/10 text-primary` token set (`TodayCard.tsx:131`) and the caption at 167 goes to `text-xs text-muted-foreground` — one "Level" control on the step, at 44 px. Allowlists: remove `src/goals/components/north-star/WorkoutPrograms.tsx` from `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` (`architecture.test.ts:596-…`, it no longer contains `fetch(`) and from `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` (`:686-…`); remove `BuildBoard.tsx` from `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` only if `grep -n 'text-\[10\(\.5\)\?px\]\|text-zinc-600' BuildBoard.tsx` is then empty — if other lines remain they are not this phase's and the entry stays. *Test:* `tests/unit/goals/trainingProgramCard.test.tsx` — "shows a skeleton while the read is loading", "a read with error set shows the amber line and still offers Pick and Change, never 'No program yet' — even when the list is empty", "a retired program id shows the amber line naming it, not a crash", "one running program names it, its level and its days in turn", "three running programs render three rows and one Manage button", "an ended program says finished and offers what is next", "Change program carries view=programs, program=<id> and a return address to the templates step", "no class in the card matches /zinc-|sky-|emerald-/ except the finished line, nothing is under 12 px, no bg-primary".
4. **The catalogue, the picker and the running band leave Life Mastery for good.** `src/goals/components/north-star/BuildYourOwn.tsx` (delete), `src/programs/components/RunningPrograms.tsx` (delete), `src/programs/components/SavedWeeks.tsx` (delete), `src/programs/customLifts.ts` + `tests/unit/programs/customLifts.test.ts` (delete — DESTRUCTIVE to code only, gated on open question 1: its only readers are BuildYourOwn.tsx:29-111 and CustomProgramBuilder.tsx:68, the palette's typed-lift memory under localStorage `custom-lifts-v1`; the written box accepts any lift name, so nothing a person can do is lost, and the browser key is simply left unread). Delete the other files; their surviving jobs move to /programs — saved weeks are its Yours rows (step 11), building is `?view=build` (step 10). `tests/unit/programs/lifts.test.ts:26` imports `emptyCustomSchedule` from `CustomProgramBuilder` (a re-export at `:1221`) — repoint it at `@/src/programs/builder` (`builder.ts:51`). Allowlists: remove `RunningPrograms.tsx`, `SavedWeeks.tsx`, `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx` from `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` and `BuildYourOwn.tsx`, `RunningPrograms.tsx`, `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx` from `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` (the "only shrinks" tests at `architecture.test.ts:749` and `:776` fail otherwise). *Test:* `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` — new `test("Life Mastery hosts no second copy of the training feature")`: every file under `src/goals/components/north-star/**` may import from `src/programs/components/**` exactly one module, `ProgramRow`; hooks, services, data and types from `src/programs` are allowed (they are the point of reuse). (The spec says "exactly one module"; it is written as "exactly one *component* module" because the enrollment hook, `describeProgramWeek` and the types are imports too — open question 5.)

7. **Life Mastery's step address survives a round trip.** `app/life-mastery/page.tsx` (reads `?step=`, `:24-25`, unchanged) and `components/BackLink.tsx` (unchanged; it strips the query before the label lookup, `:44`, so `ROUTE_LABELS["/life-mastery"] = "Your plan"`, `navTabs.ts:51`, applies). No NorthStarFlow URL writing is added — the address is put on the link at the moment it is made, which is the one place that knows the step. lm-templates-05's other half — an edited program lost when you leave — is closed by moving the editor to ProgramDetail (step 9), where Start is the save and leaving before Start is a deliberate exit from a dedicated screen; the two false promises ("it will still be here", "kept until you do") are deleted with `PROGRAM_COPY` in step 3. *Test:* `tests/unit/navigation/returnTo.test.ts` — existing; plus in `trainingProgramCard.test.tsx` "every outbound link carries from=%2Flife-mastery%3Fstep%3Dtemplates".
