### Acceptance
- `tests/unit/programs/programWeekService.test.ts` — the week line has one owner; an unanchored program never shows a weekly count, a renamed day comes from the enrollment, "forgotten" and the last-trained date are computed in the account's timezone, an unknown id throws.
- `tests/unit/goals/programReferenceService.test.ts` — the plan adopts the one running program, keeps an ended reference so it can say so, adopts nothing when several run, and never changes on a failed or unfinished read even when the list is empty.
- `tests/unit/goals/trainingProgramCard.test.tsx` — all six card states render as specified; failed is never rendered as "No program yet"; a retired id is an amber line, not a crash; every link carries a return address; no zinc/sky/emerald, nothing under 12 px, no orange.
- `tests/unit/goals/systemsTrainingWeek.test.tsx` — a linked week is derived and read-only with no "days a week"; failed, ended and several-running all hide the designer and say why; an unlinked week's designer is 44 px with a rename that keeps spaces and refuses blank.
- `tests/unit/programs/programEditor.test.tsx` — full day names, one options button per row, rename on save, 16-px inputs, kg→lb converts, ProgramDetail has one orange.
- `tests/unit/programs/buildYourWeek.test.tsx` + `enrollSchemaLabel.test.ts` — parse errors block Start loudly; Start names the week and cannot fire twice; "already running" is decided by structure; saved weeks round-trip by id; an old autosave is converted.
- `tests/unit/programs/trainingTypeFloor.test.ts` — the kit file is gone, nothing imports it, no sub-12-px text, every allowlisted line still exists, no non-16-px input, no per-keystroke rename, emerald is never a button; its allowlist only shrinks.
- `tests/unit/programs/catalogReachable.test.ts` — all thirteen programs reachable; no private discipline list anywhere under src/, including `schemas.ts`.
- `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` — "Life Mastery hosts no second copy of the training feature" (north-star imports one component module from src/programs: ProgramRow); "the unreadable-text allowlist only shrinks" and "the fetching-screens allowlist only shrinks" pass **because** this phase's entries were removed (WorkoutPrograms, BuildYourOwn, RunningPrograms, SavedWeeks, CustomProgramBuilder, ProgramEditor, EditActiveProgram, RoutineCard, ui.tsx; BuildBoard only if clean); "no NEW screen fetches its own data" passes because BuildYourWeek's network calls live in `src/programs/hooks/useProgramDrafts.ts`; the icon-registry rule stays green with no registry change (`AlertTriangle`, `ChevronRight`, `Minus`, `MoreVertical`, `Plus`, `Search`, `Trash2` are all in `UTILITY_ICONS`, `iconRoles.ts:18-53`).
- `tests/e2e/life-mastery-program-block.spec.ts` and `tests/e2e/programs-build-week.spec.ts` (training project, one worker, also ignored by the default project) — the card is ≤ 200 px, the round trip through "Your plan" names the program just started, the Systems week is identical in a fresh browser context, the failed state is honest, three programs are three rows, and a written week starts under its own name.
- `.playwright-mcp/` screenshots of the card in none / one / three / failed / ended states at 390 px and of the Systems card linked and failed, read in full by the executor before "done" — including a judgement that the slate card on the zinc step looks intended.
- Guards that must stay green, with allowlists that may shrink but never grow: `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` (all of the above), `tests/unit/navigation/routeReachability.test.ts` (`TEST_LINKS_ALLOWED` unchanged — no new link into `/test`), `tests/unit/navigation/backNavigation.test.ts` (the card's links are `Link`s, the way back is BackLink), `tests/unit/writeCoverage.test.ts` (no new write function; baseline unchanged), `tests/unit/goals/programReferenceKept.test.ts` (still passes — `applyProgramToWorkoutRoutine` is what reconcile calls), `tests/unit/programs/enrollmentLoadFailure.test.tsx` (the hook's keep-last-known behaviour that step 2 depends on).

### Attack log
- Step 2 keyed "fetch failed" on `enrollments === null`; `useActiveEnrollments` never yields null — it keeps the last-known list (`[]` on a first failure) and sets `error` (`useEnrollment.ts:99-113`), so a first-load failure would have read as "none running" and adopted nothing but the Systems card would have shown the editable designer. Fixed: reconcile and both cards key on `error`/`loading`; a new test covers "error set, list empty".
- Whose clock: step 1's "Last trained Fri 12 Sep", "started 18 Jun" and the 14-day rule named no timezone (today `RunningPrograms.tsx:106,152` use `Date.now()` and the browser locale). Fixed: `now` and `timeZone` are parameters, `app/life-mastery/page.tsx` reads `getUserTimezone` for every step and passes it down, calendar days via `getTodayInTimezone`; a Copenhagen midnight-boundary test added.
- Step 2 reused Phase 3's `chooseCardEnrollment`, which the spec defines over today's sessions per program — data this step never fetches. Replaced with the rule the spec's item 8 actually states (adopt the single running program); several running → no adoption, both cards say so.
- Step 15 added the new specs only to `training.testMatch`; `playwright.config.ts:54-58` is the default project's `testIgnore`, so they would also have run in parallel Chromium against the shared account. Fixed.
- The draft never mentioned that `architecture.test.ts` already holds `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` (`:686`) and `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA` (`:596`) listing every file this phase repaints or deletes, and that the "only shrinks" tests (`:749`, `:776`) fail unless the entries are removed. Each step now names its removals.
- Step 10's `BuildYourWeek.tsx` would contain `fetch(` in a `/components/` file — an offender under `architecture.test.ts:758`, fixable only by growing an allowlist the draft forbids growing. Fixed: network calls move to `src/programs/hooks/useProgramDrafts.ts` (the `useEnrollment.ts` pattern).
- Step 13 created a second owner for the 10 px / zinc-600 rule that `architecture.test.ts:739` already owns, and allowlisted "exactly one" `text-[11px]` line in LiveWorkoutScreen when there are four (`:255, :292, :348, :435`). Fixed: the existing rule keeps its scope, the new file owns the stricter training floor, its allowlist is seeded from the day's grep with every entry asserted present.
- Acceptance said `writeCoverage` "classifies the label field"; it classifies write *functions* (`writeCoverage.test.ts:16-21`). Reworded: no new write function, baseline unchanged.
- Step 10's "already running" compared the typed text to `formatProgramText` of the running schedule — the enrollment's weights move after the first session, so the match would break exactly when it matters. Fixed: structural comparison of day labels and lift names, weights ignored, with a test that says so.
- Step 1 throws on an unknown program id, and the card called it inside render — one retired catalogue id would have crashed the whole Life Mastery page. Fixed: callers catch that one error and render the failed state naming the id; test added.
- Step 14's "no private discipline list" would have failed on `schemas.ts:350`, where `DraftBody.discipline` spells the seven ids out. Widened: the schema derives from `DISCIPLINES`, so the rule has one home and the test is true.
- Step 9 left ProgramDetail's active level/unit toggles as `Button variant="default"` (`ProgramDetail.tsx:125`) — two more oranges beside Start, against the one-orange rule. Fixed, with a test.
- Step 4 deleted the palette without noticing `src/programs/customLifts.ts` and its test become dead (only readers: BuildYourOwn.tsx:29-111, CustomProgramBuilder.tsx:68). Named, gated on open question 1, and the orphaned `custom-lifts-v1` key explained.
- Step 4 deleted `RunningPrograms.tsx` outright while spec S2 says Phase 5's Running rows "use RunningPrograms's app-tone data". Added a grep gate: if Phase 5 mounted it, strip the tone/planDays/confirm instead of deleting.
- Acceptance asserted `scrollHeight ≤ 6,000 px`; the walk measured 12,661 px with the block closed, and the block is a fraction of that — the rest is every area's goal sets, which this phase does not touch. Replaced with an honest measure and open question 8, and the plain-language opening now says the step stays long.
- Step 5 missed `splitPreview` (`northStarService.ts:1456`, `RoutineCard.tsx:540`), a third reader of the copied days. Added.
- Step 7 claimed lm-templates-05 closed without saying what happens to an edit abandoned before Start on ProgramDetail (component state, lost on leaving). Said plainly: Start is the save; the false promises are deleted.
- Step 3 mounted the hook in the card while step 2 mounted it in NorthStarFlow — two readers of one store. Fixed: NorthStarFlow owns the read and passes it down; the card has no hook and no fetch.
- Step 8 named `prescriptionSummary` as if exported; it is a private helper (`ProgramEditor.tsx:86`). Corrected. `renameDay` throws on blank (`customize.ts:178`); the dialog now validates first so the throw is never the UI.
- Line numbers corrected: `OFFERED` is `:55` not `:54`; `onChange={setSchedule}` is `:412` not `:407`; `Start tracking this` is `:511`.
- Icons checked against `iconRoles.ts`: all seven used are in `UTILITY_ICONS` (`:18-53`); no registry change. `MoreVertical`'s context (overflow menu) is the registered one.
- e2e (3) "fresh browser context" relied on reconcile running on the Systems step; made explicit that the effect lives in NorthStarFlow (any step) and named the `storageState` reuse.
- Added the requirement that Phase 5's URL writes on /programs keep `from` — without it "Your plan" is lost after Start hands off to Today; step 7's browser test now proves it.
- Verified, not assumed: every symbol named in a step was grepped (`effectiveProgram`, `isWeekdayAnchored`, `WEEKDAYS`, `enrollmentName`, `applyProgramToWorkoutRoutine`, `useActiveEnrollments`, `withReturn`, `ROUTE_LABELS`, `materializeSchedule`, `isModified`, `fromKg`/`toKg`, `parseProgramText`, `formatProgramText`, `renameSplitDay`, `routineSummary`, `splitPreview`, `renameDay`, `moveDay`, `removeDay`, `swapExercise`, `removeExercise`, `emptyCustomSchedule`, `getUserTimezone`, `getTodayInTimezone`, `AddLift`, `CreateDraftSchema`, `UpdateDraftSchema`, the drafts PATCH/DELETE/start routes, `Textarea`, `Dialog`, `icon-sm`). `chooseCardEnrollment`, `BottomSheet`, `ProgramRow` do not exist yet (Phase 5/3 outputs) — listed as pre-checks, not assumed.

### Open questions for Phase 8

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** Decision 5 from the design spec: delete the tap-to-build program builder (about 1,200 lines, the 21-button lift picker) and its custom-lift memory, and keep only the written 'Write your week' box on the Training page? This phase is written on a yes.

*Recommendation:* Yes. The written mode already handles supersets and bodyweight, it was the mode that worked in the walkthrough, and the tap-to-build mode is where most of the unreadable blue-grey styling lives. If no, the builder is repainted at roughly the cost of the rest of this phase combined.

**Open question 2.** Decision 11: Life Mastery stops starting programs itself. The Templates step becomes a status card; picking, editing, ending and building all happen on the Training page and link back. The plan stores only which program is linked. This phase is written on a yes.

*Recommendation:* Yes — it is the only way to have one copy of the catalogue and one copy of the week.

**Open question 3.** When the program the plan points at has ended and nothing else is running, the card wants to say 'StrongLifts 5×5 is finished — pick what is next'. To know the name without a second request, this phase keeps the ended reference (with the name as it read when started) in the plan in this browser until a new program is adopted, rather than fetching the finished-programs list too. Acceptable?

*Recommendation:* Yes, keep the reference. It is one browser's memory of a name, the database still decides everything, and the alternative is a second request on every open of the step.

**Open question 4.** The half-written week in the box at /programs?view=build is autosaved in the browser's own storage (text only), so it survives a reload on that device but does not follow you to another device unless you press 'Save for later'. Keep that per-device scratch, or make 'Save for later' the only memory?

*Recommendation:* Keep the scratch. Losing a half-typed week to a reload is the complaint that created the old localStorage design; saved weeks are the cross-device answer and are one tap away.

**Open question 5.** The spec says an architecture test should assert 'src/goals imports exactly one module from src/programs'. Written literally it fails for reasons that are not faults (types, the enrollment hook, the week-line function, the catalogue names). This phase writes it as 'the Life Mastery folder imports exactly one component from the training feature — the shared ProgramRow'. OK to narrow it that way?

*Recommendation:* Yes. The rule's purpose is 'no second copy of the catalogue, editor or builder inside Life Mastery'; forbidding component imports except ProgramRow is exactly that, and it stays true of hooks and types.

**Open question 6.** ProgramPicker (the old green program chooser) is also used on the /test/new-goals bench page, where choosing a program starts it when the plan is saved. The spec lists ProgramPicker for deletion; this phase leaves it because deleting it silently removes that behaviour from a bench screen the phase does not own. Leave it for whoever promotes or deletes /test/new-goals?

*Recommendation:* Leave it. It is invisible in production (every /test page answers 404 there), and the architecture test in this phase is scoped so it does not count.

**Open question 7.** Step 9 adds the program editor to the Training page's program detail screen (change days and swap lifts before you press Start). That is a visible change on /programs for everyone, and /programs is Phase 5's surface. Is this phase the right owner, or should Phase 5 mount it?

*Recommendation:* This phase. The editor is this phase's component, and Life Mastery loses 'change it before you start' unless the detail screen gains it in the same change; Phase 5 keeps ownership of the screen's list, tabs and addresses.

**Open question 8.** The spec's acceptance says the Templates step should be at most 6,000 px tall on a phone. It is 12,661 px today with the program block closed, and the block is only part of that — the rest is every area's goal sets listed in full under it, which this phase does not touch. Removing the block cannot reach 6,000. Should a separate small phase collapse each area's goal sets behind its heading (roughly one component, BuildBoard's area cards, plus a test that the step opens under 6,000 px), or is 'the training block is small and at the top' enough for now?

*Recommendation:* Add the small collapse phase after this one. It is one component and one browser assertion, and it is the only way the spec's 6,000 px number becomes true; this phase asserts the card is ≤ 200 px and sits in the first screen, and records the step's height in the run log so the next phase has the number.


### Blockers for Phase 8

No blockers.


## Phase 9 — One kit, one language
**What you can do after:** Every training screen — the Tracking card, Training's Today/History/Progress, the live workout, the Life Mastery program block — looks like one app: the same orange for "do this", green only for "done", the same buttons, cards and tabs as the rest of the app, nothing on a phone smaller than a fingertip (44 px) and no text under 12 px (one 11 px column caption on the live set grid is the single exception — and the bottom tab bar, which today is 11 px and 43.75 px tall on every page, comes up to the same floor). Tapping a number box on an iPhone no longer zooms the page. The blue-grey "kit" that painted Training a different colour is gone, and a test fails the moment anyone brings a second colour scheme back.
**Why it is in this order:** Part A (the kit) must land before Phases 3, 5, 6 and 8 so they build from the app's own parts instead of repainting later; Part B (the sweep) can only measure zero once those phases have rebuilt their screens, so it runs last.
**Findings this phase closes:** programs-today-10, design-system-01, design-system-02, design-system-03, lm-templates-08, lm-build-own-11, design-system-04, design-system-07, history-progress-11, design-system-08, design-system-09, design-system-10, live-workout-08, lm-build-own-10, lm-templates-09, programs-today-11

**Dependencies named, not owned here.** programs-today-06 / design-system-05 (tab bar on /programs) and design-system-06 (six button styles on Today) are Phase 5's S2 shell; walk-07 (day name cut to "Wor…") is Phase 8's S4 editor; the "scenarios completed" icon reuse disappears when Phase 5 deletes `TodaySessionWidget.tsx` (spec S2 (d)); `MoreHorizontal` → `MoreVertical` on the live screen is Phase 6 (spec S3 element 5); `Calendar` for the History calendar toggle is Phase 7's registry ask; wiring the `sweep-*` projects into CI (the other half of tests-and-ci-07) is Phase 10 — this phase hands Phase 10 the zeroed numbers and a ratchet that already runs in `npm test`. lm-templates-09's second "Level" row (the program's seed-weight chips) is removed by Phase 8's status card; this phase's lint is what keeps it from coming back — BuildBoard's own level pills are Life Mastery's style and are not touched.

**No date logic in this phase.** Nothing here computes "today". Two `new Date()` calls are in scope and both stay: `src/programs/components/LiftHistory.tsx:108` names a CSV file on the user's own device from the user's own clock; `:131` formats a stored date for an `aria-label` in the browser's locale — display of a saved date, never a decision about which day it is.

**No migration, no RLS, no payments, no auth.** One registry line needs a yes (step A7). One shared-bar change needs a yes (step A3, Open question 7).

**Verified, not assumed.** `@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *))` at `app/globals.css:8` and no file adds a `dark` class (grep of `app/`, `components/`, `src/`), so every `dark:` class in the app is dead — the black outline button and the red hover are real. The bottom tab bar's six items measure 56 × 43.75 px and 11 px type on `/dashboard/tracking` at an iPhone 14 viewport (measured 2026-09-17 on the dev server with account A; the sweep's own selector counts all six).

### Steps

#### Part A — the kit (run BEFORE Phases 3, 5, 6, 8)

1. **Fix the app's bordered and transparent buttons once, for every screen (Decision 1).** `components/ui/button.tsx` · `buttonVariants`. Today: `outline` is `border bg-background shadow-xs hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground dark:…` (line 16) and `ghost` is `hover:bg-accent hover:text-accent-foreground dark:…` (line 20); `--accent` is sunset red (`app/globals.css:25`), `--background` is the page black (`:13`), and `app/layout.tsx:68` renders `<html lang="en">` with no `dark` class while `globals.css:8` makes `dark:` depend on that class — so the `dark:` variants never apply: every outline button is a black box on a slate card and every outline/ghost hover flashes red (the "disabled-looking Start" in walk §1). Change: `outline` → `border border-border bg-transparent hover:bg-muted/50 hover:text-foreground`; `ghost` → `hover:bg-muted/50 hover:text-foreground`. The `dark:` fragments on these two variants are deleted, not kept — they are dead code that hides the real value (the `destructive` variant's `dark:` stays; it is not this phase's). Gated on **Open question 1**. *Test:* `tests/unit/shared/buttonVariants.test.ts` — "outline and ghost buttons never paint the page background or the red accent" (asserts `buttonVariants({variant:"outline"})` contains `border-border` and neither it nor `ghost` contains `bg-background`, `hover:bg-accent` or `dark:`). Reverting line 16 fails it. Also run `npm run test:e2e -- --project=smoke-iphone --project=mobile-nav` — Settings and Goals use outline buttons and must still pass.

2. **Training's sizes and widths come from one file.** New `src/programs/components/trainingStyles.ts` (a `.ts`, so it can never hold JSX or a colour of its own) exporting the spec's cross-cutting classes as named constants, each with the one-line reason from the spec: `TRAINING_COLUMN = "mx-auto max-w-2xl px-4"` (spec: /programs and /programs/live share one width — programs-today-11); `TRAINING_CARD = "gap-0 py-0"` + `TRAINING_CARD_BODY = "px-4 py-4"` (Card already carries `py-4 sm:py-6 gap-4`, `components/ui/card.tsx:10`) + `LIFT_CARD_BODY = "px-3 py-2.5"` (spec S3 element 2 — the live lift card is deliberately tighter); `TINTED_BUTTON = "w-full border-primary/50 bg-primary/10 text-primary hover:bg-primary/15 hover:text-primary"`; `CHIP_ON = "border-primary/50 bg-primary/10 text-primary"` (the `aria-pressed` state of every choice chip — today typed in `TodayCard.tsx:131` and copied by hand elsewhere); `SKELETON = "h-[92px] rounded-xl bg-muted/40 animate-pulse"`; `FAILED_LINE = "flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400"` (the spec's literal; the `dark:` half is dead today like every other, and is kept only because SessionNotices.tsx:52 and StatTile already carry it — deleting it app-wide is not this phase's); `SECTION_LABEL = "text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground"`; `SECTION_HEADING = "text-sm font-semibold"`; `GRID_CAPTION = "text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground"` (the only 11 px allowed anywhere in training — spec S3 element 8); and `DONE = { row: "bg-emerald-500/10", tick: "border-emerald-500/50 bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-500", dot: "bg-emerald-500", text: "text-emerald-500", notice: "rounded-md border border-emerald-500/30 bg-emerald-500/10 px-2.5 py-2 text-xs text-emerald-500" }` — the one place green exists (see step 8: emerald is forbidden everywhere else). Today: widths are literals in three files (`TrainingScreen.tsx:130` `max-w-3xl`; `LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx:153,175,228,518` and `RestBar.tsx:109,155` `max-w-2xl`), padding is doubled (`TrainingCard.tsx:158`, `TodayCard.tsx:100` `CardContent p-4` inside a padded Card), and emerald is typed in sixteen files. Change: Phases 3, 5, 6, 8 import these; nothing in `src/programs/**` writes a `max-w-<size>` or `emerald-` literal again — that rule is enforced by step 8's lint (which has a shrinking debt list), not by this file's test, so nothing is red on the day this lands. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/trainingStyles.test.ts` — "the training screens' widths, paddings and the colour of done come from one file" (imports the module; every exported string is token classes only — none matches `\b(zinc|sky|slate|neutral|gray|rose|red|green|violet|white|black)\b`; the only `text-[…px]` anywhere in the module is `GRID_CAPTION`'s `text-[11px]`; `DONE` has exactly the five keys above and every value contains `emerald`; `TRAINING_COLUMN` contains `max-w-2xl`). Reverting any constant to a raw colour or a different width fails it.

3. **One bottom sheet for every menu, and the bar it comes from reaches the floor.** New `components/BottomSheet.tsx` · `BottomSheet` + `SheetRow`, lifted from `components/MobileTabBar.tsx:79–134` (`fixed inset-0 z-50`, backdrop `bg-background/60 backdrop-blur-sm`, panel `bg-card rounded-t-2xl border-t border-border pb-safe animate-slide-up`). Not verbatim — three things the More sheet gets wrong come right in the move: rows gain `min-h-11` (today `px-3 py-3` with no minimum, line 110/125), the close X becomes `Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Close"` (today a bare 20 px `<button>`, line 95–100), and the sheet gains `role="dialog"` + `aria-modal="true"` + Escape-to-close (today neither). The `@keyframes slideUp` / `.animate-slide-up` rule lives in a `<style>` tag inside `MobileTabBar.tsx:138–146` — it moves to `app/globals.css` next to `.pb-safe` (line 228) so the class still exists once the sheet is used from four other files. `SheetRow` takes `destructive` → `text-destructive`; rows are `flex min-h-11 items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-3 py-3 text-sm font-medium`. Props: `open`, `onClose`, `title`, `testId`, `children`; at ≥ 640 px it renders as a centred `Dialog` (`components/ui/dialog.tsx` · `Dialog`, `DialogContent`, `DialogTitle`) so desktop gets a real dialog rather than a phone sheet stretched wide. `MobileTabBar` switches to it (keeps `data-testid="more-sheet"` via `testId`), so the app has one sheet, not one per menu. **In the same edit, the bar itself:** the five `Link`s (line 55) and the More `button` (line 69) gain `min-h-11` and their labels go `text-[11px]` → `text-xs` (lines 61, 73). Measured today: every item is 43.75 px tall and 11 px — six sub-44 controls and six sub-12 texts on every page that mounts the bar, which after Phase 5 includes /programs, so without this the zero in step 13 and the 12 px floor in step 15 are unreachable. This is a visible change to every page's bottom bar (labels one pixel larger, items a quarter-pixel taller) — gated on **Open question 7**. Today: `MobileTabBar.tsx:79–134` is the only sheet, and the spec needs four more (program ⋮, lift ⋮, set menu, editor day ⋮) — Phases 5, 6, 8 consume this. *Test:* `tests/unit/shared/bottomSheet.test.tsx` — "the More menu and a training menu are the same sheet" (renders `BottomSheet` open with two `SheetRow`s under jsdom: `role=dialog` present, every row carries `min-h-11`, the destructive row carries `text-destructive`, the close control is a `[data-slot=button]`, pressing Escape calls `onClose`; and a source read of `components/MobileTabBar.tsx` shows it imports `BottomSheet`, no longer contains `fixed inset-0` or `@keyframes`, and its item classes contain `min-h-11` and `text-xs` and not `text-[11px]`; `app/globals.css` contains `@keyframes slideUp`). `tests/unit/navigation/backNavigation.test.ts` stays green (it looks for `<MobileTabBar` at line 157, which is unchanged). Run `npm run test:e2e -- --project=mobile-nav` afterwards: the More menu is the one flow that opens the sheet.

4. **A number you can tap or type, at thumb size.** New `components/ui/stepper.tsx` · `Stepper`: `Button variant="outline" size="icon-sm"` Minus · `Input className="h-11 w-14 text-center tabular-nums"` · Plus, keeping the kit's draft-on-blur rule and its comment (`src/programs/components/ui.tsx:189–234`: typing "1" on the way to "12" must not apply 1; stepping from an open draft moves from what is on screen; empty or nonsense reverts). Today: the kit `Stepper` (ui.tsx:202) is 36 px (`h-9 w-11`, line 261), 12.5 px type (same line — defeats the phone's 16 px rule and makes Safari zoom, design-system-04), and painted in `bg-black/25` / `text-zinc-100`. Phase 8's editor (sets, reps) uses this. *Test:* `tests/unit/shared/stepper.test.tsx` — "typing 1 on the way to 12 does not apply 1, and the box is 44 px with 16 px type" (fires change "1" then "12" then blur → `onChange` called once with 12; + from a draft "7" gives 8; blur on "" leaves `onChange` uncalled and shows the last value; the input's class list includes `h-11` and `text-base` and no `text-[1`; both buttons carry `data-slot="button"` and `size-11`).

5. **One row shape for every program list.** New `src/programs/components/ProgramRow.tsx` · `ProgramRow` (spec cross-cutting): `Link` when `href` is given, `button` otherwise; `flex min-h-14 w-full items-center justify-between gap-3 px-4 text-left` › `font-medium truncate` name + `text-xs text-muted-foreground` meta + optional right slot (`Badge` / text / a ⋮ `Button`) + `ChevronRight size-4 text-muted-foreground`. Today: Running (`RunningPrograms.tsx`), Catalogue (`ProgramCatalog.tsx` — 13 cards with orange "View"), Finished (`PastPrograms.tsx` — 26 px buttons, measured), Saved weeks (`SavedWeeks.tsx`) and the Life Mastery list are five different rows. Phases 5 and 8 build every list from this one. *Test:* `tests/unit/programs/programRow.test.tsx` — "one row shape for every program list" (with `href` renders an `a`, without it a `button`; both carry `min-h-14`; the meta line carries `text-xs`; the right slot renders; the name element carries `truncate` — jsdom cannot measure overflow, so the class is the contract).

6. **Every shared choice control is 44 px on a phone, and the kit's Segmented is deleted rather than resized.** Three roots, one rule: (a) `components/ui/tabs.tsx` · `TabsList` today `inline-flex h-11 sm:h-10 … p-1` (line 16) with `TabsTrigger` `px-3 py-2 sm:py-1.5` (line 31) → triggers render ≈ 36 px inside the 44 px list. Change: `TabsList` → `h-auto`, `TabsTrigger` gains `h-11 sm:h-8`; the training call site then only passes `grid w-full grid-cols-3` (Phase 5). Settings and Inner Game use these tabs and get the same 44 px — say so in the commit; their `TAP_TARGET_DEBT` numbers (`/dashboard/settings: 11`, `/dashboard/inner-game: 8`) can only fall (step 13 re-measures every route for exactly this reason). (b) `src/timetrack/components/primitives.tsx` · `Segmented` today `h-10 text-sm sm:h-8` / `h-9 text-xs sm:h-7` (line 440) → `h-11 text-sm sm:h-8` / `h-11 text-xs sm:h-7` (12 px is the floor). (c) `src/programs/components/ui.tsx` · `Segmented` today 28 px at `size="sm"` (line 116, `px-2 py-1`, no minimum) and sky-blue when active (line 118) — it is not resized; its four importers leave in Phases 5 and 8 (`TrainingScreen.tsx:25` → shadcn `Tabs`; `WorkoutPrograms.tsx:49` and `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx:33` → deleted with S4; `ProgramEditor.tsx:36` → repainted) and the file is deleted in step 10. Chips that are a choice (kg/lb, level, discipline, day) are `Button variant="outline" size="sm" aria-pressed` with `CHIP_ON` when pressed, which is already 44 px on touch (`button.tsx:31`). *Test:* `tests/unit/shared/touchTargets.test.ts` — "every shared control is 44 px on a phone" (reads `button.tsx`: every `size` value contains `h-11` or `size-11`; `input.tsx`: `h-11`; `tabs.tsx`: trigger contains `h-11`, list contains `h-auto`; `primitives.tsx` Segmented: both size branches contain `h-11`; `stepper.tsx`: `h-11` and `icon-sm`; `MobileTabBar.tsx`: item classes contain `min-h-11`). Reverting any one root fails it.

7. **ASK FIRST — the rest-timer icon (Decision 8), and a registry that checks context, not just presence.** `src/shared/iconRoles.ts` · `SEMANTIC_ICON_ROLES.Timer`. Today: `Timer: ["time tracker navigation"]` (line 129), yet `src/programs/components/live/RestBar.tsx:29` and `RestTimer.tsx:21` import it for a rest countdown — a context the registry does not list, which the rules say needs your yes. `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts:950` only checks that a multi-file icon is registered, not that its context matches, which is why this passed silently (design-system-09). Change, only after the yes: `Timer: ["time tracker navigation", "rest countdown (RestBar)"]`. Widening — reuse the mechanism the file already has rather than adding a second one: `CUSTOM_ICON_COMPONENTS` (line 159) already pairs an import pattern with `allowedPathPatterns` and the test at `architecture.test.ts:1008` already enforces it. Add a sibling export `CONTEXT_LOCKED_ICONS: Record<string, RegExp[]>` in the same shape — `Timer: [/^components\/navTabs\.ts$/, /^src\/programs\/components\/live\/RestBar\.tsx$/, /^src\/programs\/components\/RestTimer\.tsx$/]` — and extend the existing loop to check lucide imports (`import\s*\{[^}]*\bTimer\b[^}]*\}\s*from\s*['"]lucide-react['"]`) against it. `RestTimer.tsx` is on the list only because Phase 5 has not deleted it yet; when it goes, its pattern goes (the list shrinks; the existing "the list may shrink but not grow" convention applies). `CheckCircle2` is **not** locked: it is imported in nine files across eight slices today (`GoalsSummarySection`, `PeriodRollupRow`, `Checkout`, `HomePage`, `SettingsPage`, `ScenariosHub`, `ValuesHub`, `AspirationalCheck`, `TodaySessionWidget`) — its registry role "completed scenarios" is already stale app-wide, which is a different finding, and a lock would fail on day one; the training reuse disappears with `TodaySessionWidget.tsx` in Phase 5. *Test:* `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` — existing "Icons used in multiple files must be registered in iconRoles.ts" stays green, plus new "context-locked icons are only imported where their role lives" (fails if `Timer` is imported from `lucide-react` in any file outside the patterns). If the answer is no: RestBar switches to `Hourglass` (exists in the installed lucide; a new icon — listed for approval instead) and `CONTEXT_LOCKED_ICONS.Timer` drops the two training patterns.

8. **The lint that makes one language permanent.** `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` · new `describe("One visual language on the training screens")` beside the existing "Screens and the data they show" block (whose `unreadableText()` at line 732 checks only `text-[10px]`/`text-[10.5px]`/`text-zinc-600` and still allows 11 px and zinc-500). Files in scope: `src/programs/**/*.tsx`, `src/goals/components/north-star/WorkoutPrograms.tsx`, `components/BottomSheet.tsx`, `components/ui/stepper.tsx`. **Matching is by JSX opening tag, not by line:** a `className` is almost never on the same line as its `<button`, so the scanner takes each opening tag from `<button|<Button|<a |<Link|<input|<Input|<select|<textarea|<Textarea` to its closing `>` (brace-aware, so `onClick={() => …}` does not end the tag early) and tests the class string inside it. Four tests: **"no NEW training file speaks a second visual language"** — forbidden anywhere in the file: `/\b(bg|text|border|divide|ring|placeholder|hover:bg|hover:text|focus:border|focus:ring)-(zinc|sky|slate|neutral|gray|rose|red|green|violet|white|black)\b/` (the zinc/sky/white/black kit, `text-red-500` at `ProgressionView.tsx:296` and `PastPrograms.tsx:188`, `text-green-500` in the old logger), `hover:bg-accent` and `hover:text-accent-foreground` (the red flash — every hand-rolled button must be a `Button`), `text-\[(9|9\.5|10|10\.5|11|11\.5|12|12\.5|13)px\]` anywhere except exactly one `text-[11px]` in `trainingStyles.ts` (`GRID_CAPTION`), `emerald-` anywhere except `trainingStyles.ts`, a `tone=` / `tone ===` prop (the per-component second skin, `RunningPrograms.tsx:45,48,77,86`), `max-w-(xs|sm|md|lg|xl|\dxl)` (widths come from `TRAINING_COLUMN`; `max-w-full` and `max-w-[…]` are not widths of the column and are allowed); and, inside a control's opening tag, `(?<!sm:)\b(min-h-(7|8|9|10)|size-(4\.5|5|6|7|8|9|10)|h-(7|8|9|10))\b` (a control under 44 px on touch; `sm:` sizes are fine). **"the training-style debt list only shrinks"** — `TRAINING_STYLE_DEBT`, seeded on the day this lands with every file the first test fails on — **the executor derives it by running the test once and pasting its output, never by typing names**; run today (2026-09-17) with the rules above it is 25 files: `WorkoutPrograms.tsx`, `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx`, `EditActiveProgram.tsx`, `HistoryTab.tsx`, `LiftHistory.tsx`, `PastPrograms.tsx`, `ProgramEditor.tsx`, `ProgramPicker.tsx`, `ProgramsApp.tsx`, `ProgressTab.tsx`, `ProgressionView.tsx`, `RestTimer.tsx`, `RunningPrograms.tsx`, `SavedWeeks.tsx`, `SessionNotices.tsx`, `TodayCard.tsx`, `TodaySessionWidget.tsx`, `TrainingScreen.tsx`, `WeekStrip.tsx`, `live/AddLift.tsx`, `live/FinishSheet.tsx`, `live/LiveWorkoutScreen.tsx`, `live/RestBar.tsx`, `live/SetRow.tsx`, `ui.tsx` (`TrainingCard.tsx` is clean today and must not be listed) — and a file that becomes clean or is deleted must be removed or the test fails, the same shape as `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` (line 687) and its shrink test (line 749). **"no training input under 16 px on a phone"** — any `<input|<select|<textarea|<Input|<Textarea` opening tag in scope whose class string carries `text-(xs|sm|\[1[0-5](\.5)?px\])` without an `sm:`/`md:` prefix fails (design-system-04: `ui.tsx:175,261`, `HistoryTab.tsx:276`, `EditActiveProgram.tsx:182`, `CustomProgramBuilder.tsx:308,398,498,913,1036,1065`, `WorkoutPrograms.tsx:453,589` today; same debt list). **"green is only ever done"** — across scope, `DONE.` is imported only by `live/SetRow.tsx`, `live/RestBar.tsx`, `SessionNotices.tsx` (finished-program notice), `WeekStrip.tsx`, `ProgressTab.tsx` (trained-day dot), `WorkoutReceipt.tsx` (Phase 4b's receipt, "New best"), and `WorkoutPrograms.tsx` (spec S4 (c) "Finished — everything you logged is kept" — written as `DONE.text`, not the spec's literal `text-emerald-600`, so that the one-place rule holds; see Open question 8), and no file outside that list contains `DONE.`. The green tick in SetRow is a `<button>` and is meant to be — the rule is *which files*, not *which elements*. *Test:* the four titles above. All four pass on the day they land because of the debt list; the point of the list is that the number can only go down, and reverting any later cleanup fails the first test directly.

9. **Stop iPhone zoom at the root — without shrinking the boxes that are meant to be big.** `app/globals.css` · the `@media screen and (max-width: 767px) { input, textarea, select { font-size: max(16px, 1em) } }` rule (lines 242–246). Today: an element selector (specificity 0,0,1) loses to any Tailwind size class (0,1,0), so every `text-[12.5px]` box defeats it and Safari zooms on focus — 433 such boxes exist app-wide (321 of them under `src/goals`), which is why the fix belongs in the stylesheet and not in a per-file lint. Change: add `!important`, **but** guard it so the nine inputs the app deliberately makes large keep their size: `input:not([class~="text-lg"]):not([class~="text-xl"]):not([class~="text-2xl"]):not([class~="text-3xl"]), textarea:not(…same…), select:not(…same…) { font-size: max(16px, 1em) !important }` — without the guards, `QuickAddModal.tsx:202`, `FieldReportPage.tsx:1319`, `CustomReportBuilder.tsx:567,612`, `ExercisingPage.tsx:359`, `AreaDialog.tsx:125`, `V11ViewA.tsx:231`, `V11ViewB.tsx:798,1004` would drop from 18 px to 16 px on phones. Reason in a comment. The training lint of step 8 is the second net. Gated on **Open question 3**. *Test:* `tests/unit/shared/phoneInputZoom.test.ts` — "the phone input rule can no longer be beaten by a size class, and large boxes stay large" (reads `app/globals.css`; inside the 767 px media block the `font-size: max(16px, 1em)` declaration carries `!important` and the selector carries `:not([class~="text-lg"])`); plus `tests/e2e/programs-one-language.spec.ts` (step 15) — "no input on the training screens is under 16 px on a phone" (computed `font-size` of every `input, select, textarea` on /programs Today, History, and /programs/live ≥ 16 px at 390 px).

#### Part B — the sweep (run AFTER Phase 8)

10. **Delete the blue-grey kit.** `src/programs/components/ui.tsx` · `TYPE`, `IconButton`, `Segmented`, `Field`, `Stepper`, `Panel`, `Action`, `GroupLabel` — the whole file (329 lines). **New icons:** none, unless Open question 2 is answered no — then `Hourglass` (RestBar only) is listed for registry approval in place of the `Timer` line.

**No blockers.** Every command in this phase was run once against the tree as it stands (the greps, the tag scan, the iPhone-viewport measurement on the dev server); nothing needed an account, a device or a migration.

### Acceptance
- `tests/unit/shared/buttonVariants.test.ts` "outline and ghost buttons never paint the page background or the red accent" — the black-box Start and the red hover are gone from every screen in the app at once.
- `tests/unit/programs/trainingStyles.test.ts` "the training screens' widths, paddings and the colour of done come from one file" — width, card padding, chip, skeleton, failed line and green live in one place; a raw colour or a second width in that file fails.
- `tests/unit/shared/bottomSheet.test.tsx` "the More menu and a training menu are the same sheet" — one sheet component, rows 44 px, a real close button, Escape closes, the animation survives the move, the bar's own items reach 44 px and 12 px.
- `tests/unit/shared/stepper.test.tsx` "typing 1 on the way to 12 does not apply 1, and the box is 44 px with 16 px type" — the kit's one useful behaviour survives on the app's own Input/Button.
- `tests/unit/programs/programRow.test.tsx` "one row shape for every program list" — Running, Yours, Choose one and the Life Mastery card cannot drift apart.
- `tests/unit/shared/touchTargets.test.ts` "every shared control is 44 px on a phone" — Button, Input, Tabs, Segmented (timetrack), Stepper and the tab bar; reverting any one fails.
- `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` "context-locked icons are only imported where their role lives" — Timer cannot be reused in a new file silently again (registry line gated on Open question 2).
- `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` "no NEW training file speaks a second visual language", "the training-style debt list only shrinks", "no training input under 16 px on a phone", "green is only ever done" — the lint, tag-aware; by the end of Part B its debt list is deleted.
- `tests/unit/shared/phoneInputZoom.test.ts` "the phone input rule can no longer be beaten by a size class, and large boxes stay large".
- `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` "the blue-grey kit is gone" — `ui.tsx` does not exist and nothing imports it.
- `tests/unit/programs/tabsShareOneStyle.test.ts` "History, Progress and the lifts list use the shared heading, card and done classes".
- `tests/unit/goals/splitDesignerControls.test.ts` "the training-day rows are thumb-sized and readable".
- `tests/unit/navigation/sweepDebt.test.ts` "known debt never grows and never guards a page that is gone" — runs in `npm test`, not only in a sweep nobody schedules; `tests/e2e/sweep/route-sweep.spec.ts` "/programs holds up" on `sweep-phone` passes with no `TAP_TARGET_DEBT` entry.
- `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` "no training file is on any style debt list".
- `tests/e2e/programs-one-language.spec.ts` (training project, ignored by chromium, 390 × 844): "Today, History and Progress: every control is 44 px, no text under 12 px, exactly one orange per tab"; "the live screen: every control is 44 px, only the caption row is 11 px, one orange"; "no input on the training screens is under 16 px on a phone"; "nothing on the training screens is painted blue-grey or red on hover".
- Guards that must stay green throughout, and whose allowlists may shrink but never grow: `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` (all of it — including `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED`, `COMPONENTS_THAT_FETCH_THEIR_OWN_DATA`, `UNPAGED_READS_ALLOWED`, the icon registry and the new `CONTEXT_LOCKED_ICONS`), `tests/unit/navigation/routeReachability.test.ts`, `tests/unit/navigation/backNavigation.test.ts`, `tests/unit/writeCoverage.test.ts`, `tests/unit/navigation/sweepDebt.test.ts` (`KNOWN_DEBT`, `TAP_TARGET_DEBT`). `npm test` after every step, not at the end.

### Attack log
- **Wrong line numbers, seven places** — `tabs.tsx` TabsList/TabsTrigger are lines 16/31, not 174/189 (the file is 54 lines); `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` is at 687 not 671, `unreadableText()` at 732 not 736, the `ui.tsx` entry at 728 not ~699, the shrink test at 749; LiftHistory's `new Date()` is line 108 not 107; `SplitDesigner` starts at 538. Every one corrected against the file.
- **Step 14 described an allowlist that does not exist** — the draft said `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED` names ten training files including `TodaySessionWidget.tsx` and `TrainingCard.tsx`; it names six `src/programs` files plus `WorkoutPrograms.tsx`, `BuildYourOwn.tsx` and `RoutineCard.tsx`. Rewritten from the file.
- **Step 7 would have failed on the day it landed** — `CheckCircle2` is imported in nine files across eight slices, so locking it to `src/scenarios/` fails immediately; dropped (its stale app-wide role is a separate finding). `Timer`'s allowlist omitted `RestTimer.tsx`, which still imports it until Phase 5 deletes it — added, with the shrink rule; the `src/timetrack/` pattern matched nothing and is gone. The draft also invented a second registry mechanism (`SINGLE_ROLE_ICON_FILES`) beside the `allowedPathPatterns` one that already exists at `iconRoles.ts:159` and is enforced at `architecture.test.ts:1008` — reuse it.
- **Step 3 was not "verbatim" and would have lost the animation** — the More sheet's rows have no `min-h-11` (`py-3` only), its close X is a bare 20 px button, it has no `role=dialog` or Escape, and `@keyframes slideUp` lives in a `<style>` tag inside `MobileTabBar.tsx:138–146`, so an "extracted verbatim" sheet used from another file would carry a class that no longer exists. All four fixed in the step and asserted.
- **The tab bar makes the phase's headline claims false** — measured on `/dashboard/tracking` at 390 px: all six bar items are 43.75 px tall and 11 px. Phase 5 mounts the bar on /programs, so "zero tap debt on /programs" (step 13) and "no text under 12 px" (step 15) were unreachable as drafted. Fixed in step 3 (`min-h-11`, `text-xs`), which also lowers every other page's debt by six — gated on a new Open question 7 because it is a visible change to a shared bar.
- **Step 2 planted a test that fails for weeks** — its source scan for `max-w-`/`emerald-` would be red from day one until Phases 5/6 land, against "never leave a failing test". The scan moved into step 8's lint (which has a shrinking debt list); `trainingStyles.test.ts` now tests only the constants. The `max-w-` regex also caught `max-w-full` and `max-w-[calc(…)]` in `primitives.tsx`; narrowed to named sizes.
- **Step 8's control-size regex was line-based and would miss almost everything** — `className` is essentially never on the `<button` line in this codebase. Rewritten as an opening-tag scan (brace-aware so `onClick={() => …}` does not end the tag). Verified the approach with a throwaway scanner: 25 files fail today, not the 24 the draft guessed — `TrainingCard.tsx` is clean and was wrongly listed; `ProgramsApp.tsx`, `live/AddLift.tsx`, `ProgramPicker.tsx` fail and were missing. The instruction to derive the list by running the test stands; the list in the step is what that run produces today.
- **Step 8's "green is only ever done" test was unsatisfiable and half-meaningless** — "no `DONE` on a `<button` line inside `trainingStyles.ts`" tests a `.ts` file with no JSX, and the tick in SetRow *is* a button that goes green. Rewritten as a file allowlist; added `WorkoutReceipt.tsx` (Phase 4b's summary, "New best") and `WorkoutPrograms.tsx` (spec S4's finished-state emerald text), and surfaced the spec-vs-rule conflict on that literal as Open question 8.
- **Step 9's `!important` would have shrunk nine inputs the app makes large on purpose** — `max(16px, 1em)` reads the *parent's* size, so with `!important` a `text-lg` box (`QuickAddModal.tsx:202`, `FieldReportPage.tsx:1319`, `CustomReportBuilder.tsx:567,612`, `ExercisingPage.tsx:359`, `AreaDialog.tsx:125`, `V11ViewA/B`) drops to 16 px on phones. Guarded with `:not([class~="text-lg"])…`; a unit test on the stylesheet added so reverting fails without a browser. Scale of the class confirmed: 433 sub-16 inputs app-wide, so a lint could never carry it — the stylesheet is the right owner.
- **Step 13's ratchet was delivered to nobody** — a test inside the sweep spec runs only under `sweep-*`, which no CI job invokes (e2e.yml:30,73). The two tables move to `tests/support/sweepDebt.ts` and the ratchet becomes a Vitest test in `npm test`. Also: the draft re-measured two routes after changing Button/Tabs/Segmented/bar app-wide; "only shrinks" means every route must be re-measured. And a warning the executor needs: `/dashboard/tracking` measures 29 sub-44 controls on the dev server today against a budget of 6 — measure on the production build before lowering anything.
- **Step 15 said "copied … so the two specs share them"** — a copy is not sharing. The helpers move; `programs-live-workout.spec.ts` imports them and is now in `touches`. And the new spec had to be added to the `chromium` project's `testIgnore` as well as the `training` `testMatch`, or it runs in parallel against the shared account — the exact fault the brief warns about.
- **Step 12 listed half the slice** — `SplitDesigner` also has a `text-[10px]` picker header (545), `text-[11px]` preset pills (553), a `text-[10px] text-zinc-600` "cancel" (558), a `text-[10px] size-4.5` badge (584) and `text-zinc-600` separators in the footnote (626/631/633); the draft's own test would have failed on them. All enumerated and changed.
- **Step 11 missed a second `new Date()`** — `LiftHistory.tsx:131` formats stored dates for an `aria-label`; it is display, not "today", and is now stated rather than left for the executor to find.
- **Checked and held:** no date logic computes "today" anywhere in scope (whose-clock rule not triggered); no two facts stored apart — every constant has one owner (`trainingStyles.ts`, `sweepDebt.ts`, `CONTEXT_LOCKED_ICONS`); nothing writes rows; the only deletion (ui.tsx) is code, gated on importer count, not data; no RLS/auth/payments touched; the `dark:`-is-dead claim in step 1 verified against `globals.css:8` and a grep for any `dark` class writer (none); spec S2 tab sizing (`h-auto` list, `h-11 sm:h-8` trigger), S3 caption row 11 px exception, cross-cutting BottomSheet/ProgramRow/Stepper shapes, and the tinted-button token set all match the spec line by line; every step's file and symbol exist (`buttonVariants`, `TabsList`/`TabsTrigger`, `Segmented` in both files, `SEMANTIC_ICON_ROLES`, `CUSTOM_ICON_COMPONENTS`, `UNREADABLE_TEXT_ALLOWED`, `TAP_TARGET_DEBT`, `KNOWN_DEBT`, `staticRoutes`, `HistoryTab`, `ProgressTab`, `LiftHistory`, `SplitDesigner`, `MobileTabBar`) — grepped; `Hourglass` exists in the installed lucide; `Badge` has a `secondary` variant; jsdom + Testing Library are installed for the `.test.tsx` files.

### Open questions for Phase 9

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** 1. Fix the app's bordered ('outline') and transparent ('ghost') buttons once, for every screen? Today they paint themselves the page's black on a slate card and flash red on hover — that is why 'Start' on the Tracking card looked disabled. The fix is two lines in components/ui/button.tsx and changes every such button in the app (Settings, Goals, everywhere). The training card works without it (it uses an orange-tinted style). Yes or no?

*Recommendation:* Yes — it removes the class of fault, not the instance; the smoke and mobile-nav browser suites are run afterwards to confirm nothing else changed shape.

**Open question 2.** 2. May the stopwatch icon (Timer) be registered for a second job, 'rest countdown', on the live workout's rest bar? Today the registry allows it only for the Time-tracker tab, and the rules say a reused icon needs your yes. The alternative is a different glyph (an hourglass) for rest, which is then a new icon to approve instead.

*Recommendation:* Yes — a stopwatch on a rest countdown is what people expect; the new test then locks it to those files only.

**Open question 3.** 3. May the phone-only rule that keeps typed boxes at 16 px (so Safari does not zoom) be made unbeatable with '!important' in app/globals.css? Today any box styled smaller than 16 px defeats it and the page zooms when you tap it — 433 boxes across the app do this. The change applies to every input on screens under 768 px wide; the nine boxes the app deliberately makes large (quick-add, field report, report builder) are excluded so they stay large. The only visible effect is that small boxes get slightly bigger text on phones.

*Recommendation:* Yes — it is the one place that can fix the whole class; the training lint is the second net.

**Open question 4.** 4. On the live screen's rest bar the progress track turns green and says 'Ready when you are' when the rest is up (the spec keeps this). One finding (programs-today-10) calls that 'two accents in one bar'. Keep the green-when-done flip, or leave the bar orange throughout?

*Recommendation:* Keep the spec: green means 'done' and the rest is done; the phase's rule is 'green only ever means done', which this satisfies.

**Open question 5.** 5. History and Progress sections: this phase makes them the same slab of card the Today tab uses (one finding asks for that), rather than plain rule-separated sections (the other finding asks for that). Card everywhere, or flat everywhere?

*Recommendation:* Card everywhere — the Today tab in the agreed spec is card-based, and one section shape across the three tabs is the point.

**Open question 6.** 6. The Life Mastery page paints its own near-black ground (zinc) while the training block inside it now uses the app's slate cards. The spec accepts 'app tokens on the zinc shell'; one finding (design-system-08) suggests instead setting the app's colour variables (--card, --border, --muted) on the Life Mastery wrapper so the block blends in. Blend it in, or keep the spec's contrast?

*Recommendation:* Keep the spec for now (no wrapper override): it is one card on that page, and it must look the same card as on /programs. If it reads as a foreign patch when you see it, the wrapper override is a three-line change on NorthStarFlow.tsx:696 and touches no training file.

**Open question 7.** 7. The bottom tab bar (Dashboard · Training · Tracking · Scenarios · Time · More) is 43.75 px tall per item and its labels are 11 px on every page — a quarter-pixel and one pixel under the floors this phase promises. May it be brought to 44 px and 12 px? It is a visible change to a bar on every page of the app, though a very small one.

*Recommendation:* Yes — without it the Training page can never be measured clean once the bar is on it, and every other page's tap-target debt drops by six for free.

**Open question 8.** 8. The spec writes the Life Mastery card's 'Finished — everything you logged is kept' line as a literal green class (text-emerald-600). This phase's rule is that green exists in exactly one file and every screen borrows it from there. May that one line use the shared 'done' token instead of the literal? Same colour on screen, one owner in the code.

*Recommendation:* Yes — the spec fixes what the owner sees, not which file the class is typed in; the one-place rule is the whole point of this phase.


### Blockers for Phase 9

No blockers.


## Phase 10 — Proof: tests and CI that vouch for the three doors
**What you can do after:** Every push of this branch gets a real green or red check on GitHub — lint, types, unit tests, the database tests, and every browser project, none of them silently skipped. The browser tests that press Start on the Tracking card, on the Training page and from Life Mastery are written by Phases 2, 3 and 5; this phase is what makes them run in CI, on an account nobody uses by hand, with "today" taken from the account's own timezone, and it adds the proofs those phases do not carry (the database rules, the two-screens-agree check, the route sweep, the guards that keep all of it honest). If one of the three doors breaks, the check goes red before you find out in the gym.
**Why it is in this order:** Steps 1–5, 11 (guard half), 12 and 13 start on day one so every earlier phase lands on a branch that is checked; the browser additions (7, 8, 10) run after the phase whose screen they press has landed and been attacked.
**Findings this phase closes:** tests-and-ci-01, tests-and-ci-04, tests-and-ci-06, tests-and-ci-07, tests-and-ci-09, tests-and-ci-10, tests-and-ci-11, after-the-fact-logging-12, data-integrity-07, data-integrity-11, data-integrity-05 (ASK FIRST — a decision, not a step)

**Owned by other phases (listed there, not here, so no id is counted twice):** tests-and-ci-02, tests-and-ci-03 and tracking-card-e2e-never-taps-start — Phase 3 step 15 rewrites `tests/e2e/dashboard-training-card.spec.ts` with every S1 state, the double-tap and the unavailable state; tests-and-ci-08 — Phase 5 rewrites all three `mobile-training.spec.ts` cases (44 px cells, loose start, ticked sets in History); lm-templates-12 — Phase 2 steps 24–25 write `life-mastery-program-link.spec.ts` and re-point `openBuilder`. This phase makes those specs run on the training account (step 5) and adds what they lack (steps 7, 8, 11).

### What is true today (re-checked 2026-09-17 against the code; the 14 Sept findings are three days stale in places)
- **Lint is no longer red.** Commit c8d7a901 built the lint baseline the findings asked for: `scripts/lint-ratchet.mjs` + `scripts/lib/ratchet.mjs`, `eslint-baseline.json` (352 known errors, by file and rule), CI's Lint step is `npm run lint:ratchet` (`.github/workflows/ci.yml:29-30`), `.husky/pre-push` runs it. Run today: "Lint errors: 352, none new", exit 0. A "ratchet" is a gate that only lets a number go down. Still missing: no test imports `scripts/lib/ratchet.mjs`, so "may only shrink" is proven by nothing that runs in CI; and the baseline excuses 11 errors inside the training slice's own tests (`tests/unit/programs/builder.test.ts` 9, `tests/unit/programs/customLifts.test.ts` 2). Note for step 1: `assertCheckerRan` and `applyRatchet` do not throw — they call `process.exit(1)` (`ratchet.mjs:85, 89, 95, 99, 139, 155, 165`).
- **The branch is pushed but never checked.** `origin/training-rebuild` is level with local (0 ahead, 0 behind), 23 commits ahead of `main`. Both workflows trigger only on `main` and `beta` (`ci.yml:3-7`, `e2e.yml:3-7`). `gh` is not installed.
- **The database tests never run in CI.** `npm test` is `vitest run`, whose config excludes `tests/integration/**` (`vitest.config.ts:13`); `ci.yml` has no `test:integration` step. The 16 files under `tests/integration/db/` have never run anywhere but a laptop.
- **Ten Playwright projects run nowhere.** `sweep-desktop`, `sweep-phone`, `sweep-webkit`, `timetrack-sync`, `timetrack-sync-phone`, `toggl-webkit`, `toggl-firefox`, `toggl-iphone-safari`, `toggl-android`, `error-reporting` are defined (42 projects in `playwright.config.ts`; verified importable from vitest under `// @vitest-environment node`) and named in neither CI command. The two browser jobs have no `needs:` between them.
- **The test schema is 15 policies short, not 12.** Parsing every `CREATE POLICY` in `supabase/migrations/*.sql` (case-insensitively — `20260827000000_create_life_answers.sql:59-69` writes `create policy … on public.life_answers`) against tables `tests/integration/schema.sql` declares: 4 on `workout_logs`, 4 on `workout_sets`, 4 on `program_enrollments`, 3 on `life_answers` are missing. `finish_program_workout` is absent; `.rpc(` names in `src/db/*.ts` are `claim_beta_slot` (present), `finish_program_workout` (absent), `match_embeddings`, `match_embeddings_test` (pgvector, cannot be modelled). `workout_sets_weight_max` says `<= 1000` (`schema.sql:768`); production says `999.99` (`20260910090000`). `ProgressTab.tsx:227` already carries `data-testid="lift-bests"`; `:113` carries `week-dots`.
- **The orphan-tables migration is committed and applied** (d56d3ef7; `supabase migration list --linked`: 20260914120000 local = remote). Untracked in the tree today: `.main-check/` and `docs/plans/life-mastery-deployment.md` — another agent's; never commit them from this phase. This phase has no migration and pushes nothing.
- The four guards and the related unit tests are green today (`e2e-isolation`, `architecture`, `routeReachability`, `backNavigation`, `writeCoverage`, `shared/weight`, `programs/trainingCardState`: 58/58). Docker is available locally, so the integration suite runs here.
- **Other phases already own parts of the draft's scope** (read from their drafts): Phase 0 step 6 makes personal bests all-time (`/api/health/workout/bests`, `liftBests(logs, timezone)`); Phase 1 step 1 adds six `SECURITY INVOKER` program-write functions and copies them into `schema.sql`; Phase 1 pins each setup's timezone with `PUT /api/settings/time-preferences`; Phase 1 step 12 makes cleanups delete the live workout first; Phase 2 steps 24–25 write the Life Mastery link spec and re-point `openBuilder`; Phase 3 step 15 rewrites the Tracking card spec; Phase 4 step 1 changes `finish_program_workout`'s signature (`20260917120000_finish_workout_times_and_kind.sql`: `DROP FUNCTION` + three new parameters) and step 12 builds `tests/e2e/helpers/seedWorkout.ts` · `seedFinishedWorkout` and `tests/e2e/programs-past-workout.spec.ts` (replacing `health-past-workout.spec.ts`), re-seeding `programs-history-progress.spec.ts` through the live path; Phase 5 writes `programs-today.spec.ts` + `programs-catalog.spec.ts`, rewrites `mobile-training.spec.ts`, and removes `"/programs": 3` from the tap-target table. Phases 6–9 drafts were not available to read; their hooks are taken from the spec (S3 `set-row-n`/`tick-n`, S4 `lm-training-program`/`lm-training-unavailable`), see Blocker 3.

### Order of execution
Day one, before or alongside Phase 0: steps 1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 11, 12, 13. Step 6 (the after-the-fact guard) after Phase 4. Step 7 after Phases 3 and 5. Step 8 after the phase that builds S4 (Phase 8) has re-pointed Phase 2's spec. Step 10 after Phases 0, 4 and 5. A red run in 6–10 before its phase exists is that phase's failure, not this one's — never write the spec to today's screen to make it green. Later phases that touch a file this phase moved (the debt maps, the wipe helper) edit the new location.

Today: RLS ("row level security" — the database's own rule about whose rows a signed-in person may touch) is enabled in the test schema only on `beta_invites`, `beta_testers`, `waitlist_emails`, `program_drafts`; the three workout tables have neither `ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY` nor a `GRANT … TO authenticated`, so a "someone else is refused" test would pass for the wrong reason; `finish_program_workout` exists nowhere under `tests/`; `:768` says `<= 1000`; `weight.test.ts` reads the ceiling from the `20260305` migration only. copy `finish_program_workout` with its `REVOKE`/`GRANT` from the **latest** migration that defines it — Phase 4's `20260917120000` (three extra parameters) if landed, else `20260907110000:27-97`; change `:768` to `999.99`. *Test:* `tests/integration/db/workoutFinish.integration.test.ts` (through `asUser`, i.e. `set_config('test.uid', …)` + `SET ROLE authenticated` on one connection) — "finishing a workout twice is refused the second time, and the program moves once" (second call raises SQLSTATE 55000; `cursor->>'sessionCount'` is 1); "another signed-in person cannot finish your workout" (other uid → 55000 — RLS hides the row from the `FOR UPDATE` read; `ended_at` still null; enrollment unchanged); "a finish computed against an older program state is refused" (wrong `p_expected_session_count` → 55000; `ended_at` still null); "999.99 kg is the ceiling: it is accepted, and 1000 writes nothing" (999.99 inserted; 1000 refused — SQLSTATE 22003 numeric overflow, because `NUMERIC(5,2)` coerces before the CHECK runs, or 23514 if the engine ever evaluates the CHECK first — the test accepts either and asserts the row count is unchanged; the CHECK's own text is proven by the unit test below, since no value the column can hold is above 999.99). `tests/unit/shared/weight.test.ts` — "the integration schema says the same ceiling as the migrations" (fails today on 1000). `tests/unit/db/schemaMirror.test.ts` — "every policy production declares on a table the test schema also declares is in the test schema" (case-insensitive `create policy "<name>" on [public.]<table>` across `supabase/migrations/*.sql` minus any later `drop policy`, for every `<table>` with a `CREATE TABLE` in `schema.sql` — fails today on 15); "every database function the app calls by name exists in the test schema" (`.rpc("<name>"` in `src/db/*.ts` must have `FUNCTION <name>(` in `schema.sql`; `PGVECTOR_ONLY = new Set(["match_embeddings", "match_embeddings_test"])` is the exemption set, which only shrinks — fails today on `finish_program_workout`); "the latest definition of every CHECK constraint and every function named in both places matches" (per name, the last `ADD CONSTRAINT <name> CHECK (…)` / the last `CREATE [OR REPLACE] FUNCTION <name>(` … `$$;` block in migration order, whitespace-normalised, equals `schema.sql`'s — fails today on `workout_sets_weight_max`, and fails again the day Phase 4 changes the signature without updating the mirror, which is the point). Step 3 is what makes the integration file run in CI.

### ASK FIRST — data-integrity-05 (a decision only you can make; no step here writes a policy)
**Plain language:** anyone signed in to their own account can, with a few lines of code against the public API, set their own program's working weights, session counter and "finished" state directly, and add 999.99 kg sets to their own workouts, without going through the app's engine or its double-finish guard. Only their own account is affected, but the app would then show personal records, an estimated max, gym-session goal progress and streaks that were never earned. This is a security question: CLAUDE.md says every change here is asked first.
**The exact lines that allow it (verified today):**
- `supabase/migrations/20260618_create_program_tables.sql:41` — `CREATE POLICY "Users can update own enrollments" ON program_enrollments FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);` — no column restriction, so `exercise_state`, `cursor`, `replay_events`, `initial_exercise_state` are writable from the browser (read-only probe: `has_column_privilege('authenticated','program_enrollments','cursor','UPDATE')`).
- `supabase/migrations/20260305_create_health_tracking_tables.sql:87` — `CREATE POLICY "Users can update own workout logs" … FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = user_id);` — `ended_at`, `started_at`, `duration_min` settable directly.
- `supabase/migrations/20260305_create_health_tracking_tables.sql:113-114` — sets can be added to any own workout, finished or not.
- `supabase/migrations/20260907110000_finish_workout.sql:43` — `SECURITY INVOKER` (the function runs with the caller's own permissions): its checks bind only callers who use the function. Phase 1 step 1 adds six more functions of the same kind (`end_enrollment`, `start_enrollment`, `resume_enrollment`, `remove_session_and_replay`, …), so after Phase 1 every engine write already goes through a function — but each is still bypassable by a direct UPDATE.
**Two honest options.** (A) Treat the enrollment's computed columns as system-only, as `.claude/rules/database.md` says computed data must be: one migration on the pattern of `20260828140001_profiles_rls_hardening.sql:35-45` (`revoke update on program_enrollments from authenticated; grant update (custom_schedule, label, is_active, unit_system, bar_weight_kg) …`), and `finish_program_workout` plus Phase 1's write functions become `SECURITY DEFINER` (run with the database owner's permissions) with an explicit `user_id = auth.uid()` check inside each. Because Phase 1 already builds the functions, the cost is roughly one day: one migration, one keyword and one check per function, and one integration test per function in step 12's file. `workout_logs` / `workout_sets` stay own-row: a person's own ticked sets are typed-in claims, and the offline queue writes them one at a time. (B) Leave every policy as is and write down, in `scripts/auditRlsExpectations.ts` (step 13), that program state and workout rows are self-reported, so records, streaks and goal progress are the person's own claim.
**Recommendation:** (A), scoped to `program_enrollments`, done as the last step of Phase 1 (its functions) rather than here. Nobody writes it until you say yes; if you say (B), the sentence goes into the expectations file in step 13 and the finding closes as a decision.

### Attack log
- Steps 6 (Tracking card, ten tests), 8 (Life Mastery walk) and 9 (mobile-training) duplicated Phase 3 step 15, Phase 2 step 24 and Phase 5's mobile rewrite line for line, and coveredIds claimed five ids those phases list (tests-and-ci-02, -03, -08, tracking-card-e2e-never-taps-start, lm-templates-12). Removed; ids handed back; this phase keeps only what those specs lack (steps 7, 8 failed state) and the account/clock/guard work.
- Step 7 created `tests/e2e/programs-today.spec.ts`, the same filename Phase 5 creates with different tests, and none of its three tests proved anything Phase 3 + Phase 5 do not. Replaced by one test that opens both doors on one state — the walk §2 disagreement, which no phase tested across surfaces.
- Step 10 said bests become all-time in "Phase 7"; it is Phase 0 step 6. It also wrote its own seed and dates while Phase 4 owns `seedFinishedWorkout` and deletes the `POST /api/health/workout` route the draft seeded through. It invented `progress-bests` when `lift-bests` exists (`ProgressTab.tsx:227`). It asserted the History row by printed date, which HistoryTab prints in the browser's zone until Phase 7. It assumed a Monday week; the account has `week_start_day`. "not 60" would fail on a 1350 kg volume line; now `/\b60\b/`. It claimed the 400-day best shows 120 — with an all-time best the fixture's 130 is the best; fixed. Its "ceiling 4 → 0" ignored that three of the four sleeps live in other tests of the file; each is now named with its replacement.
- Step 1's test said `assertCheckerRan` "throws"; it calls `process.exit(1)` (`ratchet.mjs:85-99`), which would kill the vitest worker. The test now stubs `process.exit`.
- Step 12's "1000 kg refused by CHECK not overflow (23514)" would fail: `NUMERIC(5,2)` coerces the value before the CHECK runs, so Postgres raises 22003 — the 2026-09-10 migration says so itself. No value the column can hold exceeds 999.99, so the CHECK text is proven by the unit test, the column by the integration test. "Twelve policies" was fifteen (three lowercase `create policy … on public.life_answers`); the mirror parser is now case-insensitive and strips `public.`. Copying `20260907110000` "verbatim" would be stale the day Phase 4's `20260917120000` replaces the signature; the mirror copies the latest definition and a new test compares latest-definition text. `asUser` would have been a second copy of the SET-ROLE trick; moved to `tests/integration/setup.ts`.
- Step 4's unit test "no key starts with /programs" would have been red in `npm test` from day one until Phase 6 — a failing test left on purpose, which CLAUDE.md forbids. Rewritten as a ceiling that only goes to "absent". Also Phase 5 already removes `"/programs": 3`; the maps move day one so Phase 5's edit lands in the new file.
- Step 2's `needs:` alone would skip the cross-browser job whenever chromium is red, hiding results; added `if: ${{ always() }}`. The glob `goals-*` in the run line needs real expansion (`goals-tour` matches it); named `minimatch`. `training-setup` was not in any CI command; added to the chromium command and to the "may appear in both" set. The training secrets had no test; added.
- Step 5's "no machine clock" pattern (`new Date()` anywhere) would have failed Phase 3's legitimate `Date.now() + 7 h` stale fixture; narrowed to date-part construction and made the timezone reader a single owner (`time-preferences` appears nowhere else). The helper's wipe order now includes Phase 1 step 12's rule (live workout first, logs before ending). The setup now pins the account's zone the way Phase 1's setups do. `programs-drafts.spec.ts`'s wipe sits inside an evaluate that returns a result — said how to split it. `seedPastWorkout` dropped from the helper (Phase 4 owns seeding).
- Step 11 rewrote `openBuilder`, which Phase 2 step 25 already does, and asserted saved weeks on S2 rows that the S4 phase (Decision 5) will define; kept only the class fix (`redirectShims()` + the isolation test) and named the S4 phase as owner of the structured-builder assertions.
- The findings header listed lm-templates-12 and after-the-fact-logging-12 with steps that wrote tests for deleted components; after-the-fact-logging-12 now closes with a revert-detecting guard (step 6) instead of prose.
- The opening said this phase writes "browser tests that press Start on the Tracking card, on the Training page and from Life Mastery" — those are Phases 3, 5 and 2. Rewritten to what the owner will actually see from this phase.
- Data-integrity-05 option A was costed at "two days, four new functions"; Phase 1 step 1 already builds the write functions, so A is one keyword and one check per function plus a column revoke — about one day, and belongs at the end of Phase 1. Recommendation updated, still ASK FIRST.

### Open questions for Phase 10

Numbered as the steps above refer to them. Each carries a recommendation.

**Open question 1.** May the executor change both GitHub workflows to run on a push to ANY branch (plus a manual trigger), instead of only main and beta? The branch is on GitHub (origin/training-rebuild, level with local) but nothing has ever checked its 23 commits because the workflows do not fire for it. The alternative is a pull request to main, which cannot be opened from this machine (gh is not installed).

*Recommendation:* Yes — run on every branch. A branch that is never checked is the fault being fixed; narrow it to pull requests later if minutes hurt.

**Open question 2.** Steps 2 and 3 add roughly 25–40 minutes to every CI run: three route sweeps, the time-tracker on four engines, crash reporting, the database tests (Docker, which GitHub's runners have), and the two browser jobs running one after the other. Accept that on every push, or run the sweeps and time-tracker projects only on pull requests to main?

*Recommendation:* Accept the full run on every push for now. A guard that runs nowhere is worth nothing; trim later if the wall time hurts.

**Open question 3.** Two findings ask for tests of things the agreed design deletes: the catalogue's cards (tests-and-ci-06; S2 turns them into rows) and the two fill-in-afterwards forms (after-the-fact-logging-12; Decision 3 deletes both). Steps 6 and 7 are written to the spec — a guard that the forms stay deleted, and a test that the two doors agree. Confirm that is what you want.

*Recommendation:* Confirm. A test pinned to a screen being removed goes red for the wrong reason and gets ignored.

**Open question 4.** data-integrity-05 (security): should the program's computed columns — working weights, session counter, replay events, 'finished' state on program_enrollments — become writable only through the app's own database functions (a migration that revokes browser-side UPDATE on those columns and makes Phase 1's write functions plus finish_program_workout run as the database owner with a who-is-calling check, about one day), or stay as they are, with the app documenting that records, streaks and goal progress are self-reported?

*Recommendation:* Make them system-only (option A), scoped to program_enrollments, done at the end of Phase 1 where the functions are built; leave workout_logs and workout_sets own-row. Nobody writes the policy until you answer.

**Open question 5.** After Phase 5 makes every control on /programs, and Phase 6 every control on /programs/live, at least 44 px, the tap-target debt entries for those pages are deleted entirely (a page with no number must be clean) rather than lowered. If a phase leaves a control small, the sweep goes red rather than the number being raised. Agreed?

*Recommendation:* Agreed — route-sweep.spec.ts already says a number may never be raised; deleting the entry is the same rule applied honestly.


### Blockers for Phase 10

1. **A third test account for the automated training specs (TEST_USER_TRAINING_EMAIL / TEST_USER_TRAINING_PASSWORD) does not exist as far as I can tell, and creating a login is an auth action the rules say to ask about first. Until it exists, step 5's training-setup project cannot log in and every training spec fails loudly — nothing wipes the wrong account, but nothing runs either, and steps 7, 8 and 10 cannot be proven.** *Attempted:* Read tests/e2e/fixtures/test-user.ts (only TEST_USER_* and TEST_USER_B_* exist) and both login setups; the permission system refused reading .env.local, so I cannot confirm a third account is absent from the local env either; `which gh` finds nothing and no GitHub token is available, so repository secrets cannot be set from here. tests/e2e/signup-flow.spec.ts shows accounts can be created through the product's own signup without touching the database. *Needs from you:* 1. Yes/no: may the executor create test-user-training@daygame-coach-test.local through the app's own signup on localhost, pin its timezone to Europe/Copenhagen (the setup does this with PUT /api/settings/time-preferences, the way Phase 1 pins the other two accounts), and add the two variables to .env.local? 2. Add TEST_USER_TRAINING_EMAIL and TEST_USER_TRAINING_PASSWORD as GitHub repository secrets (Settings → Secrets and variables → Actions), or hand the executor a token that can. *Recommendation:* Yes to both. Until the secrets exist in GitHub, run the training projects locally only; step 5's unit guard already fails if a training spec is pointed at any other account.

2. **Steps 6, 7, 8 and 10 press screens and helpers that Phases 0, 2, 3, 4, 5 and the S4 phase build (test ids today-card, also-running, lm-training-unavailable, lift-bests all-time; seedFinishedWorkout; the training-account helper). Written before those land, the specs fail for a reason unrelated to what they prove.** *Attempted:* Read the current code and the Phase 0–5 drafts: TrainingCard.tsx has no test id on its error branch and returns null while loading; StartWorkoutSchema has no startedAt; app/programs/ contains only page.tsx and live/page.tsx; ProgressTab still fetches days=365; Phase 4's seedWorkout.ts does not exist yet. *Needs from you:* Nothing to decide — ordering only: run steps 1–5, 11, 12 and 13 immediately; run each of 6, 7, 8, 10 after the phase it names has landed and been attacked. *Recommendation:* Land the CI, lint, isolation, schema and audit steps on day one so every later phase is checked; hold each browser addition until its surface exists, and treat a red run there as the earlier phase's failure, not this one's.

3. **The drafts for Phases 6, 7, 8 and 9 were not in the scratchpad (only runA-P0–P5 and phase0.md exist), so this phase's dependencies on them — S3's set-row-n / tick-n ids and the /programs/live tap targets (Phase 6), HistoryTab printing dates in the account's zone (Phase 7), S4's lm-training-program / lm-training-unavailable ids and the re-pointing of Phase 2's Life Mastery spec to S4's door (Phase 8) — are asserted against the design spec, not against a draft. If those phases name the ids differently, steps 4, 8 and 10 fail for a naming reason.** *Attempted:* Listed the scratchpad (ls) and grepped every runA-*.md and phase0.md for the ids and terms; no Phase 6–9 file exists to read. *Needs from you:* Have the assembler run the cross-phase coverage check with Phases 6–9 included before this phase is executed, and confirm those phases keep the spec's ids (S3 §(b)9, S4 §(b)2 and §(c)) and that the S4 phase owns re-pointing Phase 2 step 24 (it presses 'Start tracking this' inside Life Mastery, which S4 deletes). *Recommendation:* Bind every phase to the spec's ids as written; where a later draft renames one, the later draft changes, not the spec.
