# Part 3 — The design spec (what Phases 3, 5, 6 and 8 build)

## Training surfaces — final spec

Synthesised from three proposals and two judges' scores. Winners: **S1 door-first**, **S2 door-first**, **S3 door-first**, **S4 status card (one-kit's shape, door-first's one-representation rule)**. Every graft and every disagreement is resolved inline and marked *Resolved:*. Facts below were re-checked against the repo today, not taken from the proposals.

### What changes, in plain words

1. **The Tracking page always shows a training card.** Today it shows nothing when no program is running (the complaint). Now it shows what today is and one button that does the obvious thing: start, resume, finish, or see what you did.
2. **Training's Today tab is today's workout, not an inventory.** One card, one orange Start button, the week at the top. Programs (pick, change, end, build) move to their own screen behind a menu.
3. **The live workout screen becomes a real tracker.** Hevy-shaped set rows (SET · PREVIOUS · KG · REPS · ✓), a menu per lift with swap / reorder / warm-up / rest / history, swipe to delete, editable start and end time on finish, and one summary that is the same thing the history shows.
4. **Life Mastery stops hosting a second copy of the catalogue.** Its 12,000 px step gets a small status card that links to the one Programs screen.
5. **One visual language.** Everything is built from the app's own buttons, cards and tabs (`components/ui`). The blue-grey "kit" (`src/programs/components/ui.tsx`) and the green Life Mastery buttons on training surfaces are removed, not repainted.

### Cross-cutting decisions (apply to every surface)

| Rule | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| Source of "today" | One widened pure function `trainingCardState()` (programsService.ts L1930) feeds **both** the Tracking card and the /programs Today card. Gains kinds `unavailable`, `done`, `finished`; fields `lifts: string[]`, `endurance?`, `also[]`, `lastTime?`, `doneToday?`, `setsTicked?`. Takes all enrollments, today's logs, the live workout, the account's timezone. | The two screens disagreed about today (walk §2). One owner of the rule. |
| Which program the card shows | New unit-tested `chooseCardEnrollment()` (graft from tracker-first): among running programs with a non-rest session today, the most recently trained; ties → earliest started; never-trained only when nothing else qualifies; never `enrollments[0]`. | The rule is a tested owner, not prose. |
| Today's date | From the profile timezone (account B is Europe/Copenhagen), never the browser clock. Acceptance: a Monday 23:45 Copenhagen workout reads "Trained today"; Tuesday 00:10 reads "Workout B". | A 23:30 workout must count as today. |
| The one orange | Exactly one solid-orange (`bg-primary`) control per screen. Tracking: "Start Session". /programs Today: "Start workout". Live: "Finish". Life Mastery step: its own violet step button (unchanged). Programs screen: only ProgramDetail's "Start …". | Hierarchy. |
| The training card's button | Tinted-primary: `Button variant="outline" className="w-full border-primary/50 bg-primary/10 text-primary hover:bg-primary/15 hover:text-primary"` — the token set TodayCard's active day chip already uses. Keep testids `training-card-start` / `training-card-resume`. | Obviously tappable, not a second orange. Hover override is required because outline/ghost hover is `--accent` = sunset red. |
| Root-cause button fix (needs your yes, Decision 1) | `components/ui/button.tsx` `outline` → `border border-border bg-transparent hover:bg-muted/50`; `ghost` hover → `bg-muted/50`. Verified: `app/layout.tsx:68` renders `<html lang="en">` with no `dark` class, so the `dark:` variants never apply and outline renders `bg-background` #121212 on a #1f2937 card — the black "Start" in screenshot 23. | Fixes every outline/ghost button in the app at once. The tinted card button works with or without this. |
| Widths | `/programs` and `/programs/live` both `max-w-2xl px-4` (live already is; TrainingScreen changes 3xl→2xl). Tracking stays 6xl, Life Mastery 5xl. Width changes only at route boundaries. | Start must not jump width. |
| Tab bar | `<MobileTabBar/>` mounted on `/programs` (it is the bar's own "Training" destination); not on `/programs/live` (RestBar owns the fixed bottom slot). | Tapping Training landed on a page with no tabs. |
| Card padding | `<Card className="gap-0 py-0">` + inner `px-4 py-4` everywhere a Card is used for training (one-kit's sizing). | Card carries `py-4 sm:py-6 gap-4`; adding `p-4` doubles it. |
| Tabs sizing | `<TabsList className="grid h-auto w-full grid-cols-3 p-1">`, `<TabsTrigger className="h-11 sm:h-8">`. | TabsList is `inline-flex h-11 p-1`; triggers render ~36 px unless sized. |
| BottomSheet | New `components/BottomSheet.tsx`, extracted verbatim from MobileTabBar's More sheet (`components/MobileTabBar.tsx:79–130`: `fixed inset-0 z-50`, backdrop `bg-background/60 backdrop-blur-sm`, panel `bg-card rounded-t-2xl border-t border-border pb-safe animate-slide-up`, rows `min-h-11 px-3 py-3 rounded-lg text-sm font-medium`). MobileTabBar switches to it. At ≥640 px it renders as a centred `Dialog`. | Four menus need it; no shadcn Sheet/DropdownMenu exists in the repo. |
| ProgramRow | New shared row: `Link/button.flex.min-h-14.items-center.justify-between.px-4` › `font-medium` name + `text-xs text-muted-foreground` meta + right slot (Badge / text / ⋮) + ChevronRight. Used by Running, Catalogue, Finished, Saved weeks, and the Life Mastery card. | One row shape, four lists. |
| Stepper | New `components/ui/stepper.tsx`: `Button variant="outline" size="icon-sm"` Minus / `Input className="h-11 w-14 text-center"` / Plus. Only shape the kit had that shadcn lacks. | Kit Stepper is 36 px. |
| Type floors | Body ≥ `text-sm` (14), meta `text-xs` (12), column captions `text-[11px]` only on the live set grid. Nothing at 10 px. | Constraint. |
| Honest states | Every surface: loading = `h-[92px] rounded-xl bg-muted/40 animate-pulse` block; failed = amber `AlertTriangle` line + Try again with a `*-unavailable`/`*-failed` testid (StatTile's pattern); empty; ready. No uncomputable number is ever 0 — it is omitted or "—" with "not counted". | House pattern. |
| Prerequisites that are not UI | (a) `/programs/workout/[id]` receipt page does not exist yet (`app/programs/` has only `page.tsx` and `live/`) — Phase 4b is a prerequisite for every "See today's workout" state. (b) `StartWorkoutSchema` has `enrollmentId`, `dayId`, `clientKey` — add `startedAt`. (c) `FinishWorkoutSchema` has `endedAt`, `durationMin` — add `startedAt`, guard ≤599 min on the span. (d) `WorkoutAdjustments` has `skipped/incomplete/swapped/added/order` — add `rest?: Record<exerciseId, seconds>`, saved through the existing `adjust()`. (e) `/api/workouts/lifts` has no caller and returns kg — wire it and convert. (f) `ProgramsApp` view is component state (`view.mode`), not URL — `?view=` / `?program=` / `?tab=` is new work. (g) `set_kind` CHECK is `warmup, working, amrap, backoff, drop` (migration 20260907100000 L159) — no "failure". | Listed so nothing is postponed silently. |

### S1. The training door on the Tracking page

#### (a) Layout, 390 px — between SeasonBand and StatTileGrid, always rendered after load

```
┌ Card mb-6 (bg-card border-border rounded-xl) px-4 py-4 ──────┐
│ 🏋 TRAINING                          StrongLifts 5×5 ›      │  text-xs uppercase muted · Link text-xs muted → /programs
│ Workout A                                                   │  text-lg font-semibold leading-snug
│ Squat · Bench Press · Barbell Row                           │  text-sm muted, one line, truncate
│ Last time Fri · all 15 sets                                 │  text-sm muted — absent when unknown
│ ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐│
│ │ ▶  Start workout                                         ││  tinted button, h-11, w-full (THE button)
│ └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘│
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  ≈ 176 px; ≤ 200 px in every state

no program, no workout (renders NOTHING today):
│ 🏋 TRAINING                                                 │
│ No program running                                          │
│ Start a workout and add lifts as you go, or pick a program. │
│ [ ▶  Start a workout ]                                      │
│ Pick a program ›                                            │  Link text-sm text-primary, min-h-11

workout running (card gets border-primary/50):
│ 🏋 TRAINING                          StrongLifts 5×5 ›      │
│ Workout A in progress · 23 min                              │
│ 6 of 15 sets ticked                                         │
│ [ Resume ]                                                  │

could not load:
│ 🏋 TRAINING                                                 │
│ ⚠ Could not load your training.                             │  amber, testid training-card-unavailable
│ [ Open training ]                                           │
```

#### (b) Elements
1. Card — `<Card className="mb-6 gap-0 py-0" data-testid="training-card">` › `div.px-4.py-4.space-y-2`; `border-primary/50` in live, `border-amber-500/40` in stale/unavailable. Same component mounts in HealthTrackingPanel.
2. Skeleton — `<div data-testid="training-card-loading" className="mb-6 h-[92px] rounded-xl bg-muted/40 animate-pulse">` until enrollments **and** `/api/workouts/live` have both answered.
3. Label row — `flex items-center justify-between`; `span.text-xs.uppercase.tracking-wide.text-muted-foreground` with `Dumbbell size-3.5` "Training" (registered role: workout logging entry point); right `Link.text-xs.text-muted-foreground.truncate` naming the **chosen** program (`chooseCardEnrollment`) + " ›" → `/programs?program=<id>`; hidden in none/unavailable.
4. Headline — `p.text-lg.font-semibold.leading-snug` — copy per state table.
5. Context — `p.text-sm.text-muted-foreground.truncate` — lift names joined " · "; endurance blocks described ("4 × 4 min run / 3 min walk · about 30 min"); never a lift count on endurance days.
6. Second context — `p.text-sm.text-muted-foreground` — "Last time Fri · all 15 sets" only when a log for this day exists; with 2+ programs: "Also today: Run 3 · Sprint Triathlon ›" (Link → `/programs?program=<id>`) or "Also running: X, Y" (Link → `/programs?view=programs`).
7. THE button — tinted `Button` (cross-cutting rule), `Play` icon only when it starts something, `Loader2` while starting. Behaviour per state. Start: POST `/api/workouts` `{enrollmentId, dayId?, clientKey: startKeyFor(enrollmentId|null)}` → `router.push("/programs/live?from=/dashboard/tracking")` so the live BackLink and summary return here.
8. Start error — `p.text-sm.text-destructive role=alert` "Could not start that workout." + Link "Open training"; button re-enabled.
9. Secondary link (none / finished / done states) — `Link.min-h-11.text-sm.text-primary.hover:underline` (RecentSessionsCard "View All" style).
10. Unavailable — `p.flex.items-center.gap-2.text-sm.text-amber-600.dark:text-amber-400` with `AlertTriangle size-4`, `data-testid="training-card-unavailable"`.

#### (c) States
| State | Headline | Context | Button → does |
|---|---|---|---|
| loading | — | 92 px pulse | none |
| unavailable (either fetch failed) | — | "Could not load your training." | "Open training" → /programs. Never a Start (it could collide with an open workout) |
| none | "No program running" | "Start a workout and add lifts as you go, or pick a program." | "▶ Start a workout" → loose start → live. Link "Pick a program ›" → `?view=programs` |
| today | "Workout A" | lift names; last-time line | "▶ Start workout" → start → live |
| endurance day | "Run 2" | blocks + "about 40 min" | "▶ Start workout" |
| rest day | "Rest day" | "Next is Workout B on Wed" (weekday only when anchored) | "▶ Start Workout B early" → POST with `dayId` (schema already accepts it). *Resolved:* door-first's one tap beats tracker-first's three |
| done today | "Trained today" | "Workout A · 45 min · 14 sets" (missing numbers omitted) + "Next is Workout B on Wed" | "See today's workout" → `/programs/workout/[id]`. Link "Start another workout ›" (loose). *Resolved:* the door never re-offers Workout B as if today were untouched |
| live ≤ 6 h (`STALE_WORKOUT_HOURS`) | "Workout A in progress · 23 min" | "6 of 15 sets ticked" (count omitted if unreadable) | "Resume" → live. Border-primary/50, still tinted (one orange per page) |
| stale > 6 h | "Monday's Workout A is still open" | "Started Mon 18:02 · 3 of 15 sets ticked" | "Finish or discard it" → live (opens with the time row expanded) |
| 2–3 programs | chosen program's session | + "Also today:" / "Also running:" link | one button for the shown session |
| program finished, none else due | "StrongLifts 5×5 is finished" | "47 sessions · started 3 Feb" | "Pick what's next" → `?view=programs`. Link "Start a workout ›" (loose). *Resolved:* door-first lost the loose door here; restored |
| start failed | unchanged | destructive line | re-enabled |

#### (d) Deleted
- `if (state.kind === "none" && !liveUnknown) return null` and `if (loading) return null` in TrainingCard.tsx.
- `variant="outline"` black-box Start, the solid-orange Resume and the comment justifying it.
- `enrollments[0]`-only logic; the rest-day sentence with no button; `CardContent className="p-4"` double padding.
- Quick Actions sixth row "Training →" (QuickActionsCard.tsx) — a duplicate door 2,800 px below the real one (Decision 2).

#### (e) Acceptance (Playwright, 390×844)
- Account A (no program, no workout): `[data-testid=training-card]` count = 1, within the first 900 px, contains "No program running" and exactly one `button` with text "Start a workout"; text "Start a workout" does not appear elsewhere on the page.
- Every state: card contains exactly 1 `button`; its height ≥ 44, width ≥ 300; computed background ≠ #121212 and ≠ #1f2937; page has exactly one element with computed background rgb(255,107,53) and its text is "Start Session".
- Card height ≤ 200 px in every state; no text inside with computed font-size < 12 px.
- After finishing Workout A (account B): card contains "Trained today", not "Workout B" as a headline; button text "See today's workout".
- Rest-day fixture: contains "Rest day" and "Next is"; button text starts "Start Workout"; clicking lands on /programs/live with that session's name in the header.
- Three programs: card count = 1, one button, contains "Also today:" or "Also running:".
- Endurance fixture: no text matching `/\b0 (lifts?|sets?|kg)\b/`.
- `/api/programs/enrollments` mocked 500: `[data-testid=training-card-unavailable]` visible, no button text matches `/^Start/`, text "No program" absent. Delayed 2 s: `[data-testid=training-card-loading]` visible first, height 92.
- Open workout started 7 h ago: contains "still open" and "Finish or discard".
- Timezone: 23:45 Copenhagen Monday workout → Monday reads "Trained today", Tuesday 00:10 reads next session.
- Quick Actions contains no row with text "Training".

#### (f) Desktop
Same max-w-6xl slot; at `sm` the inner div becomes `sm:flex sm:items-center sm:justify-between sm:gap-6`, text left, button `sm:w-auto sm:min-w-[200px]` right — the page header's own "Start Session" arrangement, ≈ 96 px tall.

#### New icons
None. Dumbbell (registered), Play, AlertTriangle, Loader2 (utility).

#### (b) Elements

Shell — `div.mx-auto.max-w-2xl.px-4.pb-tab-bar.pt-4` data-testid `training-screen`, `<MobileTabBar/>` at the end. *Resolved:* the one-line masthead stays (TrainingScreen.tsx documents why the 184 px one was removed); the "part of your Life Mastery plan" aside is deleted from the h1 and becomes a text link on the Programs screen.

Week strip — inside the card (one-kit graft): `div[data-testid=week-strip].grid.grid-cols-7.gap-1`; cells `button.h-11.rounded-md.text-xs` (anchored) or `div` (unanchored); weekday `text-xs uppercase muted`; dot `size-2 rounded-full`: `bg-emerald-500` trained this week, `ring-1 ring-primary` today, `bg-muted-foreground/30` to come. No legend, no "Runs in order" sentence, no "done" word. `trainedWeekdays` filtered to this week (already done).

Title row — `p.text-lg.font-semibold` day label + `Button variant="ghost" size="sm"` "Change day ▾" revealing chips `Button variant="outline" size="sm" aria-pressed` (active `border-primary/50 bg-primary/10 text-primary`); only for multi-day programs. *Resolved:* chips collapsed (one-kit) — shrinks the card most.

THE button — `<Button size="lg" className="w-full" data-testid="start-workout"><Play/>Start workout</Button>`; POST with `dayId` + `startKeyFor`; live → `Resume · 23 min` (testid `resume-workout`, still default variant — it is this page's primary); stale → "Finish or discard Monday's workout"; another program's workout open → `variant="outline"` "Finish the workout you have open first" → live; done today → `variant="outline"` "See today's workout" + text link "Train again". Pushes `/programs/live?from=/programs`.

Also-running — `h2.text-xs.uppercase.tracking-wide.text-muted-foreground` "Also running" + `<Card data-testid="also-running" className="gap-0 py-0"><ul className="divide-y">` of ProgramRow buttons; tap swaps the session card and writes `?program=<id>` (tracker-first graft, so S1 and S4 can deep-link); shown only with 2+ enrollments.

Programs screen — `data-testid="programs-screen"`; BackLink fallback /programs "Training"; `h1.text-lg.font-semibold` "Programs"; sections `h2.text-xs.font-semibold.uppercase.tracking-wide.text-muted-foreground` Running / Yours / Choose one; each list `<Card className="gap-0 py-0"><CardContent className="divide-y p-0">` of ProgramRow. Yours = saved weeks (`/api/programs/drafts`) + PastPrograms rows with ⋮ → "Start again" / "Delete" (Dialog: "Delete StrongLifts 5×5? Its 47 sessions stay in your history and stop counting towards this program."; separate `resumingId` / `deletingId`). Choose one = catalogue as ProgramRow with `Badge variant="secondary"` level letters; discipline choice = `Tabs` with `TabsList className="flex w-full overflow-x-auto hide-scrollbar h-auto p-1"`; tapping a row opens ProgramDetail (unchanged, already shadcn; its Start is the flow's one orange), which gains the repainted ProgramEditor + seeded weights as Label+Input so "change it before you start" survives (S4). Last row dashed "+ Build your own" → `?view=build`.
19. Load-failed strip — existing `training-load-failed` amber alert; Try again becomes `Button size=sm variant=outline`.
20. History / Progress tabs — Phase 4 and Phase 5 as planned (sticky month header, Calendar toggle, lift Filter, rows min-h-14 → `/programs/workout/[id]`, no bin on rows; lazy; existing testids).

#### (c) States
| State | What shows |
|---|---|
| server `failed` | amber alert + outline Try again; last-known card stays; never the empty state; no Start |
| client refetch loading | session card → 92 px pulse |
| none | empty-state card, one orange, "Pick a program ›" row; loose workout open → "Resume · 12 min" |
| one program, due | full card; one orange |
| rest day | title "Rest day", "Next is Workout B on Wed", chips open by default under "Train anyway?", Start reads "Start Workout B early" |
| done today | outline "See today's workout" + "Train again" link; today's dot emerald |
| live / stale / other program open | Resume · N min / Finish or discard … / outline "Finish the workout you have open first" |
| endurance day | block rows, "about 40 min", Start |
| layoff | amber notice above lifts |
| program finished | emerald notice with outline "End it — I am done" / "Run it again"; no Start |
| several programs | chosen card + Also running rows; one orange |
| Programs screen: Running empty → "Nothing running."; Yours empty → hidden; catalogue failed → amber row + Try again (each section independent) |

#### (d) Deleted
- Kit `Segmented` tab strip; the "Anything else" tab.
- `<details>` "Log a workout you already did" + `TodaySessionWidget.tsx` ("Did it exactly as shown? Just save.", "I did all of this — save it") + `RestTimer.tsx` (its only user).
- `WorkoutLogger.tsx` from /programs. It is also mounted in `HealthTrackingPanel.tsx` and `app/test/health/page.tsx` — Decision 3 covers those.
- `StartLooseWorkout.tsx` card (three mounts, two extra oranges).
- Inventory-as-Today (three chevron cards); "Your programs" h2 + ghost "+ Browse"; "‹ All programs".
- ProgressionView's button row and History disclosure from Today (Skip/Reset/End → sheet; sparklines stay in Progress).
- `EditActiveProgram` from Today (→ `?view=edit`, repainted).
- WeekStrip's "Runs in order…" sentence, 10 px weekday, "done" word, legend.
- ProgramCatalog's 13 orange "View ›" buttons and card grid; PastPrograms' 24 px buttons and shared `busy`.
- "· part of your Life Mastery plan" in the h1; `max-w-3xl`; the comment claiming the route never mounts a tab bar.
- `src/programs/components/ui.tsx` once S4's importers are gone.

#### (e) Acceptance
- One program: first `p.text-lg` inside `[data-testid=today-card]` is the day label; `[data-testid=start-workout]` is the only element with computed background rgb(255,107,53); height ≥ 44; top < 700 px.
- `[role=tablist]` width = container width (358 ± 2); exactly 3 `[role=tab]`, each height ≥ 44; no text "Anything else".
- DOM contains none of: "I did all of this", "Did it exactly as shown", "Day (blank = today)", "Start a workout now", "Browse programs", "All programs"; "Start an empty workout" appears at most once.
- `[data-testid=mobile-tab-bar]` present on /programs at 390, absent at 1280 and absent on /programs/live; Training item `aria-current=page`.
- Week strip: 7 cells ≥ 44 px; account A (144 old workouts) shows 0 emerald dots before training this week; after finishing, today's cell has one; no text < 12 px.
- `document.body.scrollHeight` on first paint, one 3-lift program, ≤ 1,100 px.
- Every `button, a[href], [role=tab]` on the Today tab ≥ 44 px tall.
- Three programs: `[data-testid=today-card]` count 1; `[data-testid=also-running] button` count 2; clicking one changes the day label and the URL gains `?program=`; orange count stays 1.
- No program: `[data-testid=start-loose-workout]` is the only orange; "Pick a program" row → `/programs?view=programs` → `[data-testid=programs-screen]` with h2s Running / Yours / Choose one.
- Programs screen: 0 orange buttons; 13 catalogue rows each ≥ 56 px; 0 buttons with text "View".
- `[data-testid=program-menu]` opens a sheet with rows Change this program, Skip this session, Reset to start, All programs, End program, each ≥ 44; End opens a `[role=dialog]`.
- Start again (2 s mocked latency): no element ever contains "Deleting".
- `[data-testid=log-past-workout]` opens a dialog with `input[type=datetime-local]`; yesterday 18:00 → /programs/live and `workout_logs.started_at` = yesterday 18:00 in the profile timezone.
- Rest day: "Rest day" and "Next is" present; one orange whose text contains "early".
- Refetch mocked to fail: `[data-testid=programs-load-error]` present and the previous card still on screen.

#### (f) Desktop
Same max-w-2xl column centred; tab bar hidden ≥ 640 px; triggers `sm:h-8`; catalogue rows stay single-column (a list reads best narrow); BackLink is the way back.

#### New icons
- `MoreVertical`, `ChevronRight`, `Trash2` — utility.
- `Calendar` for the History calendar toggle — registered for "date display / date pickers"; new context, ask (Decision 8).
- `History` and `SkipForward`/`RotateCcw` leave with ProgressionView; sheet rows carry no icons.

#### (b) Elements
1. Header — structure kept; BackLink `text-sm min-h-11` honouring `?from`; title `text-base font-semibold truncate`; elapsed `text-sm tabular-nums text-muted-foreground` as mm:ss (*Resolved:* a rest clock beside "0 min" looks broken); `Button size="sm" data-testid="finish-workout"` = the one orange. `app/programs/live/page.tsx` L46: a self-built week shows its own name, never "Your own program".
2. Lift card — `<Card className="gap-0 py-0" data-testid="lift-{id}">` › `div.px-3.py-2.5.space-y-1`; `opacity-60` + collapsed body when skipped.
3. Name — `button.min-h-11.text-left.text-base.font-semibold.truncate` → lift history sheet. Superset tag `rounded bg-primary/15 px-1.5 text-xs uppercase text-primary`.
4. Sub line — `p.text-xs.text-muted-foreground` describeSets + " · rest 3:00" (reflects `adjustments.rest`) / "added on the day" / "· skipped".
5. ⋮ — `Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="hover:bg-muted/50" aria-label="Options for Squat"` `MoreVertical`. (MoreHorizontal was in one file only, so the "unregistered drift" claim was overstated; MoreVertical is utility regardless.)
6. Lift menu — BottomSheet `data-testid="lift-menu"`. Rows: **Swap this lift…** (AddLift search inline in the sheet; writes `adjustments.swapped[fromId] = {name, libraryId}` — the shape that exists; ticked sets stay logged under the old lift and show collapsed "2 × 100 kg × 5 logged" above the new rows). **Move up / Move down** (`adjustments.order`, disabled at the ends; *Resolved:* rows, not a drag handle). **Add a warm-up set** (inserts `kind: "warmup"` row above set 1 with **both inputs blank** — *Resolved:* door-first's "50 % of set 1" invents a number; nothing prescribed → nothing pre-filled). **Rest after this lift** — inline `span.tabular-nums` + two `Button variant=outline size=icon-sm` Minus/Plus in 15 s steps, writes `adjustments.rest[exerciseId]` through `adjust()` so it survives the reload every phone lock causes (one-kit graft); caption "(our suggestion)" until edited. **Last times for Squat** → history sheet. **Skip this lift / I did do this one** (existing). **Remove** (`Trash2`, added lifts only).
7. Lift history sheet — BottomSheet; reuses `LiftHistory.tsx`'s row rendering (`show()` unit conversion, `Sparkline`) with data from `/api/workouts/lifts` filtered to one lift (start from the existing component, do not build a new one): 5 rows `min-h-11` "Fri 12 Sep · 5 × 100 kg × 5", `text-xs muted` "Best: 105 kg × 5"; loading (3 × `h-11 animate-pulse bg-muted/40`), failed (amber "Could not load your past sets."), empty ("Nothing logged for this lift yet.").
8. Caption row — `grid grid-cols-[2.75rem_4.5rem_1fr_1fr_2.75rem] gap-2 px-1 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider text-muted-foreground`: SET · PREVIOUS · KG/LB/SEC · REPS · blank. *Resolved:* PREVIOUS (Hevy's name) fits 72 px at 11 px uppercase; the 11 px caption is within the floor.
9. Set row — same grid, `data-testid="set-row-{n}"`, ticked `bg-emerald-500/10`. Set number `button.h-11.w-11.rounded-md.text-sm.tabular-nums.text-muted-foreground aria-label="Set 1 type"` (*Resolved:* 44×44, not tracker-first's 32 px) → set menu; PREVIOUS `button.h-11.text-left.text-xs.tabular-nums.text-muted-foreground` "100×5" copies into the inputs, blank `span` when none (never 0), loose workouts read it from `/api/workouts/lifts`; weight `Input type=number inputMode=decimal className="h-11"` or "bodyweight" text; reps `Input h-11` with range/"max"/"reps" placeholder; tick `button[data-testid=tick-{n}].h-11.w-11.rounded-md.border` `Check size-5`, ticked emerald, disabled until reps typed.
10. Set menu — BottomSheet: Warm-up · Working · Drop set (write `set_kind`; label W / n / D) — *Resolved:* no "Failure" (the CHECK constraint has no such value; Decision 6); "Effort (RPE)" row with `<Slider min=6 max=10 step=0.5>` → existing `rpe` (*Resolved:* RPE off the row, a sixth column does not fit 334 px); "Delete this set" (`text-destructive`).
11. Swipe to delete — touch drag ≤ −88 px reveals `w-22 bg-destructive text-white text-sm` "Delete" zone, 44 px; ticked → `removeSet` (DELETE); untouched extra row → drops locally. Pointer devices: `group-hover` `Button variant=ghost size=icon-sm` Trash2 at the row's right edge. Delete is therefore offered three ways (swipe / hover X / sheet row) — *Resolved:* swipe alone is undiscoverable.
12. Unsaved marker — `span.col-span-5.text-xs.text-amber-500` "Saving…" only after a `pending:` id is 1,500 ms old; after 10 s "Not saved — no signal. It will retry."; never hidden until the server acks. Fixes the one-frame flash (walk §4).
13. Add a set — `button[data-testid=add-set-{id}].min-h-11.w-full.rounded-md.border.border-dashed.border-border.text-sm.text-muted-foreground` "+ Add a set", pre-filled from the previous row. "one fewer" removed.
14. Plates line — `p.text-xs.text-muted-foreground` unchanged logic.
15. Superset — one Card, two headers, `divide-y`, "rest 2:00 after the pair" line; rest fires on the pair's last tick (`isLastOfGroup`, exists).
16. Endurance plan — `<Card className="gap-0 py-0" data-testid="endurance-plan">` › `px-3 py-2.5`; blocks become rows with a `h-11 w-11` tick each so the finish sheet has something to count; caption raised to text-xs.
17. Live error — existing `[data-testid=live-error]` box + Retry link.
18. AddLift — unchanged (min-h-11 dashed, h-11 input, results min-h-11).
19. Discard — `Button variant="ghost" size="sm" className="text-destructive"` → `Dialog` "Throw this workout away? Its N ticked sets will be deleted." `Button variant="destructive"` "Throw it away" / `variant="outline"` "Keep it". `window.confirm` removed.
20. Unsaved count — `span.text-xs.text-amber-600` "2 not saved yet", only when > 0.
21. RestBar — fixed bar kept; `Timer size-5`; countdown `text-lg font-medium tabular-nums`; `h-1` track orange → emerald + "Ready when you are" for 5 s then hides; −30 s / +30 s as `Button variant="outline" size="sm"`; ✕ `Button variant="ghost" size="icon" aria-label="Skip the rest"`; caption `text-xs`; target = `adjustments.rest[lift] ?? restSec ?? default`.
22. Finish sheet — body swap kept (*Resolved:* a centred Dialog with a keyboard on a phone is worse). Unfinished list from prescribed lifts only (`asked > 0`, not in `adjustments.added`); added lifts get "added, nothing ticked · drop it" which removes the lift. "Change" reveals **Started and Ended** `Input type=datetime-local h-11` (needs `startedAt` on `FinishWorkoutSchema`); 599-minute guard on Ended − Started; a stale workout opens with this row expanded and the header reading "since Mon 18:02". Intensity cells `flex-1 h-11 aria-pressed`. `Switch` row "Keep these changes for next time" (`label.flex.items-center.justify-between.min-h-11.text-sm`) only when adjustments are non-empty — on save with it on, the swap/added/order/rest are written to the enrollment's schedule (Strong's update-template question; tracker-first graft). Notes Textarea. Save disabled with reason while unsaved > 0 (exists).
23. Lost-finish guard — `useLiveWorkout.ts` ~L359: on a failed finish, re-read `/api/workouts/live`; if closed, clear the start key and show the receipt (Phase 6 step 7).
24. Summary — `WorkoutReceipt` (new, Phase 4b) with `data-testid="workout-summary"` here and `workout-receipt` inner, rendered identically at `/programs/workout/[id]`. Stats "—" + `text-xs` "not counted" when uncomputable, never 0. "New best" (Award, registered "milestone badges") only when `detectPersonalRecords` finds a prior logged set; otherwise "First time logged: Squat, Bench Press". `recordsUnavailable` amber line kept. Final `Button className="w-full"` label from `?from`.

#### (c) States
| State | What shows |
|---|---|
| no open workout | server redirect to /programs (exists) |
| ready | as drawn; PREVIOUS blank on a lift never done |
| prescription could not be resolved | `live-error` "Could not read today's plan — the boxes start empty. Your ticks are still saved."; blank inputs, never "nothing prescribed" as if by design |
| endurance day | plan card with tick rows; Finish asks the time |
| set ticked, ack < 1.5 s | row emerald, rest bar appears, no amber text ever renders |
| set ticked, no ack after 1.5 s / 10 s | "Saving…" / "Not saved — no signal. It will retry."; footer count; Save disabled "Saving your last set…" |
| write failed permanently | `live-error` "One set could not be saved and has been dropped: Squat set 3."; row un-ticks |
| lift skipped / swapped / added | opacity-60 + undo link / old sets collapsed above new rows / empty rows, "added on the day", Remove in menu, never a miss |
| rest running / done / dismissed | orange fill → emerald "Ready when you are" 5 s → hidden |
| stale (opened from the door) | header "since Mon 18:02", amber line "This workout was started on Monday. Finish it with the right times, or discard it."; finish opens with times expanded |
| loose workout | header "Workout · 12 Sep"; PREVIOUS from `/api/workouts/lifts`; no miss list, no keep-changes row |
| finishing / keep going | body swap; back to rows |
| finish reply lost | re-read; receipt if closed, else error and Finish stays |
| finished elsewhere | centred "This workout is finished." + `Button` "See the summary" → receipt |
| summary totals failed | `[data-testid=summary-unavailable]` amber "Saved, but the totals could not be worked out."; stats "—" |
| discard | Dialog; after → /programs |

#### (d) Deleted
- Plain-div lift card; inline `flex flex-wrap` menu with two 26 px chips; read-only "Rest 3 min (our suggestion)"; `MoreHorizontal`.
- "LAST" caption; the 1.75rem set-number column; "one fewer"; `window.confirm`.
- The immediate `unsaved` render on a `pending:` id; "N min" elapsed; added lifts counted as misses; "New best" on a first-ever set; "Your own program" fallback.
- Ended-only time editor; the summary's own markup (→ WorkoutReceipt); "Back to training" as a fixed label.
- Hand-rolled −30s/+30s and ✕; `text-[11px]` on PREVIOUS cells, RestBar caption, plates, superset tag, unsaved line (→ text-xs). Only the caption row stays 11 px.
- `RestTimer.tsx`.

#### (e) Acceptance
- Exactly one rgb(255,107,53) element before finishing ("Finish", in the sticky header, ≥ 44 px); one on the finish sheet ("Save this workout"); one on the summary.
- First lift's caption row reads SET, PREVIOUS, KG, REPS in order for a kg account; "LAST" absent.
- Every `[data-testid^=set-row-]`: set-number button ≥ 44×44, both inputs ≥ 44 tall, tick ≥ 44×44; computed `grid-template-columns` starts "44px 72px".
- With a previous session, row 1's PREVIOUS matches `/\d+×\d+/`; on a lift never done it is empty and contains no "0".
- "Options for Squat" opens `[data-testid=lift-menu]` with rows Swap this lift, Move up, Move down, Add a warm-up set, Rest after this lift, Last times for Squat, Skip this lift, each ≥ 44; "Remove" present only after "Add a lift".
- Add a warm-up set: new first row's label "W", both inputs empty; ticking it does not change the "n of 5" count on the finish sheet.
- Rest +15 s twice: sub line reads "rest 3:30"; next tick's `[data-testid=rest-remaining]` starts at 3:30; survives reload (adjustments.rest persisted).
- Swap: tick 2 Squat sets, swap to Front Squat → card title Front Squat, "2 × … logged" line, `workout_sets` rows = 2, finish sheet lists Squat 2 of 5 and not Front Squat.
- Set menu on set 1: rows Warm-up, Working, Drop set, Delete this set, an `[role=slider]`; choosing Warm-up → "W" and `set_kind = warmup`. No row reads "Failure".
- Swipe (touchscreen context, −100 px) on set 3 reveals "Delete"; tapping reduces set-row count by 1 and issues a DELETE for a ticked set, none for an untouched extra row.
- Tick with default network: "Saving"/"not saved" never attached to the DOM (poll 400 ms); with a 3 s route delay it appears after ≥ 1,400 ms.
- Superset fixture: two lifts in one `[data-testid^=lift-]` card; A1's last tick shows no RestBar; A2's does.
- Finish sheet "Change" reveals two `datetime-local` inputs labelled Started and Ended; Started −60 min → line reads "· 60 min"; a 10-hour span disables Save with the too-long text.
- A swapped session shows `[role=switch]` "Keep these changes for next time"; without changes it is absent; saving with it on changes the enrollment's schedule (read back via `/api/programs/enrollments`).
- Discard opens `[role=dialog]` with a `bg-destructive` button; `page.on('dialog')` count = 0; Escape keeps the workout.
- Summary on the account's first ever workout: no "New best"; "First time logged" present. Summary and `/programs/workout/<id>` produce identical `[data-testid=workout-receipt]` innerText; five identical sets render as "5 × 20 kg × 5".
- Started via `?from=/dashboard/tracking`: BackLink text "Tracking"; summary button "Back to Tracking"; landing shows the card in the done-today state.
- Abort the finish response, reload: page offers Finish again or shows the receipt; never a raw error; a later Start is not refused.
- No text < 11 px; only the caption row is 11 px; `document.body.scrollHeight` for 3 lifts × 5 sets ≤ 1,700 px; RestBar height ≤ 96 px + safe area; `[data-testid=mobile-tab-bar]` absent.

#### (f) Desktop
Same max-w-2xl column; inputs/ticks drop to 36 px via the `sm:` sizes already on Button/Input; every BottomSheet renders as a centred Dialog; swipe becomes the hover Trash2; RestBar stays fixed full-width with its inner max-w-2xl.

#### New icons
- `Timer` in RestBar — registered only for "time tracker navigation" (three files use it; the architecture test passes today, but the role is wrong). Ask to add "rest countdown" (Decision 8).
- `MoreVertical`, `Trash2`, `Plus`, `Minus`, `Check`, `Play`, `Loader2` — utility. `Award` — existing registered use.
- No swap / reorder / warm-up / history icons: menu rows are text-only, so tracker-first's `ArrowLeftRight` ask is dropped.

### S4. The Life Mastery program block

**Decision:** a *status card inside the step that links out*, not its own screen and not a second copy. The catalogue, ProgramDetail (with editor + seeded weights), the running-program editor, the builder and saved weeks exist **once**, on `/programs` (S2's views), reached with `?from=/life-mastery?step=templates` so the BackLink reads "Your plan" and returns here. Life Mastery renders one Card. An architecture test asserts `src/goals` imports exactly one module from `src/programs` (door-first graft).

#### (a) Layout, 390 px — inside the step's existing `px-5 py-4 border-b border-white/10` wrapper (Life Mastery chrome stays)

```
TRAINING PROGRAM                                          text-xs uppercase tracking-wide text-muted-foreground
┌ Card gap-0 py-0 · px-4 py-4 (app tokens on the zinc shell) ──┐
│ StrongLifts 5×5                              [Beginner]      │  text-base font-semibold · Badge secondary
│ Workout A · Workout B, in turn                               │  text-sm muted (anchored: "Mon · Wed · Fri — Upper / Lower / Upper")
│ Last trained Fri 12 Sep · 12 sessions                        │  text-xs muted
│ [ Change program ]              Today's session ›            │  Button outline sm → ?view=programs&from=… · Link min-h-11 → /programs
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  ≤ 200 px

none running:
│ No program yet                                               │
│ A goal says where you are going. A program says what you    │
│ do on Tuesday.                                               │

could not load:

Systems step — "Training week" RoutineCard, program linked:

(SplitDesigner, "Name your training days" and the ×/wk stepper are hidden while a program is linked)
```

#### (b) Elements
1. Section label — SeasonBand label class; replaces the emerald "RUNNING NOW — 3 PROGRAMS".
2. Card — `<Card className="gap-0 py-0" data-testid="lm-training-program">` › `div.px-4.py-4.space-y-2`; mounted at `BuildBoard.tsx:171` in place of `<WorkoutPrograms/>`. `WorkoutPrograms.tsx` shrinks to this ~120-line card (one-kit); it reads `/api/programs/enrollments` via the same hook S1 uses and nothing else.
3. Name row — `p.text-base.font-semibold.truncate` + `Badge variant="secondary"` level.
4. Week line — derived from the enrollment's schedule: anchored → "Mon · Wed · Fri — Upper / Lower / Upper"; unanchored → "Workout A · Workout B, in turn"; endurance → "Week 3 of 8 · 6 sessions this week". *Resolved:* never a weekly count for an unanchored program — the catalogue declares no frequency (TrainingScreen.tsx comment); door-first's "3×/wk" was invented, one-kit's "in turn" is honest.
5. Last-trained line — `p.text-xs.text-muted-foreground` / "Not trained yet" / amber "Never trained — started 18 Jun" after 14 days (RunningPrograms' rule).
6. Actions — `Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm"` (→ `/programs?view=programs&from=…`, built with `src/shared/returnTo.ts`) + `Link.min-h-11.text-sm.text-muted-foreground` "Today's session ›". No orange: the violet step button is this page's primary.
7. Multi-running rows — ProgramRow ×N, each → `?view=programs&program=<id>`. *Resolved:* both judges rejected one-kit's non-interactive chips; tracker-first's End buttons inside the block were a second management place, so ending stays on /programs.
8. Adoption — the plan stores **only** `enrollmentId` in `NsRoutineProgram`; days, names and count are derived live from the enrollment. When the step mounts and finds a running enrollment the plan does not reference, it writes the ref and shows once `p.text-xs.text-muted-foreground` "Your training week below now follows StrongLifts 5×5." *Resolved:* one representation of the week (door-first) makes tracker-first's "Match it" button unnecessary — there is nothing to mismatch; the phone/laptop split (walk §6) closes because the days are never copied into localStorage.
9. Systems-step RoutineCard — for the workout blueprint with a linked program: derived `text-sm` line + "Change on the Training page ›"; SplitDesigner and stepper hidden. With no program: existing SplitDesigner, its `size-5` step buttons → `Button variant="ghost" size="icon-sm"`, `text-[10px]` → `text-xs`. Enrollments failed → amber "Could not read your program", SplitDesigner hidden (never a guessed 2×/week). `applyProgramDays` no longer writes `daysPerWeek`.
10. Programs screen home of the editor — `ProgramEditor` repainted in tokens and mounted in two places: inside ProgramDetail before Start (one-kit graft) and at `?view=edit&enrollment=` for a running program (from the ⋮ sheet). Day rows: `Card gap-0 py-0` divide-y, `min-h-11`, `font-medium` full name + `text-xs muted` "3 lifts" + **one** `Button ghost size=icon` MoreVertical → BottomSheet Rename / Move up / Move down / Remove (tracker-first graft; fixes "Wor…"). Tapping a day expands lift rows with ⋮ → Swap (AddLift search) / Sets and reps (two `Input h-11 w-20`) / Starting weight (`Input h-11 w-24`) / Remove. "+ Add a day", "+ Add a lift" dashed rows. Refusals (5/3/1 main lifts, endurance) = `Badge variant="secondary"` + read-only rows + one sentence. "Save changes" `Button` default = that view's one orange; problems as `text-destructive` lines; edits kept on failure. Reset → sheet row on the title's ⋮.
11. Build your own — `?view=build` inside ProgramsApp (*Resolved:* not a new route; one program screen, one URL family). h1 `text-lg font-semibold` "Build your own week"; kg/lb `Button variant=outline size=sm aria-pressed`; "Write your week" `<Textarea className="min-h-[240px] font-mono text-base">` = the existing parser (`programText.ts`, `+ Lateral Raise` pairs supersets; parser and tests kept); one help line `text-xs muted` "3x8 is sets by reps · 3x8-12 is a range · @60 is the starting weight · @bw is bodyweight"; read-back rows `text-sm` per day, parse errors as `text-destructive` lines with the line number (fail loudly); `Button size="lg" className="w-full"` "Start tracking this" = the one orange; "Save for later" link → Dialog `Input h-11` "Name this week" → `/api/programs/drafts`. Saved weeks listed under Yours as ProgramRow; tapping loads the text. The structured tap-to-build mode: Decision 5.
12. Copy removed — "Starting a program saves it to your account, so you will need to be signed in" (the route is gated signed-in); PROGRAM_COPY.help shrinks to the two sentences above.

#### (c) States
| State | What shows |
|---|---|
| loading | 92 px pulse; goal sections below do not move |
| failed | amber line + Try again, `data-testid=lm-training-unavailable`; Pick/Change links still render; "No program yet" absent |
| none | "No program yet", sentence, outline "Pick a program", link "Build my own ›" |
| one running | name + badge + week line + last-trained + two actions; adoption notice once |
| one running, never trained ≥ 14 d | amber last-trained line |
| 2–3 running | "N programs running" + rows + amber sentence + "Manage programs" |
| finished, none running | name + `text-xs text-emerald-600` "Finished — everything you logged is kept" + outline "Choose what is next" |
| returning via BackLink | lands on `?step=templates`, block refetches on mount, names the program just started |
| Systems step, linked / none / failed | derived line + link / editable SplitDesigner (44 px steppers) / amber, designer hidden |
| Build: empty / parse error / valid / saving / refused | primary disabled + "Write at least one day" / destructive lines, disabled / read-back, enabled / Loader2 / `role=alert` |
| Editor: unchanged / changed / refused / save failed | Save disabled / enabled / Badge + read-only / destructive line, edits kept |

#### (d) Deleted
- `WorkoutPrograms.tsx`: ModeSwitch, discipline/level/kg-lb chips, program-card grid, inline ProgramEditor, SeededWeights, emerald "Start tracking this", "Go to today's session", "is running" line, 10 px captions, the signed-in caption.
- `BuildYourOwn.tsx` (sky frame, wrapped heading); `SavedWeeks.tsx` (→ ProgramRow under Yours).
- `RunningPrograms.tsx` dark tone and the "you may have started this and forgotten it" / "More than one is running…" paragraphs; the `app` tone's data path survives as S2's Running rows.
- `ProgramPicker.tsx` (zinc/emerald; GoalsConfigStep links to `?view=programs` instead).
- ProgramEditor's kit rendering (Panel, four IconButtons, NumberBox, 10 px captions); CustomProgramBuilder's kit imports and the 21-button ExercisePalette; EditActiveProgram's emerald Save.
- `src/programs/components/ui.tsx` (TYPE, IconButton, Segmented, Field, Stepper, Panel, Action, GroupLabel) — no importers remain.
- `applyProgramDays` writing `daysPerWeek`; the localStorage copy of split days while a program runs; RoutineCard's 20 px steppers and 10 px labels; BuildBoard's 9 px badge in this section.

#### (e) Acceptance
- `/life-mastery?step=templates` at 390 px: `[data-testid=lm-training-program]` count 1, height ≤ 200 px in every state; account B `document.body.scrollHeight` ≤ 6,000 px (was 12,661).
- Step DOM contains none of: "Take a ready-made one", "Build my own" as a segmented option, "RUNNING NOW", "Go to today's session", "Start tracking this", "you will need to be signed in".
- Inside the block: 0 classes matching `/zinc-|sky-|emerald-|violet-/` (except the finished-state emerald text), 0 rgb(255,107,53) backgrounds; every `a, button` ≥ 44 px; no text < 12 px.
- Three programs: 3 ProgramRows, one `button` ("Manage programs"), one link ("Today's session"); tapping a row lands on `/programs?view=programs&program=<id>`.
- "Change program" href contains `view=programs` and `from=`; BackLink on /programs reads "Your plan"; pressing it returns to `?step=templates` and the block names the program just started without a manual reload.
- StrongLifts running: Systems routine card contains "Workout A" and "in turn", does not contain "2 days a week" or `/\d×\/wk/`; no `aria-label` matching `/days for/`. In a fresh browser context for the same user the same text appears (nothing read from localStorage).
- Enrollments aborted: `[data-testid=lm-training-unavailable]` visible; "No program yet" absent; the Systems card shows no number.
- `?view=build`: exactly one orange ("Start tracking this"); a malformed line "Bench Press 3x" shows a destructive line containing "line 2" and the primary is disabled; the two-day sample creates one enrollment and 2 read-back rows.
- Editor: day rows show "Workout A" unclipped (`scrollWidth === clientWidth`); exactly one `button[aria-label^="Options for"]` per row, ≥ 44×44; no ChevronUp/ChevronDown/X icon buttons on day rows.
- Unit tests: `src/programs/components/ui.tsx` does not exist; grep `from "./ui"` in src/programs = 0; a new test forbids `zinc-|sky-|text-\[10px\]|text-\[10\.5px\]` in `src/programs/**` and in `WorkoutPrograms.tsx` (one-kit graft, the way architecture.test.ts forbids unregistered icons); architecture test asserts `src/goals` imports exactly one module from `src/programs`.

#### (f) Desktop
One Card at the top of the max-w-5xl step, actions right-aligned at `sm` (the S1 two-column shape); `?view=build` and `?view=edit` use the /programs max-w-2xl column with day cards in one column.

#### New icons
None in the block (`AlertTriangle`, `ChevronRight` utility). Editor/builder reuse `Search`, `Plus`, `Trash2`, `MoreVertical` (utility).

---

### Decisions the owner must confirm

Each one: what it is in plain words, then the recommendation. "Go with your recommendations" is a valid reply.

1. **Fix the app's bordered button once, for every screen.** Today the bordered ("outline") button paints itself the page's black on a slate card, and hovering any bordered or transparent button flashes red — that is why "Start" looked disabled. The fix is one line in `components/ui/button.tsx` and changes every such button in the app (Settings, Goals, everywhere). The training card works without it (it uses an orange-tinted style). *Recommend: yes — it removes the class of fault, not the instance.*
2. **Remove the "Training →" row from Quick Actions on the Tracking page.** The card above the fold is now the door; the row 2,800 px down is a duplicate. *Recommend: remove.*
3. **Delete both fill-in-afterwards forms.** "I did all of this — save it" (TodaySessionWidget) and the older WorkoutLogger (warm-up checkboxes, notes per set) go. Logging a past workout becomes: History → "Log a past workout" → pick when and which session → the normal live screen opens with that start time, and you can also change Started and Ended when finishing. WorkoutLogger is also mounted in the goals hub's Health panel and a `/test/health` page; those mounts go too (the Health panel keeps the training card). *Recommend: delete both; this is how Hevy and Strong log past sessions.*
4. **Two new fields in the workout API.** `startedAt` when starting and when finishing (so past workouts and stale ones get their real times), and a per-lift rest time saved on the workout (so an edited rest survives the phone locking). Both are small; the finish keeps its 599-minute ceiling. *Recommend: yes; without them "log a past workout" cannot exist.*
5. **Delete the tap-to-build program builder (about 1,000 lines) and keep only "Write your week".** The written mode did the job in the walk and already handles supersets; the structured mode is the single largest source of the old blue-grey styling. If you want tap-to-build later it is a new small screen built from the lift search, not a resurrection. *Recommend: delete. If no, it is repainted at roughly the same cost as the rest of S4 combined.*
6. **No "Failure" set type.** The database only allows warm-up / working / AMRAP / back-off / drop. Adding "failure" means a migration. *Recommend: skip it; "Drop set" and the effort slider cover what a lifter marks.*
7. **The receipt page `/programs/workout/[id]` must be built first.** It does not exist. Every "See today's workout" button and the finish summary depend on it (prior plan Phase 4b). *Recommend: build it as step one of the implementation, before S1.*
8. **Icon registry changes (needs your yes per the rules).** `Timer` gains the role "rest countdown" (it is already used that way); `Calendar` gains "history calendar toggle". No swap/reorder/history icons are added — menu rows are text-only. *Recommend: approve both.*
9. **Tracking's "Resume" is no longer solid orange.** The page keeps one orange ("Start Session"); a running workout shows as an orange-bordered card with a tinted "Resume". The old code argued Resume must be orange because it is urgent. *Recommend: tinted; if you disagree it is one class and a documented exception.*
10. **The week strip shows this week only, as dots, with no legend.** Fewer captions, less height; older training shows in History and Progress instead. *Recommend: yes.*
11. **Life Mastery no longer starts programs itself.** Picking, editing, ending and building happen on the Training page; the Life Mastery step shows a status card and links there and back. The plan stores only which program is linked; the training week text is derived from the program so a phone and a laptop can never disagree. *Recommend: yes — it is the only way to have one copy.*
12. **/programs gets the bottom tab bar; /programs/live does not.** The Training tab currently lands on a page with no tabs. *Recommend: yes.*

No blockers beyond the decisions above. Every dependency that is not pure UI is listed in the cross-cutting table with what it takes.

# Part 4 — Order, parallelism and coverage

## Dependency order

The phases are numbered in the order they run. Each phase's own "Why it is in this order" line
says what it needs; this is the whole picture in one place.

| Run | Phase | Must come after | Why |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | **10, steps 1–4, 12, 13** (lint proof, CI triggers, database tests as a step, the sweep-debt maps, the schema mirror, the RLS audit) | nothing | These touch only CI files, `tests/support/`, `tests/integration/schema.sql` and `scripts/`; every later phase then lands on a branch that is checked. |
| 2 | **9, Part A** (steps 1–9: the bordered-button fix, `trainingStyles.ts`, `BottomSheet`, `Stepper`, `ProgramRow`, 44 px Tabs/Segmented, the icon lock, the one-language lint with its debt list, the phone-input zoom rule) | nothing | Phases 3, 5, 6 and 8 build from these parts; if they land later, four screens get repainted twice. |
| 3 | **0** | nothing | The start/finish path underneath every screen. |
| 4 | **1** | 0 | One-statement program writes, the replay history, the account's clock; Phase 0 step 5's open-workout guard composes with Phase 1 step 1's functions. |
| 5 | **2** | 1 | The Life Mastery ↔ database link; needs Phase 1's transactional start (for "start a fresh copy") and hands Phase 8 the derived week. |
| 6 | **10, step 5** (the third test account and the one wipe helper) | 1 (step 12) | The helper's wipe order is Phase 1 step 12's rule; it must not land before the specs it rewrites. |
| 7 | **3** | 0, 1, 9A | The Tracking door; needs the one start helper (0), `clockKnown` (1) and the tinted button / skeleton classes (9A). |
| 8 | **4** | 0, 3 | One way to log; needs `summaryFor` (0) and the door's `?from` convention (3). |
| 9 | **5** | 0, 3, 4, 9A | /programs Today; needs `trainingCardState` (3), the receipt page's absence handled (4), Tabs/BottomSheet/ProgramRow (9A). |
| 10 | **6** | 4, 5 | The live screen; needs the finish-sheet times (4) and the sheet/row primitives and `?from` (5). Builds the receipt page (see resolution 7 below). |
| 11 | **7** | 1, 4, 5, 6 | History and Progress; needs safe recalculation (1), the receipt page (6), `?tab=` (5), the set kinds and rest the live screen writes (6). |
| 12 | **8** | 2, 5 | The Life Mastery status card; needs the derived week (2) and the Programs screen with `?view=` / `?program=` / `from=` (5). |
| 13 | **9, Part B** (steps 10–15: delete the kit, the tab sweep, the split-designer rows, the sweep numbers to zero, the debt lists to empty, the one-language browser spec) | 5, 6, 7, 8 | Can only measure zero once the screens are rebuilt. |
| 14 | **10, steps 6–8, 10** (the forms-stay-deleted guard, the two-doors-agree test, the Life Mastery failed-state test, the Progress numbers) | 4; 3 + 5; 8; 0 + 4 + 5 | Each presses a screen built by the phase it waits for; a red run before that phase exists is that phase's failure, never a reason to write the test to today's screen. |

## What an agent team can run side by side

Almost nothing, honestly. 92 files are edited by two or more phases, and the eight hottest —
`src/programs/programsService.ts` (8 phases), `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts` (10),
`src/programs/types.ts` (7), `src/programs/schemas.ts` (6), `src/db/programRepo.ts` (6),
`src/db/workoutRepo.ts` (5), `playwright.config.ts` (7), `tests/e2e/programs-live-workout.spec.ts`
(6) — sit under every phase. The rule from `plans.md` is that no two agents edit the same file, so:

- **Safe in parallel:** run 1 (Phase 10's day-one steps) beside run 3 (Phase 0) — Phase 10's
  day-one steps touch `.github/`, `scripts/`, `tests/support/`, `tests/integration/schema.sql`,
  `tests/unit/{lintRatchet,ciWorkflows,db/schemaMirror,db/auditRlsExpectations}.test.ts`; Phase 0
  touches none of those. Run 2 (Phase 9 Part A) beside Phase 0 as well — it touches
  `components/ui/*`, `components/BottomSheet.tsx`, `components/MobileTabBar.tsx`, `app/globals.css`,
  `src/shared/iconRoles.ts` and the new `trainingStyles.ts`; Phase 0 touches none of those, and
  the only shared file is `tests/unit/architecture.test.ts`, where each adds its own `describe`
  block — a merge, not a conflict, if they land as separate commits.
- **Everything else is sequential**, one executor, in the order above. Two agents on Phases 3
  and 4, or 5 and 6, would both edit `programsService.ts`, `types.ts` and the same spec files.

## Files two phases both touch — who wins

The later phase edits after the earlier one has landed and re-reads the file first (convention 6).
Where two phases mint the same helper under two names, the resolutions below are binding.


## Files two phases both touch


## Resolutions I made after reading every phase (binding on the executor)

Two phases drafted by two different agents sometimes reach for the same helper under two names.
The rule is the plan's own: one owner. The earlier phase builds it; the later phase uses it and
drops its own copy. Nothing else in the later phase changes.

1. **The client start helper.** Phase 0 step 3 builds `startWorkoutRequest` in
   `src/programs/hooks/useLiveWorkout.ts` (with the key rule: cleared on every reply, kept only on
   a thrown fetch, spent-key retried once). Phase 3 step 9 drafts a second one in
   `src/programs/startWorkoutRequest.ts`. **Phase 3 uses Phase 0's**, keeps its own two additions
   — the `dayId` the card named is always posted, and on any non-ok reply the card re-reads the door
   and lets the facts decide Resume vs error — and its guard test ("no third caller posts
   `/api/workouts`") points at Phase 0's file. Phase 4 step 8 already builds on Phase 0's.
2. **"This week" on the account's clock.** Phase 1 step 9 builds `weekSoFar(logs, timezone, now)`
   and `getEnrollmentDetail` (one constructor of `EnrollmentDetail`, with `week`). Phase 5 step 5
   drafts both again. **Phase 5 uses Phase 1's**; Phase 5 keeps what Phase 1 does not have —
   `trainedToday` on `week`, the date-only `startedOn` / `lastLoggedOn` on `ProgramEnrollment`,
   `formatDateOnly`, and the presentational `WeekStrip`.
3. **Program actions on the client.** Phase 1 step 6 builds `src/programs/programActions.ts`
   (plain functions returning `{ ok, error }`). Phase 5 step 6 drafts `useProgramActions` as a hook.
   **Phase 5's hook wraps Phase 1's module** — it adds `refreshEnrollments()` on success and the
   sheet's state; it does not fetch on its own, and Phase 1's architecture test ("program actions go
   through programActions.ts") stays the guard.
4. **What Reset does.** Phase 1 step 8 names the constant `RESET_EFFECT` with `resetConfirmText()`;
   Phase 5 step 6 names it `RESET_EVENT` with `RESET_CONFIRM_COPY`. **One constant, Phase 1's
   name**; Phase 5's sheet calls `resetConfirmText()`. The test that flips the flag and expects the
   sentence to follow is the same test.
5. **`getEnrollmentDetail` callers.** Phase 1 wires `app/programs/page.tsx` and the enrollment GET
   route to it. Phase 5 step 5 additionally makes the page resolve the detail for
   `location.programId ?? chooseCardEnrollment(active)?.id` (so three programs paint a card on the
   first paint). Both stand; Phase 5's is the later edit of the same lines.
6. **`STALE_WORKOUT_HOURS`.** Phase 4 step 2 owns the six-hour rule as `isStaleWorkout`; Phase 3
   step 7 and Phase 5 step 4 read `state.kind === "stale"` from `trainingCardState`, which calls it.
   No conflict, named so nobody re-adds `TodayCard`'s private constant.
7. **The receipt page.** The design spec (Decision 7) and Phase 3 say "Phase 4 builds
   `/programs/workout/[id]`"; Phase 4's draft does not build a page — it builds `summaryFor` (Phase
   0 step 4) and the finish-sheet summary. **Phase 6 step "Summary → WorkoutReceipt" builds the
   page** (it is S3 element 24, Phase 6's surface), and Phase 3's done-today button and Phase 5's
   "See today's workout" point at it through `workoutReceiptHref`. Until Phase 6 runs, both buttons
   go to `/programs?tab=history` (Phase 3 open question 2's recommendation).
8. **What the plan stores about a program — added 2026-09-18, and it settles a
   conflict between two phases.** Phase 2 step 7 shrinks `NsRoutineProgram`
   (`src/goals/types.ts:1718`) to `{ enrollmentId }`. Phase 8 step 2 keeps all
   four of today's fields, because its "the program has ended" state names the
   program from the plan's copy of `label`. Both cannot be right.
   **`{ enrollmentId: string }` wins, and the ended state does not name the
   program.** Why: `programId` and `startedAt` are readable from the enrollment,
   so a copy of them can only be a copy that goes stale; `label` is the same, and
   the ended state does not need it — "The program this week followed has
   finished. Everything you logged is kept." plus "Choose what is next" says
   everything a person needs, with no second representation of a name. The
   comment at `types.ts:1712` defends the `label` copy with "the plan has to
   render signed out": that stopped being true when Life Mastery moved behind a
   sign-in gate (`app/life-mastery/layout.tsx`), and no code reads any of the
   four fields today, because `loadNsPlan` drops the whole `program` field on
   reload. So nothing is lost by the shrink and nothing has to be migrated.
   *Told to the session planning `docs/plans/life-mastery-deployment.md` on
   2026-09-18, because its `life_plan_routines` table mirrors these four fields
   and three of the columns now have nothing to hold.*


## Coverage check

- 126 findings in Appendix B; each is closed by exactly one phase, or listed here.

# Appendix A — The browser walkthrough (2026-09-14)

## Browser walkthrough — what I saw, 2026-09-14

Headless Chromium, iPhone-14 viewport 390×844 (device scale 2) unless stated; desktop 1280×900.
Signed in as the two test accounts via Playwright's own login setup. Screenshots under
`.playwright-mcp/walk/`. Every claim below is something I saw on screen or read back from
the database afterwards; nothing here is inferred from a comment or a test name.

### Accounts

- **A** (`test@…`, the seeded account): 144 workouts, 0 running programs, one finished
  StrongLifts (started 2026-09-09, never trained). Profile timezone UTC, kg, 20 kg bar, 1.25 plate.
- **B** (`test-user-b@…`, the "fresh" account): 0 workouts at start. Profile timezone
  Europe/Copenhagen. **Hidden state:** two running enrollments from 2026-06-18 (Sprint Triathlon,
  Bodyweight Foundations), never trained, plus one ended StrongLifts from the same date. Nothing on
  the Tracking page or `/programs` Today tab says these exist until a third program is started.
- **The user's own account** — I attempted a read-only probe of its training state and the
  permission system blocked the command. Not verified.

### 1. Tracking page (`/dashboard/tracking`) — `01-tracking-phone.png`, `11-tracking-desktop.png`

- With **no running program and no open workout** (account A): the training card renders
  nothing at all (`[data-testid=training-card]` count 0). The only trace of training on the page is
  the sixth row of Quick Actions, "Training →", below the fold at 2,889 px page height. A person
  who opens Tracking to train sees nothing to train with. This is the state the user is describing.
- With a program (account B after start): `23-tracking-card-with-program.png`. Card reads
  "TRAINING · StrongLifts 5×5 / Today: Workout A · 3 lifts / [Start]". The Start button is the
  outline variant: a black rectangle with white text on a dark card, directly under the SeasonBand's
  identical-looking "Today" outline button. It reads as disabled.
- **Minutes after finishing Workout A** the card says "Today: Workout B · 3 lifts / Start"
  (`34-tracking-card-after-finish.png`). Nothing says you already trained today. The state machine
  (`trainingCardState`) has no "done today" state.
- The card shows **`enrollments[0]` only**. Account B has three running programs; the card names
  one. The other two are invisible here.
- On a **rest day** the card has no button (code: `state.kind === "rest"` renders a sentence only).
  A person who wants to train on a rest day has no way in from this page.

### 2. `/programs` Today tab

- **No program** (A, `02-programs-today-phone.png`): one card with TWO full-width orange primary
  buttons, "Browse programs" and "Start a workout now", 40 px apart — the same two-equal-shouts
  defect the previous plan fixed once. Below it "Programs you have finished" with Start again /
  Delete.
- **Tabs** (`Segmented`): active tab is sky-blue (`bg-sky-500/15 text-sky-200`) while every primary
  control on the same screen is orange. The group is left-aligned and does not span the width.
- **Three running programs** (B, `31-programs-after-finish.png`): the tab called "Today" shows an
  inventory — three cards "StrongLifts 5×5 / Sprint Triathlon / Bodyweight Foundations" — and no
  session. You must tap one to see what today is. The Tracking card meanwhile shows StrongLifts'
  session with Start. The two screens disagree about what "today" is.
- **One running program** (A after "Start again", `43-log-after-the-fact-open.png`,
  `44-progression-open.png`): program name, "Runs in order rather than on set days / Put it on set
  days instead", TodayCard (Workout A/B chips, three lifts, orange Start workout), then a
  `<details>` **"Log a workout you already did"** which opens the OLD form: "Today — Workout A
  Session 1 / Logging / Did it exactly as shown? Just save / three collapsible lifts / How long
  (min) 45 / How hard (1–5) 3 / Day (blank = today) mm/dd/yyyy / **I did all of this — save it**"
  — the exact button the user said "is not how people do workouts". It still ships, one tap away,
  with 45 min and intensity 3 pre-filled. Then a History disclosure with Change this program / Skip
  session / Reset to start / End program.
- **"Start again"** on a finished program (`42-today-after-start-again.png`): while the request is
  in flight the Delete button next to it reads **"Deleting…"** (both buttons share one `busy` id,
  `PastPrograms.tsx:225,235`). For ~2 s the screen says it is deleting the program you asked to restart.
- **Browse** (`40-browse-catalog.png`): 13 programs as full cards, each with an orange "View"
  button — 13 orange buttons on one 3,384 px page; the card title is not tappable.
- **Program detail**: tapping the program name does nothing; only "View" opens it.

### 3. History / Progress / Anything else

- **History** (A, 144 workouts, `45-history-year.png`): loads 20 rows, month headers with totals,
  lift filter. Reads well. Three identical "Wed, Sep 9" rows are seeded duplicates. Load took >1.2 s
  (the tab showed "Loading…" in the first pass); it fetches the whole year with sets.
- **Progress** (`46-progress-year.png`): "This week 0 so far", volume chart with axis and months,
  bests. Reads well. The bars are five identical heights then a fall — that is the seeded data.
- **Anything else** (`47-anything-else.png`): a third orange "Start a workout now" (the same
  button as the Today tab's empty state), then "Log a past workout +" (WorkoutLogger). Two tabs
  offer the same action.

### 4. Live workout (`/programs/live`) — B, StrongLifts Workout A

- `24-live-first-paint.png`: sticky header "← Training / Workout A · StrongLifts 5×5 / 0 min /
  [Finish]"; per lift a card with SET · LAST · KG · REPS · ✓ grid; "+ one more set"; "Bar: just the
  bar"; "Add a lift"; red "Discard workout". LAST column empty (first session — honest). Page is
  1,646 px for three lifts.
- Tick set 1 (`25-live-after-tick-1.png`): row turns green, rest bar appears at the bottom
  "3:00 / −30s / +30s / ✕ / resting — 3:00 is our suggestion". For one frame the row shows
  "not saved yet — waiting for signal" in amber before the server answers — on a fast local
  connection. On gym wifi every tick will flash this.
- Lift menu (`26-live-lift-menu.png`): "Skip this one" and a read-only "Rest 3 min (our
  suggestion)". No swap, no reorder, no warm-up, no editable rest, no history.
- Add a lift: search "front squat" → result → added with 3 empty rows and "added on the day".
- Finish sheet (`29-finish-sheet.png`): "Not everything was ticked — These count as misses…"
  lists Squat 2 of 5, Bench 0 of 5, Row 0 of 5, **Front Squat 0 of 3** — the added lift was never
  prescribed; "3" is the three placeholder rows, and it is presented as a miss. Time line
  "01:56 PM → 01:57 PM 1 min", intensity 1–5 (3 pressed), notes, orange Save / Keep going.
- Summary (`30-summary.png`): "Done. 1 minutes · 2 sets · 200 kg lifted / **New best: Squat
  20 kg × 5**" — on the account's first ever set. "Next time: Squat only 2 of 5 → same weight…;
  Bench Press: Skipped — nothing changed; Barbell Row: Skipped…". Front Squat is not mentioned.
- Database afterwards: one `workout_logs` row, `started_at`=`logged_at`, `ended_at` set,
  `duration_min` 1, `intensity` 3, `adjustments.added` holds Front Squat with `libraryId`
  `lib_front_squat`, two `workout_sets` rows at 20 kg × 5 `working` with `exercise_id` `squat`.
  Enrollment cursor moved to dayIndex 1, sessionCount 1, squat `consecutiveFails: 1`.
- History row afterwards (`33-history-row-open.png`): "Mon, Sep 14 · 1 min · 2 sets · 200 kg /
  Squat 20×5" → expanded "Squat 2 × 20 kg × 5 / Correct this / Delete". Correct.

### 5. Life Mastery — templates step (`/life-mastery?step=templates`)

- Page height on a phone: **12,661 px** before anything is opened, 13,668 with a program open,
  17,113 with build-your-own applied. The program picker sits at the top of a step that then lists
  every area's goal sets.
- `20-lm-pick-program.png`: "Pick what you are taking on / Level chips / [Take a ready-made one |
  Build my own] / RUNNING NOW — 3 PROGRAMS (green box, End buttons, amber 'never trained — you may
  have started this and forgotten it' twice, amber 'More than one is running…') / Go to today's
  session (green outline) / Take a training program / discipline chips / program cards".
- Pick StrongLifts → `21-lm-editor-open.png`: day rows "Wor… 3 lifts" — the day NAME is truncated
  to three letters by four 44 px icon buttons; Starting weights (kg) 20/20/30/20/40; green outline
  "Start tracking this"; grey caption "Starting a program saves it to your account, so you will need
  to be signed in" shown to a signed-in user.
- Start → `22-lm-started.png`: "StrongLifts 5×5 is running. Your training week here now matches
  it." The plan's workout routine now reads **"Workout A · Workout B, 2 days a week"**
  (`35-lm-systems-after-start.png`). StrongLifts is three sessions a week. `applyProgramDays` sets
  `daysPerWeek` from the number of day templates.
- Build my own (`50b-lm-write-week.png`, `52b-lm-build-own-bottom.png`): "Your saved weeks"
  block with a 3-line wrapped heading "Your / saved / weeks" beside its caption; kg/lb; "Hide the
  written version"; "Write your week" textarea; then per-lift settings; a 21-button lift picker
  ("Chest" group) inside every open day; "Add another day"; sky-blue outline "Start tracking this".
  Writing a two-day week and pressing "Use this" worked; "Save this week" saved a draft named
  "Test week" (2 days · 4 lifts) to the account.
- Desktop (`57-lm-templates-desktop-viewport.png`): the same block at 11–12.5 px type across a
  1,280 px row; the program cards are 2-up; readable but small.
- Three visual languages on one screen: emerald outline buttons (LM), sky-blue Segmented/Start
  (programs kit), and the purple "Systems →" step button (LM nav).

### 6. Life Mastery — systems step

- After starting a program in the same browser: "What runs underneath" lists "Training week —
  Workout A · Workout B, 2 days a week" (`35-…`). In a NEW browser context the plan is empty: the
  plan lives in localStorage only, so the phone and the laptop can hold different plans while the
  database holds one enrollment.
- Split designer: see `61–63-lm-split-designer-*.png` (walk 6).

### 7. Cross-cutting

- Console errors on every page visited: 0.
- No page scrolled sideways.
- The Next.js dev-mode "N" badge appears in every screenshot; it is not part of the app.
- `npx eslint .` → 498 problems (492 errors). CI on `main` has been red since 2026-09-09
  (GitHub API, last 5 runs all failure). Unchanged since the previous plan raised it.
- `supabase migration list --linked`: everything applied, including `20260914120000` which is an
  UNTRACKED file in the working tree (another agent's work in progress).

Every row was traced in the code by one agent and checked by an independent refuter (verdict), or seen in the browser (walk-*), or added by the completeness critic. `Phase` is the phase that closes it. Full text — what the user sees, why, how to reproduce, fix sketch — is in the session's `findings-compact.json`; the phases carry what the executor needs.
